-- Daydreamer Rivals — single-file bundle with EclipseUI
-- Drop into executor scripts folder and execute.

-- LPH stubs for non-obfuscated environments
do
    local env = getfenv and getfenv() or _G
    env["LPH_JIT"]           = env["LPH_JIT"]           or function(fn) return fn end
    env["LPH_JIT_MAX"]       = env["LPH_JIT_MAX"]       or function(fn) return fn end
    env["LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE"] = env["LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE"] or function(fn) return fn end
    env["LPH_OBFUSCATED"]    = env["LPH_OBFUSCATED"]    or false
end

-- ─── Module registry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local __mods = {}
local function __def(key, src)
    -- each src is a function body string that gets executed as a module
    local ok, result = pcall(src)
    if ok then
        __mods[key] = result
    else
        warn("{*} [" .. key .. "] " .. tostring(result))
    end
end

-- ─── anticheat_disabler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('anticheat', function()
local ls3hash = "4a13bee835c3a4c26a50dbbd941dd040b83d5f61a68e77580e0054c1d102dac726e82433a4e25bf4434485d79ae743cd"
local mischash = "4f4e9d20874357821170df06d3aabe9e5b558a14c9c6da8e0aa2ffe409c5651a2836b28ab127551edabce69674d38045"
local localscript3 = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("LocalScript3", 10)
local miscctl = game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):WaitForChild("StarterPlayerScripts", 10):WaitForChild("Controllers", 10):WaitForChild("MiscellaneousController", 10)

if not localscript3 or not getscripthash(localscript3) == ls3hash then
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:Kick("[e2] anticheat update detected, you were kicked by daydreamer in order to prevent any bans. join our discord for updates: https://discord.gg/daydreamer")
    setclipboard("https://discord.gg/daydreamer")
    return
end

if not miscctl or not getscripthash(miscctl) == mischash then
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:Kick("[e2] anticheat updated, you were kicked by daydreamer in order to prevent any bans. join our discord for updates: https://discord.gg/daydreamer")
    setclipboard("https://discord.gg/daydreamer")
    return
end

local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local plr = plrs.LocalPlayer

local function kick(msg)
    pcall(setclipboard, "https://discord.gg/daydreamer")
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:Kick(msg)
end

local lpk = 1
local rtgt = "LocalScript3"
local rwait = 0.5
local retry = 5
local key = "jdsbau"

local function cph(s)
    local o = table.create(#s)
    for i = 1, #s do
        o[i] = string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(s, i), string.byte(key, (i - 1) % 6 + 1)))
    end
    return table.concat(o)
end

local function istxt(s)
    if #s == 0 then return false end
    for i = 1, #s do
        local b = string.byte(s, i)
        if b < 32 or b > 126 then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local function dcopy(v, seen)
    if type(v) ~= "table" then return v end
    seen = seen or {}
    if seen[v] then return seen[v] end
    local t = {}
    seen[v] = t
    for k, x in pairs(v) do t[dcopy(k, seen)] = dcopy(x, seen) end
    return t
end

local function cnt(t)
    local n = 0
    if type(t) == "table" then for _ in pairs(t) do n = n + 1 end end
    return n
end

local fmt = "%s_UrA_Paster_nn"
do
    local ok, r = pcall(string.format, "%*_UrA_Paster_nn", "X")
    if ok and r == "X_UrA_Paster_nn" then fmt = "%*_UrA_Paster_nn" end
end

local function idp() return string.format(fmt, plr.Name) end
local function idf() return cph(idp()) end
local function clk() return cph(tostring(workspace:GetServerTimeNow())) end

local nonces = {}

local function cls(v)
    local t = type(v)
    if t == "number" then return "number", v end
    if t == "table" then return "table", v end
    if t == "string" then
        if nonces[v] then return "nonce", v end
        local d = cph(v)
        if d == idp() then return "identity", d end
        if istxt(d) and tonumber(d) then return "clock", tonumber(d) end
        return "nonce", v
    end
    return t, v
end

local function disc(a1)
    local r = { kinds = {}, maxKey = 0, nonce = nil, nonceSlot = nil, descValue = nil }
    if type(a1) ~= "table" then return r end
    for k, v in pairs(a1) do
        local kind, val = cls(v)
        r.kinds[k] = kind
        if type(k) == "number" and k > r.maxKey then r.maxKey = k end
        if kind == "nonce" and r.nonce == nil then r.nonce, r.nonceSlot = val, k end
        if kind == "table" then r.descValue = v end
    end
    return r
end

local lbl = { identity = "I", nonce = "N", clock = "C", number = "#", table = "T" }

local function shp(d)
    local o = {}
    for k = 1, d.maxKey do o[k] = lbl[d.kinds[k]] or "." end
    return table.concat(o)
end

local function valid(d, ri)
    local bad, N = {}, d.maxKey
    if N < 2 then return { "N=" .. tostring(N) .. " < 2" } end
    if d.kinds[N] ~= "table" then
        bad[#bad + 1] = string.format("[%d] descriptor missing, got %s", N, tostring(d.kinds[N]))
    end
    for k = 1, N - 1 do
        local want
        if k == ri then want = "nonce"
        elseif k == 1 then want = "identity"
        elseif k == 2 then want = "clock"
        else want = "number" end
        if d.kinds[k] ~= want then
            bad[#bad + 1] = string.format("[%d] want %s got %s", k, want, tostring(d.kinds[k]))
        end
    end
    return bad
end

local tpl = nil
local out = { n = 5, a2d = false }

local function learn(d)
    if type(d.descValue) ~= "table" then return end
    tpl = { desc = dcopy(d.descValue), descN = cnt(d.descValue) }
end

local function build(nc, ri)
    local N = math.random(1, 8) + 1
    local p = {}
    p[1] = idf()
    p[2] = clk()
    for k = 3, N - 1 do p[k] = math.random(5, 100000) end
    if type(ri) == "number" and ri >= 1 and ri < N then
        p[ri] = nc
    end
    p[N] = (tpl and dcopy(tpl.desc)) or { {}, { 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 }, {}, {}, {}, {} }
    return p, N
end

do
    local prev = (getgenv and getgenv() or _G).dreameremulator
    if type(prev) == "table" and type(prev.stop) == "function" then pcall(prev.stop) end
end

local ev = rs:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("RemoteEvent")

local emulator = {}
emulator.phase = "LEARN"
emulator.running = true

local st = { learned = 0, sent = 0, fail = 0, suppressed = 0, retries = 0,
             inbound = 0, gamePackets = 0, lawBad = 0, killed = 0, sweeps = 0 }

local mine = false
local last = nil
local pend = nil
local lastIn = 0

local halted = false
local learn_fails = 0
local MAX_LEARN_FAILS = 3

local function fire(p, N, tag)
    if halted then return false end
    local desc1 = p[N] and p[N][1]
    if type(desc1) ~= "table" then desc1 = {} end
    local outer = { p, out.a2d and desc1 or {} }

    mine = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function() ev:FireServer(table.unpack(outer, 1, out.n)) end)
    mine = false

    if ok then
        st.sent = st.sent + 1
    else
        st.fail = st.fail + 1
    end
    return ok
end

ev.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a1, a2, a3)
    local nonce
    if typeof(a1) == "buffer" then nonce = buffer.tostring(a1)
    elseif type(a1) == "string" then nonce = a1 end
    if not nonce then return end
    if halted then return end

    nonces[nonce] = true
    st.inbound = st.inbound + 1
    lastIn = os.clock()

    local ri = (type(a2) == "number") and a2 or 1
    last = { nonce = nonce, ri = ri }

    local p, N = build(nonce, ri)
    pend = { packet = p, ri = ri }

    if emulator.phase == "LIVE" then fire(p, N, "auto") end
end)

local function obs(a, sup)
    if halted then return end
    local d = disc(a[1])
    st.gamePackets = st.gamePackets + 1

    out.n = a.n
    local desc = a[1] and a[1][d.maxKey]
    out.a2d = (type(desc) == "table" and desc[1] ~= nil and a[2] == desc[1])

    learn(d)

    local ri = pend and pend.ri or nil
    local gbad = valid(d, ri)
    local obad = pend and valid(disc(pend.packet), ri) or {}
    if #gbad > 0 or #obad > 0 then
        st.lawBad = st.lawBad + 1
    end

    if emulator.phase == "LEARN" then
        if #gbad > 0 or #obad > 0 then
            learn_fails = learn_fails + 1
            st.learned = 0
            if learn_fails >= MAX_LEARN_FAILS then
                halted = true
                emulator.phase = "HALT"
                kick("something with emulation went wrong, please rejoin. this should not persist; if it does, join discord.gg/daydreamer")
                return
            end
        else
            learn_fails = 0
            st.learned = st.learned + 1
            if st.learned >= lpk then
                emulator.phase = "LIVE"
                print("checkmate")
            else
                print("a")
            end
        end
    end
    pend = nil
end

local function wrap(f)
    if type(newcclosure) == "function" then
        local ok, c = pcall(newcclosure, f)
        if ok then return c end
    end
    return f
end

local function tap(self, ...)
    if self ~= ev or mine then return false end
    local args = table.pack(...)
    local suppress = (emulator.phase == "LIVE")
    if suppress then st.suppressed = st.suppressed + 1 end
    task.defer(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(obs, args, suppress)
        if not ok then
            halted = true
            emulator.phase = "HALT"
            kick("something with emulation went wrong, please rejoin. this should not persist; if it does, join discord.gg/daydreamer")
        end
    end)
    return suppress
end

local mode, ofire, oncall = "none", nil, nil
local htgt = nil

local function ohook()
    local g = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local o = g.oth
    if type(o) == "table" and type(o.hook) == "function" then return o.hook, "oth.hook" end
    if type(hookfunction) == "function" then return hookfunction, "hookfunction" end
    return nil, nil
end

local function ncall(self, ...)
    if self == ev then
        local m = (getnamecallmethod and getnamecallmethod()) or ""
        if m == "FireServer" and tap(self, ...) then return end
    end
    return oncall(self, ...)
end

do
    local hook, hname = ohook()
    if hook and type(getrawmetatable) == "function" then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local mt = getrawmetatable(game)
            if type(setreadonly) == "function" then pcall(setreadonly, mt, false) end
            local orig = rawget(mt, "__namecall")
            if type(orig) == "function" then
                htgt = orig
                oncall = hook(orig, wrap(ncall))
            end
        end)
        if ok and oncall then mode = hname .. ":__namecall" end
    end
end

if mode == "none" and type(hookmetamethod) == "function" then
    local ok = pcall(function()
        oncall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(ncall))
    end)
    if ok and oncall then mode = "hookmetamethod" end
end

if mode == "none" then
    local hook, hname = ohook()
    if hook then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            htgt = ev.FireServer
            ofire = hook(ev.FireServer, wrap(function(self, ...)
                if tap(self, ...) then return end
                return ofire(self, ...)
            end))
        end)
        if ok and ofire then mode = hname .. ":FireServer" end
    end
end

local me = coroutine.running()

local function towner(t)
    for lvl = 1, 32 do
        local ok, s = pcall(debug.info, t, lvl, "s")
        if not ok or s == nil then break end
        if type(s) == "string" and s ~= "" and string.find(s, rtgt, 1, true) then
            return s
        end
    end
    if type(getscriptfromthread) == "function" then
        local ok, sc = pcall(getscriptfromthread, t)
        if ok and typeof(sc) == "Instance" then
            local n = sc:GetFullName()
            if string.find(n, rtgt, 1, true) then return n end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function rsweep()
    if type(getthreads) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, list = pcall(getthreads)
    if not ok or type(list) ~= "table" then return end
    st.sweeps = st.sweeps + 1

    local cur = coroutine.running()
    for _, t in ipairs(list) do
        if type(t) == "thread" and t ~= me and t ~= cur
           and coroutine.status(t) ~= "dead" then
            local src = towner(t)
            if src then
                local killed = pcall(coroutine.close, t)
                if not killed and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
                    killed = pcall(task.cancel, t)
                end
                if killed then
                    st.killed = st.killed + 1
                    if st.killed <= 3 or st.killed % 25 == 0 then
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while emulator.running do
        if emulator.phase == "LIVE" then rsweep() end
        task.wait(rwait)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while emulator.running do
        task.wait(1)
        if emulator.phase == "LIVE" and last and lastIn > 0
           and os.clock() - lastIn > retry then
            st.retries = st.retries + 1
            local p, N = build(last.nonce, last.ri)
            fire(p, N, "retry")
            lastIn = os.clock()
        end
    end
end)

emulator.stats = st

emulator.status = function()
    --[[
    print(string.format("phase=%s  learned=%d/%d  inbound=%d  sent=%d fail=%d retries=%d",
        emulator.phase, st.learned, lpk, st.inbound, st.sent, st.fail, st.retries))
    print(string.format("game packets=%d  suppressed=%d  law broken=%d",
        st.gamePackets, st.suppressed, st.lawBad))
    print(string.format("reaper sweeps=%d  threads closed=%d  gap since inbound=%.2fs",
        st.sweeps, st.killed, lastIn > 0 and (os.clock() - lastIn) or -1))
    print(string.format("descriptor=%s entries  argc=%d  arg2=%s",
        tpl and tpl.descN or "none", out.n, out.a2d and "desc[1]" or "fresh {}"))]]
end

emulator.stop = function()
    emulator.running = false
    emulator.phase = "LEARN"
    local o = (getgenv and getgenv() or _G).oth
    if htgt and type(o) == "table" and type(o.unhook) == "function" then
        pcall(o.unhook, htgt)
    end
    --[[print("stopped: game outbound passes through again, no more sends or sweeps.")
    print("threads already closed stay dead -- rejoin to restore " .. rtgt .. ".")]]
end
getgenv().dreameremulator = emulator
return emulator

end)

-- ─── EclipseUI adapter ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('ui_eclipse', function()
--[[
    _ui_eclipse.lua
    EclipseUI adapter for Daydreamer Rivals
    Wraps the EclipseUI Library (pastebin-11) into the API surface that
    every Daydreamer tab factory (combat, misc, wallhack, etc.) expects.

    Old API              -> New API
    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    ui:add_tab(name)     -> Library:Tab(name, icon)  [icon auto-mapped]
    tab:add_toggle(id, label, desc)   -> Group acting as a collapsible section
    section:add_slider(id, label, min, max, unit, default)
    section:add_switch(id, label, default)
    section:add_dropdown(id, label, options)
    section:add_button(id, label, display)
    section:add_textbox(id, label, default, placeholder)
    section:add_color_picker(id, label, default_color)
    section:add_status_row(id, label) -> status object
    section:set_visible(v)
    ui:on_change(key, fn)             -> internal listener table
    ui:set_value(key, val, silent)
    ui:notify({title, text, duration})
    ui:unload()
    ui.state[section_id][sub_id]
    ui:finalize_defaults()
    ui:add_unload_tab(fn)
]]

local Players          = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService     = game:GetService("TweenService")
local RunService       = game:GetService("RunService")
local CoreGui          = game:GetService("CoreGui")

-- ─── colour palette ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local CFG = {
    MainColor      = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14),
    SecondaryColor = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 26),
    AccentColor    = Color3.fromRGB(189, 172, 255),
    TextColor      = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200),
    TextDark       = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120),
    StrokeColor    = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40),
    Font           = Enum.Font.Code,
    BaseSize       = Vector2.new(620, 460),
}

-- ─── utilities ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local function Create(class, props, children)
    local inst = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do inst[k] = v end
    for _, child in pairs(children or {}) do child.Parent = inst end
    return inst
end

local function Tween(obj, props, t, style, dir)
    TweenService:Create(obj,
        TweenInfo.new(t or 0.2,
            style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
            dir   or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        props):Play()
end

local function GetTextSize(text, size, font)
    return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(
        text, size, font, Vector2.new(10000, 10000))
end

-- ─── icon map: tab name -> rbxassetid ────────────────────────────────────────
local TAB_ICONS = {

    cosmetics   = "14042703711",
    spoofers    = "14042703827",
    wallhack    = "14042703655",

-- ─── ScreenGui ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local ScreenGui = Create("ScreenGui", {
    Name            = "DaydreamerEclipse",
    Parent          = CoreGui,
    ZIndexBehavior  = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    ResetOnSpawn    = false,
    IgnoreGuiInset  = true,
})

local UIScale = Create("UIScale", { Parent = ScreenGui })

local function UpdateScale()
    local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
    local w  = (vp.X - 40) / CFG.BaseSize.X
    local h  = (vp.Y - 40) / CFG.BaseSize.Y
    UIScale.Scale = math.max(math.min(w, h, 1), 0.6)
end
workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(UpdateScale)
UpdateScale()

-- ─── Notifications ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local NotifContainer = Create("Frame", {
    Parent              = ScreenGui,
    Position            = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
    AnchorPoint         = Vector2.new(1, 0),
    Size                = UDim2.new(0, 300, 1, 0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    ZIndex              = 100,
})
Create("UIListLayout", {
    Parent              = NotifContainer,
    Padding             = UDim.new(0, 5),
    HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
    VerticalAlignment   = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
})

local function ShowNotify(title, text, duration)
    local msg = (title and title ~= "" and (title .. ": ") or "") .. (text or "")
    local Frame = Create("Frame", {
        Parent              = NotifContainer,
        Size                = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30),
        BackgroundColor3    = CFG.MainColor,
        BorderSizePixel     = 0,
        ClipsDescendants    = true,
    }, {
        Create("UIStroke",  { Color = CFG.AccentColor, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5 }),
        Create("Frame",     { Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = CFG.AccentColor }),
        Create("TextLabel", {
            Text                = msg,
            TextColor3          = CFG.TextColor,
            Font                = CFG.Font,
            TextSize            = 12,
            Size                = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0),
            Position            = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextXAlignment      = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }),
    })
    Tween(Frame, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 36) }, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    task.delay(duration or 3, function()
        Tween(Frame, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.4)
        task.wait(0.4)
        pcall(function() Frame:Destroy() end)
    end)
end

-- ─── Tooltip ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local TooltipLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
    Parent              = ScreenGui,
    Size                = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20),
    BackgroundColor3    = CFG.SecondaryColor,
    TextColor3          = CFG.TextColor,
    TextSize            = 11,
    Font                = CFG.Font,
    BorderSizePixel     = 0,
    Visible             = false,
    ZIndex              = 200,
}, {
    Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,5) }),
    Create("UIStroke",  { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
})

local function AddTooltip(obj, text)
    if not text or text == "" then return end
    obj.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TooltipLabel.Text = text
        TooltipLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(GetTextSize(text, 11, CFG.Font).X + 12, 20)
        TooltipLabel.Visible = true
    end)
    obj.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TooltipLabel.Visible = false
    end)
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if TooltipLabel.Visible then
        local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        TooltipLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X + 15, m.Y + 15)
    end
end)

-- ─── Main window ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local MainFrame = Create("Frame", {
    Name            = "DaydreamerMain",
    Parent          = ScreenGui,
    Size            = UDim2.fromOffset(CFG.BaseSize.X, CFG.BaseSize.Y),
    Position        = UDim2.new(0.5, -310, 0.5, -230),
    BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, {
    Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
    Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
})

-- dragging
do
    local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil, nil
    MainFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
           inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging  = true
            dragStart = inp.Position
            startPos  = MainFrame.Position
            inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    MainFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or
           inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragInput = inp
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp == dragInput and dragging then
            local d = inp.Position - dragStart
            Tween(MainFrame, {
                Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            }, 0.05)
        end
    end)
end

-- top bar
local TopBar = Create("Frame", {
    Parent          = MainFrame,
    Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
    BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, {
    Create("Frame", {
        Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        Position         = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = CFG.StrokeColor,
    }),
})

local TitleLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
    Parent          = TopBar,
    Text            = "daydreamer | rivals",
    TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
    TextSize        = 13,
    Font            = CFG.Font,
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size            = UDim2.new(0, 220, 1, 0),
    Position        = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
    TextXAlignment  = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
    RichText        = true,
})

-- animated title typewriter
task.spawn(function()
    local frames = {
        '','d','da','day','dayd','daydr','daydre','daydrea','daydream','daydreame','daydreamer',
        'daydreamer ','daydreamer |','daydreamer | ','daydreamer | r','daydreamer | ri',
        'daydreamer | riv','daydreamer | riva','daydreamer | rival','daydreamer | rivals',
        'daydreamer | rival','daydreamer | riva','daydreamer | riv','daydreamer | ri',
        'daydreamer | r','daydreamer | ','daydreamer |','daydreamer ','daydreamer',
        'daydreame','daydream','daydrea','daydre','daydr','dayd','day','da','d',
    }
    while MainFrame and MainFrame.Parent do
        for _, text in ipairs(frames) do
            if not (MainFrame and MainFrame.Parent) then break end
            local display = text
            if text:find("rivals", 1, true) then
                display = text:gsub("rivals", '<font color="#bdacff">rivals</font>')
            end
            TitleLabel.Text = display
            task.wait(0.12)
        end
    end
end)

-- content area
local ContentContainer = Create("Frame", {
    Parent          = MainFrame,
    Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -30),
    Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
})

local Sidebar = Create("Frame", {
    Parent          = ContentContainer,
    Size            = UDim2.new(0, 56, 1, 0),
    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 17),
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, {
    Create("Frame", {
        Size             = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0),
        Position         = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = CFG.StrokeColor,
    }),
    Create("UIListLayout", {
        Padding             = UDim.new(0, 8),
        HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
        VerticalAlignment   = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
    }),
    Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12) }),
})

local PagesContainer = Create("Frame", {
    Parent          = ContentContainer,
    Size            = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, 0),
    Position        = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
})

-- toggle button (top-right)
local Visible   = true
local MenuKey   = Enum.KeyCode.Insert
local TabList   = {}

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
    if not gpe and inp.KeyCode == MenuKey then
        Visible = not Visible
        MainFrame.Visible = Visible
    end
end)

local MobileBtn = Create("ImageButton", {
    Parent          = ScreenGui,
    Size            = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38),
    Position        = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8),
    AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
    BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
    Image           = "rbxassetid://3926305904",
    ImageColor3     = CFG.AccentColor,
    AutoButtonColor = false,
}, {
    Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }),
    Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.AccentColor, Thickness = 2 }),
})
MobileBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Visible = not Visible
    MainFrame.Visible = Visible
end)

-- ─── Tab switching ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local function SwitchTab(targetPage, targetBtn)
    for _, t in ipairs(TabList) do
        Tween(t.btn, { BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor }, 0.15)
        t.btn.Icon.ImageColor3 = CFG.TextDark
        t.page.Visible = false
    end
    Tween(targetBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor }, 0.15)
    targetBtn.Icon.ImageColor3 = CFG.AccentColor
    targetPage.Visible = true
end

-- ─── Adapter state storage ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ui.state["section_id"] = { enabled = bool, sub_id = value, ... }
-- ui._listeners["section_id.sub_id"] = { fn, fn, ... }

local AdapterUI = {
    state        = {},
    _listeners   = {},
    _unload_fns  = {},
    _conns       = {},
    descriptions = {},
    lib          = {},
    Flags        = {},
    Unloaded     = false,
}

function AdapterUI:_fire(key, value)
    local list = self._listeners[key]
    if not list then return end
    for _, fn in ipairs(list) do
        pcall(fn, value)
    end
end

function AdapterUI:on_change(key, fn)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
    self._listeners[key] = self._listeners[key] or {}
    table.insert(self._listeners[key], fn)
end

function AdapterUI:set_value(key, val, silent)
    -- key is "section_id.sub_id" or "section_id.enabled"
    local sec_id, sub_id = key:match("^([^%.]+)%.(.+)$")
    if not sec_id then return end
    self.state[sec_id] = self.state[sec_id] or {}
    self.state[sec_id][sub_id] = val
    if not silent then
        self:_fire(key, val)
    end
end

function AdapterUI:notify(opts)
    if type(opts) == "table" then
        ShowNotify(opts.title, opts.text, opts.duration)
    elseif type(opts) == "string" then
        ShowNotify(nil, opts, 3)
    end
end

function AdapterUI:unload()
    self.Unloaded = true
    for _, fn in ipairs(self._unload_fns) do
        pcall(fn)
    end
    pcall(function() ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
end

function AdapterUI:finalize_defaults()
    -- nothing needed; state is already lazily initialised
end

function AdapterUI:add_unload_tab(fn)
    -- The old API adds an "Unload" tab with a button.
    -- We add a sidebar button that fires fn.
    local iconId = TAB_ICONS["unload"] or "14042703637"
    local btn = Create("TextButton", {
        Parent          = Sidebar,
        Size            = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36),
        BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
        Text            = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    }, {
        Create("ImageLabel", {
            Name            = "Icon",
            Size            = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0.6, 0),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.2, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image           = "rbxassetid://" .. iconId,
            ImageColor3     = CFG.TextDark,
        }),
        Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
    })
    AddTooltip(btn, "Unload")
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        pcall(fn)
    end)
    table.insert(self._unload_fns, fn)
end

-- ─── add_tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function AdapterUI:add_tab(name)
    local iconId = TAB_ICONS[name:lower()] or TAB_ICONS["misc"]

    -- sidebar button
    local TabBtn = Create("TextButton", {
        Parent          = Sidebar,
        Size            = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36),
        BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
        Text            = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    }, {
        Create("ImageLabel", {
            Name            = "Icon",
            Size            = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0.6, 0),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.2, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image           = "rbxassetid://" .. iconId,
            ImageColor3     = CFG.TextDark,
        }),
        Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
    })
    AddTooltip(TabBtn, name)

    -- page (two-column scrolling)
    local PageFrame = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
        Parent              = PagesContainer,
        Size                = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Visible             = false,
        ScrollBarThickness  = 2,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = CFG.AccentColor,
        CanvasSize          = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
    })
    local PagePad = Create("UIPadding", {
        Parent        = PageFrame,
        PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, 12),
        PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, 12),
        PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, 12),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12),
    })

    local LeftCol = Create("Frame", {
        Parent          = PageFrame,
        Size            = UDim2.new(0.49, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, {
        Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
    })
    local RightCol = Create("Frame", {
        Parent          = PageFrame,
        Size            = UDim2.new(0.49, 0, 1, 0),
        Position        = UDim2.new(0.51, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, {
        Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
    })

    local entry = { btn = TabBtn, page = PageFrame }
    table.insert(TabList, entry)

    TabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        SwitchTab(PageFrame, TabBtn)
    end)

    if #TabList == 1 then
        SwitchTab(PageFrame, TabBtn)
    end

    -- ── Tab object ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local ui_ref    = self
    local LeftSide  = true          -- alternates per add_toggle call

    local Tab = {
        left_col  = LeftCol,
        right_col = RightCol,
        _name     = name,
    }

    -- ── add_toggle: returns a Section object ─────────────────────────────────
    function Tab:add_toggle(id, label, description)
        -- initialise state
        ui_ref.state[id] = ui_ref.state[id] or {}
        local sec_state   = ui_ref.state[id]
        if sec_state.enabled == nil then sec_state.enabled = false end

        local ParentCol = LeftSide and LeftCol or RightCol
        LeftSide = not LeftSide

        -- outer group frame
        local GroupFrame = Create("Frame", {
            Parent          = ParentCol,
            Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize   = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 17),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, {
            Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
            Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }),
        })

        -- header row (acts as master toggle button)
        local HeaderBtn = Create("TextButton", {
            Parent          = GroupFrame,
            Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
            BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text            = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
        }, {
            Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }),
            -- bottom filler so rounded top doesn't show gap
            Create("Frame", {
                Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 4),
                Position         = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -4),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                BorderSizePixel  = 0,
            }),
        })

        -- accent dot top-right
        Create("Frame", {
            Parent          = HeaderBtn,
            Size            = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
            Position        = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, -2),
            BackgroundColor3 = CFG.AccentColor,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0) }) })

        -- enabled checkbox (small square left of label)
        local EnabledBox = Create("Frame", {
            Parent          = HeaderBtn,
            Size            = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -5),
            BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, { Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }) })

        local EnabledCheck = Create("Frame", {
            Parent          = EnabledBox,
            Size            = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            BackgroundColor3 = CFG.AccentColor,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        })

        local TitleLbl = Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent          = HeaderBtn,
            Text            = label,
            TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
            TextSize        = 11,
            Font            = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size            = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0),
            TextXAlignment  = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        })

        if description and description ~= "" then
            AddTooltip(HeaderBtn, description)
        end

        -- content area
        local ContentFrame = Create("Frame", {
            Parent          = GroupFrame,
            Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26),
            AutomaticSize   = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }, {
            Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
            Create("UIPadding", {
                PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, 7),
                PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7),
                PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, 8),
                PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, 8),
            }),
        })

        -- toggle logic
        local function SetEnabled(v)
            sec_state.enabled = v
            Tween(EnabledCheck, { BackgroundTransparency = v and 0 or 1 }, 0.1)
            Tween(TitleLbl, { TextColor3 = v and CFG.TextColor or CFG.TextDark }, 0.1)
            ui_ref:_fire(id .. ".enabled", v)
        end

        HeaderBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SetEnabled(not sec_state.enabled)
        end)

        -- ── Section object ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        local Section = {
            frame       = GroupFrame,
            _id         = id,
            _content    = ContentFrame,
            _visible    = true,
        }

        function Section:set_visible(v)
            self._visible = v
            GroupFrame.Visible = v
        end

        -- ── helpers shared across items ────────────────────────────────────────
        local function item_row(height)
            return Create("Frame", {
                Parent              = ContentFrame,
                Size                = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            })
        end

        local function item_label(parent, text, x_off, y_off, w_shrink)
            return Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent              = parent,
                Text                = text,
                TextColor3          = CFG.TextDark,
                TextSize            = 11,
                Font                = CFG.Font,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size                = UDim2.new(1, -(w_shrink or 0), 0, 14),
                Position            = UDim2.new(0, x_off or 0, 0, y_off or 0),
                TextXAlignment      = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            })
        end

        -- ── add_switch (boolean, no master toggle behaviour) ──────────────────
        function Section:add_switch(sub_id, text, default)
            sec_state[sub_id] = sec_state[sub_id] ~= nil and sec_state[sub_id] or (default == true)
            local Row    = item_row(18)
            local Box    = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -5),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, { Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }) })
            local Check  = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = Box,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0),
                AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.AccentColor,
                BackgroundTransparency = sec_state[sub_id] and 0 or 1,
            })
            local Lbl    = item_label(Row, text, 16, 2)
            Lbl.TextColor3 = sec_state[sub_id] and CFG.TextColor or CFG.TextDark

            local Btn    = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text            = "",
            })
            Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                sec_state[sub_id] = not sec_state[sub_id]
                local v = sec_state[sub_id]
                Tween(Check, { BackgroundTransparency = v and 0 or 1 }, 0.1)
                Tween(Lbl,   { TextColor3 = v and CFG.TextColor or CFG.TextDark }, 0.1)
                ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, v)
            end)
        end

        -- ── add_slider ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function Section:add_slider(sub_id, text, min, max, unit, default, step)
            step = step or 1
            local initVal = sec_state[sub_id]
            if initVal == nil then initVal = default ~= nil and default or min end
            sec_state[sub_id] = initVal

            local Row       = item_row(30)
            local Lbl       = item_label(Row, text, 0, 0)
            local ValLbl    = Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Text            = tostring(initVal) .. (unit or ""),
                TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
                TextSize        = 11,
                Font            = CFG.Font,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                TextXAlignment  = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            })
            local Track     = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 18),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0) }),
            })
            local initPct   = (initVal - min) / math.max(max - min, 1)
            local Fill      = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = Track,
                Size            = UDim2.new(initPct, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.AccentColor,
            }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0) }) })

            local dragging = false
            local function UpdateSlider(inp)
                local sz  = Track.AbsoluteSize.X
                local pos = Track.AbsolutePosition.X
                local pct = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - pos) / sz, 0, 1)
                local raw = min + (max - min) * pct
                local val
                if step < 1 then
                    local factor = 1 / step
                    val = math.floor(raw * factor + 0.5) / factor
                else
                    val = math.floor(raw + 0.5)
                end
                val = math.clamp(val, min, max)
                sec_state[sub_id] = val
                Fill.Size  = UDim2.new((val - min) / math.max(max - min, 1), 0, 1, 0)
                ValLbl.Text = tostring(val) .. (unit or "")
                ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, val)
            end

            local ClickArea = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 10),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text            = "",
            })
            ClickArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
                   inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    UpdateSlider(inp)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                if dragging and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or
                                 inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    UpdateSlider(inp)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
                   inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)

            local opt = { _visible = true }
            function opt:set_visible(v)
                self._visible = v
                Row.Visible = v
            end
            return opt
        end

        -- ── add_dropdown ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function Section:add_dropdown(sub_id, text, options)
            local cur = sec_state[sub_id] or options[1]
            sec_state[sub_id] = cur

            local Row     = item_row(34)
            item_label(Row, text, 0, 0)

            local MainBox = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 15),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text            = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ZIndex          = 5,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
                Create("TextLabel", {
                    Name            = "Val",
                    Text            = tostring(cur),
                    Size            = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, 0),
                    Position        = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    TextColor3      = CFG.TextColor,
                    TextSize        = 11,
                    Font            = CFG.Font,
                    TextXAlignment  = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                }),
                Create("TextLabel", {
                    Text            = "▼",
                    Size            = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, 0),
                    Position        = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
                    TextSize        = 9,
                }),
            })

            local ListFrame = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
                Parent              = MainBox,
                Size                = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
                Position            = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 2),
                BackgroundColor3    = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                BorderSizePixel     = 0,
                Visible             = false,
                ZIndex              = 20,
                CanvasSize          = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                ScrollBarThickness  = 2,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
            })

            local expanded = false
            for _, opt in ipairs(options) do
                local Btn = Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent          = ListFrame,
                    Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Text            = tostring(opt),
                    TextColor3      = (opt == cur) and CFG.AccentColor or CFG.TextDark,
                    TextSize        = 11,
                    Font            = CFG.Font,
                    ZIndex          = 21,
                })
                Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    cur = opt
                    sec_state[sub_id] = opt
                    MainBox.Val.Text = tostring(opt)
                    expanded = false
                    Tween(ListFrame, { Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0) }, 0.1)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    ListFrame.Visible = false
                    ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, opt)
                end)
            end

            MainBox.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                expanded = not expanded
                if expanded then
                    ListFrame.Visible = true
                    Tween(ListFrame, {
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.min(#options * 18, 90))
                    }, 0.1)
                else
                    Tween(ListFrame, { Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0) }, 0.1)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    ListFrame.Visible = false
                end
            end)

            local opt_obj = { _visible = true }
            function opt_obj:set_visible(v)
                self._visible = v
                Row.Visible = v
            end
            return opt_obj
        end

        -- ── add_button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function Section:add_button(sub_id, text, display)
            local Row = item_row(22)
            local Btn = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                Text            = display or text,
                TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
                Font            = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize        = 10,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
            })
            Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, true)
            end)
        end

        -- ── add_textbox ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function Section:add_textbox(sub_id, text, default, placeholder)
            sec_state[sub_id] = sec_state[sub_id] or default or ""
            local Row = item_row(33)
            item_label(Row, text, 0, 0)
            local Box = Create("TextBox", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 15),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.SecondaryColor,
                TextColor3      = CFG.TextColor,
                PlaceholderText = placeholder or "...",
                Text            = tostring(sec_state[sub_id]),
                Font            = CFG.Font,
                TextSize        = 11,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
                Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5) }),
            })
            Box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                sec_state[sub_id] = Box.Text
                ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, Box.Text)
            end)
        end

        -- ── add_color / add_color_picker ───────────────────────────────────────
        local function build_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
            local Color = default_color or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            sec_state[sub_id] = sec_state[sub_id] or Color

            local Row     = item_row(18)
            item_label(Row, text, 0, 2, 38)
            local H, S, V = Color:ToHSV()

            local Preview = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Size            = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 14),
                AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position        = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color,
                Text            = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
            })

            local PickerFrame = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = Preview,
                Size            = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 0),
                Position        = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 4),
                AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = CFG.MainColor,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ClipsDescendants = true,
                ZIndex          = 60,
            }, {
                Create("UIStroke", { Color = CFG.StrokeColor }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }),
            })

            local SatVal = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = PickerFrame,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1,-18,0,90),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0,9,0,9),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(H,1,1),
                Text            = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
            }, {
                Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Size            = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Image           = "rbxassetid://4801885019",
                }),
                Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Size            = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Image           = "rbxassetid://4801885019",
                    ImageColor3     = Color3.new(0,0,0),
                    Rotation        = 90,
                }),
            })

            local Cursor = Create("Frame", {
                Parent          = SatVal,
                Size            = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1),
                AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position        = UDim2.new(S, 0, 1-V, 0),
            }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0) }) })

            local HueBar = Create("TextButton", {
                Parent          = PickerFrame,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1,-18,0,10),
                Position        = UDim2.new(0,9,0,108),
                Text            = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
            }, {
                Create("UIGradient", {
                    Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    Color3.fromHSV(0,    1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.17, Color3.fromHSV(0.17, 1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromHSV(0.33, 1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  Color3.fromHSV(0.5,  1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67, Color3.fromHSV(0.67, 1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromHSV(0.83, 1,1)),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    Color3.fromHSV(1,    1,1)),
                    }),
                }),
                Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }),
            })

            local function UpdateColor()
                Color = Color3.fromHSV(H, S, V)
                sec_state[sub_id] = Color
                Preview.BackgroundColor3 = Color
                SatVal.BackgroundColor3  = Color3.fromHSV(H, 1, 1)
                Cursor.Position          = UDim2.new(S, 0, 1-V, 0)
                ui_ref:_fire(id .. "." .. sub_id, Color)
            end

            local draggingHSV, draggingHue = false, false

            SatVal.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
                   inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    draggingHSV = true
                end
            end)
            HueBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
                   inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    draggingHue = true
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
                   inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    draggingHSV = false
                    draggingHue = false
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and
                   inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                if draggingHSV then
                    local sz  = SatVal.AbsoluteSize
                    local pos = SatVal.AbsolutePosition
                    S = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - pos.X) / sz.X, 0, 1)
                    V = 1 - math.clamp((inp.Position.Y - pos.Y) / sz.Y, 0, 1)
                    UpdateColor()
                elseif draggingHue then
                    local sz  = HueBar.AbsoluteSize
                    local pos = HueBar.AbsolutePosition
                    H = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - pos.X) / sz.X, 0, 1)
                    UpdateColor()
                end
            end)

            local opened = false
            Preview.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                opened = not opened
                if opened then
                    Tween(PickerFrame, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 155) }, 0.2)
                else
                    Tween(PickerFrame, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 0) }, 0.2)
                end
            end)

            local opt = { _visible = true }
            function opt:set_visible(v)
                self._visible = v
                Row.Visible = v
            end
            return opt
        end

        function Section:add_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
            return build_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
        end
        function Section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
            return build_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
        end

        -- ── add_status_row ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function Section:add_status_row(sub_id, text)
            sec_state[sub_id] = sec_state[sub_id] or ""
            local Row = item_row(16)
            item_label(Row, text, 0, 1, 0)
            local ValLbl = Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent          = Row,
                Text            = tostring(sec_state[sub_id]),
                TextColor3      = CFG.TextDark,
                TextSize        = 10,
                Font            = CFG.Font,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size            = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                TextXAlignment  = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            })
            local row_obj = {}
            function row_obj:set_value(txt, color)
                sec_state[sub_id] = txt
                ValLbl.Text       = tostring(txt or "")
                ValLbl.TextColor3 = color or CFG.TextDark
            end
            return row_obj
        end

        return Section
    end

    return Tab
end

-- ─── Return the adapter as the "ui" object ────────────────────────────────────
return AdapterUI

end)

-- ─── _helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('helpers', function()
local helpers = {}

function helpers.dreamer(env)
    env = env or (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local dreamer = env.dreamer
    if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
        dreamer = {}
        env.dreamer = dreamer
    end
    return dreamer
end

function helpers.state(module_name, defaults, legacy_key)
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local dreamer = helpers.dreamer(env)
    local old_state = legacy_key and env[legacy_key] or nil
    local state = dreamer[module_name]
    if type(state) ~= "table" then
        state = type(old_state) == "table" and old_state or {}
        dreamer[module_name] = state
    end
    if legacy_key then
        env[legacy_key] = state
    end
    for key, value in pairs(defaults or {}) do
        if state[key] == nil then
            state[key] = value
        end
    end
    return state, dreamer
end

function helpers.class()
    local cls = {}
    cls.__index = cls
    return cls
end

function helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
end

function helpers.destroy(obj)
    if obj then
        pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
    end
end

function helpers.track(self, conn)
    self.conns = self.conns or {}
    self.conns[#self.conns + 1] = conn
    return conn
end

function helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do
        helpers.disconnect(conn)
    end
    if table.clear then
        table.clear(conns)
    else
        for i = #conns, 1, -1 do
            conns[i] = nil
        end
    end
end

function helpers.set_visible(opt, visible)
    if opt and opt.set_visible then
        opt:set_visible(visible == true)
    end
end

function helpers.status(row, text, color)
    if row then
        row:set_value(text, color)
    end
end

function helpers.enabled(ui, id)
    local st = ui and ui.state and ui.state[id]
    return st and (st.enabled == true or st.menu_enabled == true) or false
end

function helpers.short_name(name, max_len)
    name = tostring(name or "?")
    max_len = max_len or 12
    if #name > max_len then
        return string.sub(name, 1, max_len) .. "..."
    end
    return name
end

--// this parses "#rrggbb" / "rrggbb" / "r,g,b" (or a Color3) to Color3
function helpers.parse_color(str)
    if typeof and typeof(str) == "Color3" then return str end
    if type(str) ~= "string" then return nil end
    str = str:gsub("%s+", "")
    if str == "" then return nil end

    local r, g, b = str:match("^(%d+),(%d+),(%d+)$")
    if r then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.clamp(tonumber(r), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(g), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(b), 0, 255)
        )
    end

    local hex = str:gsub("#", "")
    if #hex == 3 then
        hex = hex:sub(1, 1):rep(2) .. hex:sub(2, 2):rep(2) .. hex:sub(3, 3):rep(2)
    end
    if #hex ~= 6 then return nil end
    local hr = tonumber(hex:sub(1, 2), 16)
    local hg = tonumber(hex:sub(3, 4), 16)
    local hb = tonumber(hex:sub(5, 6), 16)
    if not (hr and hg and hb) then return nil end
    return Color3.fromRGB(hr, hg, hb)
end

--// read a colour option (state key, "<key>_r/_g/_b" sliders, or "r,g,b"/hex string) -> Color3
function helpers.read_color(ui, id, key)
    local sec = ui.state and ui.state[id]
    local raw = sec and sec[key]
    if typeof and typeof(raw) == "Color3" then return raw end
    if sec then
        local r = sec[key .. "_r"]
        local g = sec[key .. "_g"]
        local b = sec[key .. "_b"]
        if r ~= nil or g ~= nil or b ~= nil then
            return Color3.fromRGB(
                math.clamp(tonumber(r) or 0, 0, 255),
                math.clamp(tonumber(g) or 0, 0, 255),
                math.clamp(tonumber(b) or 0, 0, 255)
            )
        end
    end
    return helpers.parse_color(raw)
end

--// normalise a user-typed rbxasset id. returns nil for empty/invalid input so callers
--// can fall back gracefully.
function helpers.asset_id(raw)
    if type(raw) ~= "string" then return nil end
    raw = raw:gsub("%s+", "")
    if raw == "" then return nil end
    if raw:match("^rbxassetid://") or raw:match("^http") or raw:match("^rbxasset") then
        return raw
    end
    local num = raw:match("(%d+)")
    if num then return "rbxassetid://" .. num end
    return nil
end

function helpers.lighting()
    return cloneref(game:GetService("Lighting"))
end

function helpers.run_service()
    return cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
end

--// elevate before touching instance props from non-main threads, then set a property.
function helpers.safe_set(self_or_ui, obj, prop, value)
    if not obj then return end
    local ui = type(self_or_ui) == "table" and (self_or_ui.ui or self_or_ui) or self_or_ui
    if ui and ui.elevate_thread then pcall(ui.elevate_thread) end
    pcall(function() obj[prop] = value end)
end

--// create/fetch a uniquely-named post effect we own under Lighting.
function helpers.own_effect(lighting, class, name)
    local inst = lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
    if inst and inst:IsA(class) then return inst end
    if inst then pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end) end
    local ok, created = pcall(Instance.new, class)
    if not ok or not created then return nil end
    created.Name = name
    created.Parent = lighting
    return created
end

--// destroy a child by name
function helpers.destroy_named(parent, name)
    local inst = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(name)
    if inst then pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end) end
end

--// snapshot/restore helpers for properties we mutate in-place on existing game instances
function helpers.snapshot(store, obj, props)
    if not obj then return end
    for _, p in ipairs(props) do
        if store[p] == nil then
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return obj[p] end)
            if ok then store[p] = v end
        end
    end
end

function helpers.restore(store, obj, props)
    if not obj then return end
    for _, p in ipairs(props) do
        if store[p] ~= nil then
            pcall(function() obj[p] = store[p] end)
        end
    end
end

--// prefer real colourwell but if it dont exist then we fallback to slider
--// this is so our entire script doesnt break n shit
function helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if type(section.add_color_picker) == "function" then
        return section:add_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    if type(section.add_color) == "function" then
        return section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    default_color = typeof(default_color) == "Color3" and default_color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_r", text .. " r", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.R * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_g", text .. " g", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.G * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_b", text .. " b", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.B * 255 + 0.5))
    return nil
end

--// return sorted dropdown list
function helpers.skybox_preset_list()
    local presets = helpers.skybox_presets()
    local names = { "default" }
    for name in pairs(presets) do
        names[#names + 1] = name
    end
    table.sort(names, function(a, b)
        if a == "default" then return true end
        if b == "default" then return false end
        return a < b
    end)
    names[#names + 1] = "custom"
    return names
end

--//ui, render, and teamcheck helper stuff
function helpers.get_hui()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local success, result = pcall(gethui)
        if success and result then return result end
    end
    local core_gui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local roblox_gui = core_gui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
    if roblox_gui then return roblox_gui end
    return core_gui
end

function helpers.is_enemy(player, local_player)
    if not player or player == local_player then return false end
    if player.Team ~= nil and player.Team == local_player.Team then
        return false
    end
    if player:GetAttribute("TeamID") ~= nil and player:GetAttribute("TeamID") == local_player:GetAttribute("TeamID") then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

function helpers.estimate_text_width(text, font_size)
    return math.floor(#text * font_size * 0.55)
end

function helpers.color_to_rgb_string(color)
    return string.format("%d,%d,%d", math.floor(color.R * 255), math.floor(color.G * 255), math.floor(color.B * 255))
end

local equipped_weapon_cache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function helpers.get_equipped_weapon_name(player, workspace_ref)
    if not player then return "none" end
    local now = os.clock()
    local cached = equipped_weapon_cache[player]
    if cached and now - cached.t < 0.15 then
        return cached.name
    end

    local function finish(name)
        equipped_weapon_cache[player] = { t = now, name = name }
        return name
    end

    local ws = workspace_ref or (typeof(workspace) == "Instance" and workspace) or cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
    local viewModels = ws:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not viewModels then return finish("none") end
    local playerName = player.Name

    local function weaponFromModelName(modelName)
        local dash = modelName:find("-", 1, true)
        if not dash then return nil end
        local owner = modelName:sub(1, dash - 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if owner == playerName then
            return modelName:sub(dash + 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        end
        return nil
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(viewModels:GetChildren()) do
        local w = weaponFromModelName(child.Name)
        if w then return finish(w) end
    end

    local fp = viewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    if fp then
        for _, child in ipairs(fp:GetChildren()) do
            local w = weaponFromModelName(child.Name)
            if w then return finish(w) end
        end
    end

    return finish("none")
end

--// this stuff is shared by bounding boxes and chams logic (maybe others i forgor)
local los_params = RaycastParams.new()
los_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
los_params.IgnoreWater = true
function helpers.has_line_of_sight(character, root_part, camera_position, local_player_char)
    if not (character and root_part) then return false end
    if not camera_position then return false end
    local aim_part = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or root_part
    local goal = aim_part.Position
    local direction = goal - camera_position
    if direction.Magnitude <= 0 then return true end

    los_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { local_player_char or workspace }
    local result = workspace:Raycast(camera_position, direction, los_params)
    return result == nil or result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(character)
end

function helpers.is_dynamic_box_part(part)
    return part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
end

function helpers.calculate_box(character, root_part, dynamic_box, project_fn, viewport_size, dynamic_parts)
    local min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y = 9000, 9000, -9000, -9000
    local box_width, box_height = 0, 0

    local px, py, on_screen, depth = project_fn(root_part.Position)
    if not on_screen then return 0, 0, 0, 0, false end
    if depth <= 0 then return 0, 0, 0, 0, false end

    local vp = viewport_size

    if dynamic_box then
        local parts = dynamic_parts or character:GetChildren()
        local has_parts = false
        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
            if part and part.Parent and helpers.is_dynamic_box_part(part) and part.Transparency ~= 1 then
                has_parts = true
                local part_cframe = part.CFrame
                local part_size = part.Size
                local hx, hy, hz = part_size.X / 2, part_size.Y / 2, part_size.Z / 2
                local sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(hx, hy, hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(-hx, hy, hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(hx, -hy, hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(hx, hy, -hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(-hx, hy, -hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(hx, -hy, -hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
                sx, sy = project_fn(part_cframe * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, -hz))
                min_x = math.min(min_x, sx); min_y = math.min(min_y, sy); max_x = math.max(max_x, sx); max_y = math.max(max_y, sy)
            end
        end
        if has_parts then
            box_width, box_height = max_x - min_x, max_y - min_y
        else
            local scale = (root_part.Size.Y * vp.Y) / (depth * 2)
            box_width, box_height = 3 * scale, 4.5 * scale
            min_x, min_y = px - (box_width / 2), py - (box_height / 2)
        end
    else
        local scale = (root_part.Size.Y * vp.Y) / (depth * 2)
        box_width, box_height = 3 * scale, 4.5 * scale
        min_x, min_y = px - (box_width / 2), py - (box_height / 2)
    end

    return box_width, box_height, min_x, min_y, on_screen
end

local R6_SKELETON_BONES = {
    { {"Head", oy = -0.5},         {"Torso", oy = 0.5} },
    { {"Torso", oy = 0.5},         {"Torso", oy = -0.5} },
    { {"Torso", ox = -0.5, oy = 0.25},  {"Left Arm",  oy = -0.5} },
    { {"Torso", ox = 0.5,  oy = 0.25},  {"Right Arm", oy = -0.5} },
    { {"Torso", ox = -0.25, oy = -0.5}, {"Left Leg",  oy = -0.5} },
    { {"Torso", ox = 0.25,  oy = -0.5}, {"Right Leg", oy = -0.5} },
}

local R15_SKELETON_BONES = {
    {"Head", "UpperTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
    {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"}, {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"}, {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
    {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"}, {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"}, {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
    {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"}, {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"}, {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
    {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"}, {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"}, {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
}

function helpers.get_skeleton_bones(character, parts_by_name)
    local upper = parts_by_name and parts_by_name.UpperTorso or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    local lower = parts_by_name and parts_by_name.LowerTorso or character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
    if upper and lower then
        return R15_SKELETON_BONES
    end
    return R6_SKELETON_BONES
end

function helpers.resolve_joint(character, spec, parts_by_name)
    local name = type(spec) == "table" and spec[1] or spec
    local part = parts_by_name and parts_by_name[name] or character:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not part then return nil end
    if type(spec) ~= "table" then return part.Position end
    local sz = part.Size
    return part.CFrame * Vector3.new((spec.ox or 0) * sz.X, (spec.oy or 0) * sz.Y, (spec.oz or 0) * sz.Z)
end

function helpers.getmuzzlepos()
    local view_models = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not view_models then return nil end
    local first_person = view_models:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    if not first_person then return nil end
    local player_name = local_player.Name
    for _, model in pairs(first_person:GetChildren()) do
        if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name:find("^" .. player_name) then
            local item_visual = model:FindFirstChild("ItemVisual")
            if item_visual then
                local body = item_visual:FindFirstChild("Body")
                if body then
                    local body_primary = body:FindFirstChild("BodyPrimary")
                    if body_primary then
                        local muzzle = body_primary:FindFirstChild("_muzzle")
                        if muzzle and muzzle:IsA("Attachment") then
                            return muzzle.WorldPosition
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

return helpers
end)

-- ─── _gunmods ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('gunmods', function()
local players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local replicated_storage = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local workspace = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
local local_player = players.LocalPlayer

local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local fenv = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    fenv[jit] = fenv[jit] or function(fn) return fn end
    fenv[nov] = fenv[nov] or function(fn) return fn end
end

local unpack = unpack or table.unpack

local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local old_state = env.daydreamer_rivals_gunmods
local state = dreamer.gunmods
if type(state) ~= "table" then
    state = type(old_state) == "table" and old_state or {}
    dreamer.gunmods = state
end
env.daydreamer_rivals_gunmods = state

state.rapid_fire = state.rapid_fire == true
state.no_spread = state.no_spread == true
state.no_recoil = state.no_recoil == true
state.fast_attack = state.fast_attack == true
state.instant_ads = state.instant_ads == true
state.always_backstab = state.always_backstab == true
state.backstab_range = tonumber(state.backstab_range) or 12
state.hooked_gun = state.hooked_gun == true
state.hooked_melee = state.hooked_melee == true
state.hooked_knife = state.hooked_knife == true
state.hooked_spread = state.hooked_spread == true

local Gunmods = {}
Gunmods.__index = Gunmods

function Gunmods.new(ui, tab, lib)
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        state = state,
        utility = nil,
    }, Gunmods)
end

function Gunmods:desc(id)
    local descriptions = self.ui and self.ui.descriptions or {}
    return descriptions[id]
end

function Gunmods:get_utility()
    if self.utility then return self.utility end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return require(replicated_storage.Modules.Utility)
    end)
    self.utility = ok and result or nil
    return self.utility
end

function Gunmods:is_teammate(player)
    if not player then return false end
    if player.Team ~= nil and player.Team == local_player.Team then
        return true
    end
    local my_team = local_player:GetAttribute("TeamID")
    return my_team ~= nil and my_team == player:GetAttribute("TeamID")
end

function Gunmods:valid_backstab_target(char)
    if not char or char == local_player.Character then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not root then return false end
    local player = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
    if player then
        return not self:is_teammate(player)
    end
    local range = workspace:FindFirstChild("ShootingRangeEntities")
    return range and char:IsDescendantOf(range) or false
end

function Gunmods:get_target_part(char)
    return char:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead") or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

function Gunmods:nearest_backstab_target(origin, max_dist)
    local best, best_dist = nil, max_dist or 12

    local function consider(char)
        if not self:valid_backstab_target(char) then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local dist = root and (root.Position - origin).Magnitude or math.huge
        if dist <= best_dist then
            best, best_dist = char, dist
        end
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= local_player then
            consider(player.Character)
        end
    end

    local range = workspace:FindFirstChild("ShootingRangeEntities")
    if range then
        for _, char in ipairs(range:GetChildren()) do
            consider(char)
        end
    end

    return best
end

function Gunmods:make_backstab_cf(target_root, target_part)
