    local look = target_root.CFrame.LookVector
    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    flat = flat.Magnitude > 1e-4 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    return (target_part and target_part.Position or target_root.Position) - flat * 1.75 + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0)
end

function Gunmods:make_backstab_data(item)
    if not self.state.always_backstab then return nil end
    if not (item and item.Info and tostring(item.Info.Name or ""):lower():find("knife", 1, true)) then return nil end

    local util = self:get_utility()
    if not util then return nil end

    local fighter = item.ClientFighter
    local entity = fighter and fighter.Entity
    local root = entity and entity.RootPart
    if not root then return nil end

    local reach = tonumber(item.Info.HeavyAttackReach or item.Info.AttackReach) or 7
    local max_range = math.max(reach, tonumber(self.state.backstab_range) or 12)
    local target = self:nearest_backstab_target(root.Position, max_range)
    local target_root = target and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not target_root then return nil end

    local target_part = self:get_target_part(target)
    local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.25, 0)
    local aim = self:make_backstab_cf(target_root, target_part)
    local cf = CFrame.lookAt(origin, aim)
    return {
        [utf8.char(1)] = {
            [utf8.char(0)] = util:EncodeCFrame(cf),
            [utf8.char(1)] = util:EncodeCFrame(cf),
            [utf8.char(2)] = target_part,
            [utf8.char(3)] = util:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(0.43, 0.25, 0.42)),
        },
    }
end

function Gunmods:hook_gun_module()
    local ok, gun_module = pcall(function()
        return require(local_player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
    end)
    if not (ok and type(gun_module) == "table") then return end

    if type(gun_module.StartShooting) == "function" and not self.state.original_gun_start_shooting then
        self.state.original_gun_start_shooting = gun_module.StartShooting
        gun_module.StartShooting = LPH_JIT(function(item, p26, p27)
            local old_cooldown
            if state.rapid_fire and item.Info then
                old_cooldown = item.Info.ShootCooldown
                item.Info.ShootCooldown = 0
            end
            local result = { state.original_gun_start_shooting(item, p26, p27) }
            if old_cooldown ~= nil and item.Info then
                item.Info.ShootCooldown = old_cooldown
            end
            return unpack(result)
        end)
    end

    if type(gun_module._Recoil) == "function" and not self.state.original_recoil then
        self.state.original_recoil = gun_module._Recoil
        gun_module._Recoil = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(item, multiplier)
            if state.no_recoil then return end
            return state.original_recoil(item, multiplier)
        end)
    end

    if type(gun_module.StartAiming) == "function" and not self.state.original_gun_start_aiming then
        self.state.original_gun_start_aiming = gun_module.StartAiming
        gun_module.StartAiming = LPH_JIT(function(item, ...)
            local result = { state.original_gun_start_aiming(item, ...) }
            if state.instant_ads and result[1] then
                local view_model = item and item.ViewModel
                local aim_spring = view_model and view_model._aim_spring
                if aim_spring then
                    aim_spring.Value = aim_spring.Target
                    view_model.CurrentAimValue = aim_spring.Target
                end
            end
            return unpack(result)
        end)
    end

    self.state.hooked_gun = true
end

function Gunmods:hook_spread()
    if self.state.hooked_spread then return end
    local ok, gun_module = pcall(function()
        return require(local_player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
    end)
    if not (ok and type(gun_module) == "table" and type(gun_module.StartShooting) == "function") then return end

    local old_start_shooting
    old_start_shooting = hookfunction(gun_module.StartShooting, function(...)
        local traceback = debug.traceback()
        if state.no_spread and traceback:find("ClientItem") and traceback:find("ClientFighter") and not traceback:find("ReplicatedController") then
            local values = { old_start_shooting(...) }
            values[4] = true
            return unpack(values)
        end
        return old_start_shooting(...)
    end)

    self.state.hooked_spread = true
end

function Gunmods:hook_melee_module()
    if self.state.hooked_melee then return end
    local ok, melee_module = pcall(function()
        return require(local_player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Melee)
    end)
    if not (ok and type(melee_module) == "table") then return end

    if type(melee_module.StartShooting) == "function" and not self.state.original_melee_start_shooting then
        self.state.original_melee_start_shooting = melee_module.StartShooting
        melee_module.StartShooting = LPH_JIT(function(item, p26, p27)
            local old_cooldown
            if state.fast_attack and item.Info then
                old_cooldown = item.Info.AttackCooldown
                item.Info.AttackCooldown = 0
            end
            local result = { state.original_melee_start_shooting(item, p26, p27) }
            if old_cooldown ~= nil and item.Info then
                item.Info.AttackCooldown = old_cooldown
            end
            return unpack(result)
        end)
    end

    self.state.hooked_melee = true
end

function Gunmods:hook_knife_module()
    if self.state.hooked_knife then return end
    local ok, knife_module = pcall(function()
        return require(local_player.PlayerScripts.Modules.Items.Knife)
    end)
    if not (ok and type(knife_module) == "table") then return end
    if type(knife_module.StartAiming) ~= "function" or self.state.original_knife_start_aiming then return end

    local owner = self
    self.state.original_knife_start_aiming = knife_module.StartAiming
    knife_module.StartAiming = LPH_JIT(function(item, ...)
        local result = { state.original_knife_start_aiming(item, ...) }
        local data = owner:make_backstab_data(item)
        if data and result[1] == true and result[2] == "StartAiming" then
            result[3] = data
        end
        return unpack(result)
    end)
    self.state.hooked_knife = true
end

function Gunmods:install_hooks()
    task.spawn(function() self:hook_gun_module() end)
    task.spawn(function() self:hook_melee_module() end)
    task.spawn(function() self:hook_knife_module() end)
end

function Gunmods:build()
    local tab = self.tab
    local ui = self.ui

    tab:add_toggle("rapid_fire", "Rapid Fire", self:desc("rapid_fire"))
    ui:on_change("rapid_fire.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.rapid_fire = val == true
        self:hook_gun_module()
    end)

    tab:add_toggle("no_spread", "No Spread", self:desc("no_spread"))
    ui:on_change("no_spread.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.no_spread = val == true
        self:hook_spread()
    end)

    tab:add_toggle("no_recoil", "No Recoil", self:desc("no_recoil"))
    ui:on_change("no_recoil.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.no_recoil = val == true
        self:hook_gun_module()
    end)

    tab:add_toggle("fast_attack", "Fast Attack", self:desc("fast_attack"))
    ui:on_change("fast_attack.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.fast_attack = val == true
        self:hook_melee_module()
    end)

    local always_backstab = tab:add_toggle("always_backstab", "Always Backstab", self:desc("always_backstab"))
    always_backstab:add_slider("range", "backstab range", 7, 18, "studs", 12)
    ui:on_change("always_backstab.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.always_backstab = val == true
        self:hook_knife_module()
    end)
    ui:on_change("always_backstab.range", function(val)
        self.state.backstab_range = math.clamp(tonumber(val) or 12, 7, 18)
    end)

    tab:add_toggle("instant_ads", "Instant ADS", self:desc("instant_ads"))
    ui:on_change("instant_ads.enabled", function(val)
        self.state.instant_ads = val == true
        self:hook_gun_module()
    end)
end

function Gunmods.cleanup()
    --// im too lazy to add a restore
end

return {
    Gunmods = Gunmods,
    state = state,
    install = function(ui, tab, lib)
        local gunmods = Gunmods.new(ui, tab, lib)
        dreamer.gunmods_instance = gunmods
        gunmods:install_hooks()
        gunmods:build()
        return gunmods
    end,
    cleanup = Gunmods.cleanup,
}

end)

-- ─── _esp_preview ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('esp_preview', function()
-- daydreamer Rivals — ESP visual editor (mobile only).
-- Returns { mount = function(ui) ... end }. Loaded as lib.esp_preview.

local UserInputService = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("UserInputService")) or game:GetService("UserInputService")

-- mobile token constants (mirrors modules/ui_mobile.lua)
local FONT_REGULAR = Font.new("rbxassetid://16658221428", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
local FONT_BOLD    = Font.new("rbxassetid://12187363368", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
local SURFACE      = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local TRACK        = Color3.fromRGB(62, 62, 62)
local KNOB         = Color3.fromRGB(236, 236, 236)
local TXT_PRIMARY  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local TXT_MUTED    = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)

local MIN_W = 300   -- min resize width
local MIN_H = 360   -- min resize height
local SIM_H = 158   -- height reserved for the bottom sim-controls panel; includes a
                    -- gap row below the weapon box so the resize grip sits clear of it
local FIT_HEADROOM = 5    -- studs above the head: room for the nametag bubble
local FIT_FOOTROOM = 1.5  -- studs below the feet: room for bottom text
local ZOOM_MIN, ZOOM_MAX = 0.5, 2.5
-- default geometry as ratios of the viewport (measured on 2560x1440) so the
-- editor keeps the same shape/placement on smaller screens
local DEFAULT_W_RATIO = 0.1652
local DEFAULT_H_RATIO = 0.4493
local DEFAULT_X_RATIO = 0.7219
local DEFAULT_Y_RATIO = 0.2111

local M = {}

-- small drag helper (ui_mobile's `draggable` is private; mirror its touch+mouse pattern)
local function make_draggable(ui, frame, handle)
    local dragging, drag_input, drag_start, start_pos
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            drag_start = input.Position
            start_pos = frame.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
    handle.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            drag_input = input
        end
    end)
    local conn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == drag_input and dragging then
            local d = input.Position - drag_start
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                start_pos.X.Scale, start_pos.X.Offset + d.X,
                start_pos.Y.Scale, start_pos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(conn) end
end

-- drag-to-resize via a UIScale: the window's controls use fixed offsets and
-- don't reflow, so we scale the whole window proportionally instead of free-
-- form W/H. Mirrors make_draggable's touch+mouse pattern. `span` (the caller's
-- window width+height) maps a full window-diagonal drag to roughly ±1.0 of scale.
local function make_resizable(ui, scale_obj, handle, min_s, max_s, span)
    local resizing, drag_input, start_pos, start_scale
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            resizing = true
            start_pos = input.Position
            start_scale = scale_obj.Scale
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then resizing = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
    handle.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            drag_input = input
        end
    end)
    local conn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == drag_input and resizing then
            local d = input.Position - start_pos
            scale_obj.Scale = math.clamp(start_scale + (d.X + d.Y) / span, min_s, max_s)
        end
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(conn) end
end

-- forward declares (assigned by later tasks, inside this same file, above build_window)
local build_viewport      -- function(ui, state) -> nil    (Task 4)
local wire_renderers      -- function(ui, state) -> nil    (Task 5)
local build_sim_controls  -- function(ui, state) -> nil    (Task 6)

local Players = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("Players")) or game:GetService("Players")

local function strip_for_viewport(model)
    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript")
            or d:IsA("Sound") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Trail")
            or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Highlight") then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
            d.Anchored = true
            d.CanCollide = false
            d.CanQuery = false
        end
    end
end

-- Build a synthetic R6 rig if the local character can't be cloned.
local function build_synthetic_rig()
    local model = Instance.new("Model")
    model.Name = "PreviewDummy"
    local function part(name, size, pos, color)
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name = name; p.Size = size; p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanQuery = false; p.Color = color; p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth; p.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
        p.Parent = model
        return p
    end
    local function marker(name, pos)
        local p = part(name, Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1), pos, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
        p.Transparency = 1
        p:SetAttribute("daydreamer_preview_skeleton_marker", true)
        return p
    end
    local skin = Color3.fromRGB(255, 204, 153)
    local shirt = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 120)
    part("Torso", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), shirt)
    part("Head", Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), Vector3.new(0, 4.6, 0), skin)
    part("Left Arm", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Vector3.new(-1.5, 3, 0), skin)
    part("Right Arm", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Vector3.new(1.5, 3, 0), skin)
    part("Left Leg", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Vector3.new(-0.5, 1, 0), shirt)
    part("Right Leg", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Vector3.new(0.5, 1, 0), shirt)
    local hrp = part("HumanoidRootPart", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), shirt)
    hrp.Transparency = 1

    marker("UpperTorso", Vector3.new(0, 3.55, 0))
    marker("LowerTorso", Vector3.new(0, 2.35, 0))
    marker("LeftUpperArm", Vector3.new(-1.05, 3.45, 0))
    marker("LeftLowerArm", Vector3.new(-1.5, 2.8, 0))
    marker("LeftHand", Vector3.new(-1.5, 2.1, 0))
    marker("RightUpperArm", Vector3.new(1.05, 3.45, 0))
    marker("RightLowerArm", Vector3.new(1.5, 2.8, 0))
    marker("RightHand", Vector3.new(1.5, 2.1, 0))
    marker("LeftUpperLeg", Vector3.new(-0.35, 1.85, 0))
    marker("LeftLowerLeg", Vector3.new(-0.35, 0.9, 0))
    marker("LeftFoot", Vector3.new(-0.35, 0.05, 0))
    marker("RightUpperLeg", Vector3.new(0.35, 1.85, 0))
    marker("RightLowerLeg", Vector3.new(0.35, 0.9, 0))
    marker("RightFoot", Vector3.new(0.35, 0.05, 0))

    local hum = Instance.new("Humanoid")
    hum.MaxHealth = 150; hum.Health = 150
    hum.Parent = model
    model.PrimaryPart = hrp
    return model
end

local function make_rig()
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    local src = lp and lp.Character
    if src then
        local old_archivable = src.Archivable
        pcall(function() src.Archivable = true end)
        local ok, clone = pcall(function() return src:Clone() end)
        pcall(function() src.Archivable = old_archivable end)
        if ok and clone then
            strip_for_viewport(clone)
            if not clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
                local hum = Instance.new("Humanoid")
                hum.MaxHealth = 150; hum.Health = 150
                hum.Parent = clone
            end
            local hrp = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then clone.PrimaryPart = hrp end
            return clone
        end
    end
    return build_synthetic_rig()
end

-- reads a chams state colour as either a Color3 or the _r/_g/_b slider triple
local function get_state_color(st, key, default)
    if typeof(st[key]) == "Color3" then return st[key] end
    if st[key .. "_r"] ~= nil or st[key .. "_g"] ~= nil or st[key .. "_b"] ~= nil then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.clamp(tonumber(st[key .. "_r"]) or default.R * 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(st[key .. "_g"]) or default.G * 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(st[key .. "_b"]) or default.B * 255, 0, 255)
        )
    end
    return default
end

-- chams preview: approximate the wall-hack highlight by tinting the rig parts.
-- No literal through-wall outline — colour + transparency are what's being tuned.
local function install_apply_chams(state)
    state.apply_chams = function(ui, st_root)
        local chams = ui.state and ui.state.chams
        local rig = st_root.rig
        if not rig then return end
        if not (chams and chams.enabled) then
            -- restore originals once
            if st_root._chams_on then
                for p, orig in pairs(st_root.rig_original) do
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        p.Color = orig.color
                        p.Transparency = orig.transparency
                    end
                end
                st_root._chams_on = false
            end
            return
        end
        st_root._chams_on = true
        local fill = get_state_color(chams, "fill_color", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
        local ftrans = (chams.fill_transparency or 50) / 100
        for p, orig in pairs(st_root.rig_original) do
            if p and p.Parent
                and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
                and orig.transparency < 1
                and not p:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Accessory")
                and not p:GetAttribute("daydreamer_preview_skeleton_marker") then
                p.Color = fill
                p.Transparency = ftrans
            end
        end
    end
end

-- Frame the rig in the viewport, leaving headroom above the head for the nametag
-- (it anchors a few studs above the head) and a little room below the feet. Applies
-- state.zoom so a manual zoom survives re-fits (e.g. window resize). Safe to call
-- repeatedly.
local function fit_camera(state)
    local rig = state.rig
    local cam = state.vp_cam
    local overlay = state.overlay
    if not (rig and cam and overlay) then return end
    local as = overlay.AbsoluteSize
    if as.X <= 0 or as.Y <= 0 then return end
    local ok, center, size = pcall(function()
        local cf, sz = rig:GetBoundingBox()
        return cf.Position, sz
    end)
    if not ok or not center or not size then return end
    local t = math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView) / 2)
    if t <= 0 then return end
    -- grow the framed region for the nametag (top) + bottom text, and shift the
    -- centre up so the extra room lands above the head.
    local framed_h = size.Y + FIT_HEADROOM + FIT_FOOTROOM
    -- The up-shift is a fixed world offset, so zoom magnifies it: zoomed in it
    -- shoves the rig down out of frame and leaves the top half of the window empty
    -- (the magnified nametag headroom). Ease the shift toward the body centre as
    -- the user zooms in so the zoom homes in on the rig, not the empty space above
    -- it. At zoom=1 the full headroom is kept for the nametag bubble.
    local zoom = state.zoom or 1
    local framed_center = center + Vector3.new(0, ((FIT_HEADROOM - FIT_FOOTROOM) / 2) / zoom, 0)
    local aspect = as.X / as.Y
    local dist_y = (framed_h / 2) / t
    local dist_x = (size.X / 2) / (t * aspect)
    local dist = (math.max(dist_y, dist_x) * 1.1 + size.Z / 2) / (state.zoom or 1)
    cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(framed_center + Vector3.new(0, 0, dist), framed_center)
end

build_viewport = function(ui, state)
    local vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
    vp.Name = "preview_viewport"
    vp.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 8)
    vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -(SIM_H + 24))
    vp.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16)
    vp.BorderSizePixel = 0
    vp.ZIndex = 52
    vp.Parent = state.content
    Instance.new("UICorner", vp).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    state.viewport = vp

    local rig = make_rig()
    -- face the camera and sit at a known pivot
    pcall(function() rig:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0)) end)
    rig.Parent = vp
    state.rig = rig

    -- cache original part appearance so chams preview can restore it
    state.rig_original = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            state.rig_original[p] = { color = p.Color, transparency = p.Transparency }
        end
    end

    local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
    cam.FieldOfView = 50
    cam.Parent = vp
    vp.CurrentCamera = cam
    state.vp_cam = cam

    -- overlay holder: sits exactly over the viewport, clips ESP frames to the window
    local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
    overlay.Name = "preview_overlay"
    overlay.Position = vp.Position
    overlay.Size = vp.Size
    overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    overlay.ClipsDescendants = true
    overlay.ZIndex = 54
    overlay.Parent = state.content
    state.overlay = overlay

    -- the overlay lives under the window's resize UIScale, so its AbsoluteSize
    -- already includes that scale. ESP frames are positioned as plain offsets
    -- INSIDE the overlay, which the same UIScale scales a second time — so we
    -- divide the scale back out here to get the overlay's local (unscaled) size.
    -- Without this the box/skeleton drift away from the rig as the window resizes.
    local function local_size()
        local as = overlay.AbsoluteSize
        local k = (state.scale and state.scale.Scale) or 1
        if k <= 0 then k = 1 end
        return as.X / k, as.Y / k
    end
    state.local_size = local_size

    -- holder-local projection robust to ViewportSize reporting differences:
    -- normalize by the camera's reported viewport, then scale to the overlay's
    -- local pixels (see local_size above).
    state.project = function(world_pos)
        local sp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world_pos)
        local vs = cam.ViewportSize
        local nx = (vs.X > 0) and (sp.X / vs.X) or 0
        local ny = (vs.Y > 0) and (sp.Y / vs.Y) or 0
        local lx, ly = local_size()
        return nx * lx, ny * ly, sp.Z > 0, sp.Z
    end

    -- fit the rig now, and re-fit whenever the viewport changes size (window resize)
    fit_camera(state)
    local fit_conn = vp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
        fit_camera(state)
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(fit_conn) end
    task.defer(fit_camera, state)

    -- zoom: no bar. A transparent catcher over the viewport takes mouse-wheel (PC)
    -- and gates two-finger pinch (mobile) so we only zoom when input is on the
    -- preview. state.zoom feeds fit_camera, so it persists across re-fits.
    state.zoom = 1
    local zoom_catcher = Instance.new("TextButton")
    zoom_catcher.Name = "zoom_catcher"
    zoom_catcher.Position = vp.Position
    zoom_catcher.Size = vp.Size
    zoom_catcher.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    zoom_catcher.Text = ""
    zoom_catcher.AutoButtonColor = false
    zoom_catcher.ZIndex = 58
    zoom_catcher.Parent = state.content

    local function apply_zoom(mult)
        state.zoom = math.clamp((state.zoom or 1) * mult, ZOOM_MIN, ZOOM_MAX)
        fit_camera(state)
    end

    -- PC: mouse wheel while hovering the preview
    local wf = zoom_catcher.MouseWheelForward:Connect(function() apply_zoom(1.1) end)
    local wb = zoom_catcher.MouseWheelBackward:Connect(function() apply_zoom(1 / 1.1) end)

    -- mobile: two-finger pinch over the preview. count touches that begin/end on
    -- the catcher so a pinch elsewhere (e.g. the game camera) is ignored.
    local touches_over = 0
    local tb = zoom_catcher.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then touches_over += 1 end
    end)
    local te = zoom_catcher.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            touches_over = math.max(0, touches_over - 1)
        end
    end)
    local pinch_start
    local pc = UserInputService.TouchPinch:Connect(function(_positions, scale, _velocity, st)
        if st == Enum.UserInputState.End or st == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
            pinch_start = nil
            return
        end
        if touches_over < 2 then return end
        -- lazy-init the baseline so a missed Begin (touches not yet counted) can't
        -- drop the gesture; first applied zoom equals the current zoom (no jump).
        if not pinch_start then pinch_start = (state.zoom or 1) / math.max(scale, 0.001) end
        state.zoom = math.clamp(pinch_start * scale, ZOOM_MIN, ZOOM_MAX)
        fit_camera(state)
    end)

    if ui.track_connection then
        ui:track_connection(wf)
        ui:track_connection(wb)
        ui:track_connection(tb)
        ui:track_connection(te)
        ui:track_connection(pc)
    end

    install_apply_chams(state)
end

local RunService = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("RunService")) or game:GetService("RunService")

wire_renderers = function(ui, state)
    local renderers = ui.rivals_esp_renderers or {}

    -- preview ctx (sim fields refreshed each frame by Task 6; defaults here keep it usable now)
    local function vp_local_size()
        local lx, ly = state.local_size()
        return Vector2.new(lx, ly)
    end

    local ctx = {
        holder = state.overlay,
        is_preview = true,
        viewport_size = vp_local_size(),
        distance_override = 35,
        weapon_override = "",
        camera_position = state.vp_cam.CFrame.Position,
        project = state.project,
    }
    state.ctx = ctx

    -- one synthetic target per registered renderer, all pointing at the same rig
    local rig = state.rig
    local hrp = rig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = rig:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    local head = rig:FindFirstChild("Head")

    local targets = {}
    for _, r in ipairs(renderers) do
        local ok, target = pcall(r.create, ctx)
        if ok and target then
            target.CharacterObjects = {
                Character = rig,
                HumanoidRootPart = hrp,
                Humanoid = hum,
                Head = head,
            }
            targets[#targets + 1] = { def = r, target = target }
        else
            warn("{*} esp_preview renderer create failed: " .. tostring(target))
        end
    end
    state.targets = targets

    -- per-frame: only while the window is open
    local conn = RunService.PreRender:Connect(function(dt)
        if not state.open then return end
        ctx.viewport_size = vp_local_size()
        if state.refresh_sim then state.refresh_sim(ctx, dt) end -- Task 6
        if state.apply_chams then state.apply_chams(ui, state) end -- Task 7
        for _, t in ipairs(targets) do
            local ok, err = pcall(t.def.update, nil, t.target, ctx, dt)
            if not ok then warn("[ESP Preview] update failed: ", err) end
        end
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(conn) end
end

-- minimal themed slider: calls on_change(value) on drag. Returns nothing.
local function make_slider(ui, parent, y, label_text, min_v, max_v, default_v, suffix, on_change)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, y)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.ZIndex = 55
    row.Parent = parent

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    lbl.Text = label_text
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextSize = 14
    lbl.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    lbl.ZIndex = 56
    lbl.Parent = row

    local val = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    val.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    val.Text = tostring(default_v) .. (suffix or "")
    val.TextColor3 = TXT_MUTED
    val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    val.TextSize = 14
    val.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    val.ZIndex = 56
    val.Parent = row

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24)
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 4)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = TRACK
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.ZIndex = 56
    bar.Parent = row
    Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale((default_v - min_v) / (max_v - min_v), 1)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = TXT_PRIMARY
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.ZIndex = 57
    fill.Parent = bar
    Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    if ui.create_gradient then ui:create_gradient(fill, nil, 0, true, false) end

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.new((default_v - min_v) / (max_v - min_v), 0, 0.5, 0)
    knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = KNOB
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 58
    knob.Parent = bar
    Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local dragging = false
    local function set_from_x(abs_x)
        local rel = math.clamp((abs_x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        local v = min_v + rel * (max_v - min_v)
        fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(rel, 1)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 0.5, 0)
        val.Text = tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5)) .. (suffix or "")
        if on_change then on_change(v) end
    end
    local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
    hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1; hit.Text = ""
    hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
    hit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 10)
    hit.ZIndex = 59
    hit.Parent = row
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true; set_from_x(i.Position.X)
        end
    end)
    hit.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    local conn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            set_from_x(i.Position.X)
        end
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(conn) end
end

build_sim_controls = function(ui, state)
    local sim = { health = 1.0, distance = 35, weapon = "" }
    state.sim = sim

    -- bottom-anchored controls panel: stays put while the viewport scales
    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.Name = "sim_controls"
    panel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -SIM_H)
    panel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SIM_H)
    panel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    panel.ZIndex = 55
    panel.Parent = state.content

    make_slider(ui, panel, 4, "health", 0, 100, 100, "%", function(v)
        sim.health = math.clamp(v / 100, 0, 1)
    end)
    make_slider(ui, panel, 40, "distance", 1, 300, 35, "m", function(v)
        sim.distance = v
    end)

    -- weapon textbox
    local wrow = Instance.new("Frame")
    wrow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 78)
    wrow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 40)
    wrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    wrow.ZIndex = 55
    wrow.Parent = panel

    local wlbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    wlbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    wlbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15)
    wlbl.Text = "weapon"
    wlbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    wlbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    wlbl.TextSize = 14
    wlbl.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    wlbl.ZIndex = 56
    wlbl.Parent = wrow

    local wbg = Instance.new("Frame")
    wbg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18)
    wbg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
    wbg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    wbg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    wbg.ZIndex = 56
    wbg.Parent = wrow
    Instance.new("UICorner", wbg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local wbox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    wbox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    wbox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0)
    wbox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0)
    wbox.Text = ""
    wbox.PlaceholderText = "e.g. AK-47 (blank = none)"
    wbox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)
    wbox.TextColor3 = TXT_PRIMARY
    wbox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    wbox.TextSize = 13
    wbox.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    wbox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    wbox.ZIndex = 57
    wbox.Parent = wbg
    local wconn = wbox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        sim.weapon = wbox.Text
    end)
    if ui.track_connection then ui:track_connection(wconn) end

    -- per-frame refresh consumed by wire_renderers' loop
    state.refresh_sim = function(ctx, dt)
        ctx.distance_override = sim.distance
        ctx.weapon_override = sim.weapon
        local hum = state.rig and state.rig:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.MaxHealth = 150
            hum.Health = math.clamp(sim.health * 150, 0, 150)
        end
    end
end

local function build_window(ui)
    local state = { open = false }

    -- default geometry as ratios of the screen (tuned on 2560x1440), so smaller
    -- screens get the same shape/placement; clamped to a usable minimum and to
    -- stay fully on-screen.
    local cam_vps = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
    if cam_vps.X < 1 or cam_vps.Y < 1 then cam_vps = Vector2.new(1280, 720) end
    local win_w = math.clamp(cam_vps.X * DEFAULT_W_RATIO, MIN_W, cam_vps.X)
    local win_h = math.clamp(cam_vps.Y * DEFAULT_H_RATIO, MIN_H, cam_vps.Y)
    local win_x = math.clamp(cam_vps.X * DEFAULT_X_RATIO, 0, cam_vps.X - win_w)
    local win_y = math.clamp(cam_vps.Y * DEFAULT_Y_RATIO, 0, cam_vps.Y - win_h)

    local root = Instance.new("Frame")
    root.Name = "esp_editor"
    root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(win_w, win_h)
    root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(win_x, win_y)
    root.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
    root.BorderSizePixel = 0
    root.Visible = false
    root.ZIndex = 50
    root.Parent = ui.gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", root).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 25)
    state.root = root

    -- anchor (0,0) keeps the top-left pinned so the window grows toward the
    -- bottom-right resize grip as the scale increases.
    local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Scale = 1
    scale.Parent = root
    state.scale = scale

    -- title bar (drag handle) with themed accent underline
    local title_bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    title_bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    title_bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title_bar.ZIndex = 51
    title_bar.Parent = root

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 8)
    title.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 22)
    title.Text = "visual editor"
    title.TextColor3 = TXT_PRIMARY
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.TextSize = 18
    title.FontFace = FONT_BOLD
    title.ZIndex = 52
    title.Parent = title_bar

    local accent = Instance.new("Frame")
    accent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 34)
    accent.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 2)
    accent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    accent.BackgroundColor3 = TXT_PRIMARY
    accent.ZIndex = 52
    accent.Parent = title_bar
    Instance.new("UICorner", accent).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    -- ride the live wave/rainbow theme
    if ui.create_gradient then ui:create_gradient(accent, nil, 0, true, false) end

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton")
    close.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 8)
    close.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
    close.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    close.Text = "x"
    close.TextColor3 = TXT_MUTED
    close.TextSize = 18
    close.FontFace = FONT_BOLD
    close.ZIndex = 53
    close.Parent = title_bar
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        state.open = false
        root.Visible = false
    end)

    make_draggable(ui, root, title_bar)

    -- content area below the title bar (viewport + sim controls go here)
    local content = Instance.new("Frame")
    content.Name = "content"
    content.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 40)
    content.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -40)
    content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    content.ZIndex = 51
    content.Parent = root
    state.content = content

    if build_viewport then build_viewport(ui, state) end
    if wire_renderers then wire_renderers(ui, state) end
    if build_sim_controls then build_sim_controls(ui, state) end

    -- bottom-right resize grip: an L-shaped corner bracket (two perpendicular
    -- lines meeting at a right angle) that mirrors the ESP corner look and
    -- signals the window is draggable-to-resize.
    local GRIP_ARM, GRIP_THICK = 16, 2
    local grip = Instance.new("TextButton")
    grip.Name = "resize_grip"
    grip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    grip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12)
    grip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(GRIP_ARM + 8, GRIP_ARM + 8)
    grip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    grip.AutoButtonColor = false
    grip.Text = ""
    grip.ZIndex = 60
    grip.Parent = root

    local function grip_arm(w, h)
        local arm = Instance.new("Frame")
        arm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        arm.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        arm.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
        arm.BackgroundColor3 = TXT_PRIMARY
        arm.BorderSizePixel = 0
        arm.ZIndex = 61
        arm.Parent = grip
        Instance.new("UICorner", arm).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        -- ride the live wave/rainbow theme like the title accent
        if ui.create_gradient then ui:create_gradient(arm, nil, 0, true, false) end
        return arm
    end
    grip_arm(GRIP_ARM, GRIP_THICK) -- horizontal arm along the bottom edge
    grip_arm(GRIP_THICK, GRIP_ARM) -- vertical arm along the right edge

    make_resizable(ui, scale, grip, 0.75, 1.8, win_w + win_h)

    return state
end

function M.mount(ui)
    -- mobile only: create_gradient is the mobile library's tell
    if type(ui.create_gradient) ~= "function" then return end

    local tab = ui:add_tab("render")
    local card = tab:add_toggle("esp_editor", "visual editor", "live preview of your ESP")
    if card.set_toggleable then card:set_toggleable(false) end
    card:add_button("open", "open", "open editor")

    -- build and auto-open immediately, docked to the right
    local window_state = build_window(ui)
    window_state.open = true

    -- the editor is only visible when the user has it open, the main daydreamer
    -- UI is open, AND the render tab (this `tab`) is the active tab.
    local function sync_visibility()
        local on_render = ui.active_tab == tab
        window_state.root.Visible = window_state.open and (ui.ui_visible ~= false) and on_render
    end
    sync_visibility()

    -- the card's "open" button toggles the editor; the title-bar X sets open=false.
    ui:on_change("esp_editor.open", function()
        window_state.open = not window_state.open
        sync_visibility()
    end)

    -- follow the main UI's open/close (RightShift / FAB both route through
    -- toggle_visibility, the only place ui_visible changes): hide the editor with
    -- the UI, and bring it back only if the user still has it open.
    if not ui._esp_editor_toggle_hooked then
        ui._esp_editor_toggle_hooked = true
        local orig_toggle = ui.toggle_visibility
        ui.toggle_visibility = function(self, ...)
            local result = orig_toggle(self, ...)
            pcall(sync_visibility)
            return result
        end
    end

    -- follow tab switches (select_tab is the single place active_tab changes):
    -- the editor only shows while the render tab is selected.
    if not ui._esp_editor_tab_hooked then
        ui._esp_editor_tab_hooked = true
        local orig_select = ui.select_tab
        ui.select_tab = function(self, ...)
            local result = orig_select(self, ...)
            pcall(sync_visibility)
            return result
        end
    end
end

return M

end)

-- ─── tab: combat ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_combat', function()
--[[
todo:

- flickbot
- aim assist jitter smoothing
- anti edge tracking

]]
local players = game:GetService("Players")
local run_service = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local user_input_service = cloneref(game:GetService("UserInputService"))
local replicated_storage = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local sound_service = cloneref(game:GetService("SoundService"))
local player_scripts = players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
local local_player = players.LocalPlayer
local mechanics_controller = require(player_scripts.Controllers.MechanicsController)

if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local no_virtualize = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local jit_max = "LPH_" .. "JIT_MAX"
    env[no_virtualize] = env[no_virtualize] or function(fn) return fn end
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(fn) return fn end
    env[jit_max] = env[jit_max] or function(fn) return fn end
end

-- max seconds of velocity-based lead applied at 100% prediction strength
local PREDICT_MAX_LEAD = 0.18
local AIM_OVERSHOOT_DURATION = 0.12
local DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 800
local MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 250
local MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 2000
local TRIGGER_MISS_OFFSET = 8
local HIT_SOUND_DIR = "daydreamer/rivals/assets"
local AIM_ASSIST_BIND = "RivalsAimAssistUpdate"

local los_params = RaycastParams.new()
los_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local los_filter = {nil}
local trigger_ray_params = RaycastParams.new()
trigger_ray_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local trigger_filter = {nil}

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.set_visible(opt, visible)
    if opt and opt.set_visible then opt:set_visible(visible == true) end
end

--// stateless helpers

local function is_katana_deflecting(target_model)
    local target_player = target_model and players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target_model)
    local users = env.dreamerkatana
    return target_player ~= nil and type(users) == "table" and users[target_player] == true
end

-- check if they are a valid enemy
local is_valid_enemy = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(target_model)
    local target_player = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target_model)
    if not target_player then return false end
    if target_player.Team ~= nil and target_player.Team == local_player.Team then
        return false
    end
    if target_player:GetAttribute("TeamID") ~= nil and target_player:GetAttribute("TeamID") == local_player:GetAttribute("TeamID") then
        return false
    end
    local target_humanoid = target_model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not target_humanoid or target_humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    return true
end)

local has_line_of_sight = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(part)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = local_player.Character
    if not cam or not part then return false end
    los_filter[1] = character or workspace
    los_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = los_filter
    local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
    local direction = part.Position - origin
    local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, los_params)
    return not result or result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(part.Parent)
end)

local function get_aspect_stretch()
    local settings = _G.AspectRatioSettings
    if settings and settings.Enabled then
        return settings.X / settings.Y
    end
    return 1
end

local function apply_aspect_to_viewport(screen_vec, cam)
    local stretch = get_aspect_stretch()
    if stretch == 1 then
        return screen_vec
    end
    cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return screen_vec
    end
    local center_y = cam.ViewportSize.Y * 0.5
    return Vector3.new(screen_vec.X, center_y + (screen_vec.Y - center_y) * stretch, screen_vec.Z)
end

local world_to_screen = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(world_pos, cam)
    cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam or not world_pos then
        return nil, false
    end
    local screen_vec, on_screen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world_pos)
    if not on_screen or screen_vec.Z <= 0 then
        return screen_vec, false
    end
    return apply_aspect_to_viewport(screen_vec, cam), true
end)

local function screen_anchor(x_norm, y_norm, cam)
    cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return Vector2.zero
    end
    local viewport_size = cam.ViewportSize
    local stretch = get_aspect_stretch()
    local center_y = viewport_size.Y * 0.5
    local y = center_y + (viewport_size.Y * y_norm - center_y) * stretch
    return Vector2.new(viewport_size.X * x_norm, y)
end

local function screen_center(cam)
    return screen_anchor(0.5, 0.5, cam)
end

local function make_drawing_circle()
    if type(Drawing) ~= "table" or type(Drawing.new) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, circle = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new("Circle")
    end)
    if not ok or not circle then
        return nil
    end
    circle.Visible = false
    circle.Filled = false
    circle.NumSides = 96
    circle.Thickness = 1.5
    circle.Transparency = 1
    circle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    circle.Radius = 100
    return circle
end

local function make_drawing_square()
    if type(Drawing) ~= "table" or type(Drawing.new) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local ok, square = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Square") end)
    if not ok or not square then return nil end
    square.Visible = false
    square.Filled = false
    square.Thickness = 1.5
    square.Transparency = 1
    square.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    return square
end

local function remove_drawing(obj)
    if not obj then return end
    pcall(function()
        obj.Visible = false
        obj:Remove()
    end)
end

local function muzzle_pos()
    local view_models = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not view_models then return nil end
    local first_person = view_models:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    if not first_person then return nil end
    local player_name = local_player.Name
    for _, model in pairs(first_person:GetChildren()) do
        if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name:find("^" .. player_name) then
            local item_visual = model:FindFirstChild("ItemVisual")
            if item_visual then
                local body = item_visual:FindFirstChild("Body")
                if body then
                    local body_primary = body:FindFirstChild("BodyPrimary")
                    if body_primary then
                        local muzzle = body_primary:FindFirstChild("_muzzle")
                        if muzzle and muzzle:IsA("Attachment") then
                            return muzzle.WorldPosition
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function screen_pos(world_pos)
    if not world_pos then return nil end
    local screen_vec, on_screen = world_to_screen(world_pos, workspace.CurrentCamera)
    if not on_screen then return nil end
    return Vector2.new(screen_vec.X, screen_vec.Y)
end

local function fov_center(follow_muzzle)
    if follow_muzzle then
        local muzzle_screen = screen_pos(muzzle_pos())
        if muzzle_screen then return muzzle_screen end
    end
    return screen_center(workspace.CurrentCamera)
end

-- map a hit part name to a real basepart on a character
local function hit_part_from_name(character, part_name, head_hit_chance)
    if not character then return nil end
    local fc = function(n) return character:FindFirstChild(n) end
    if (part_name == "Closest" or part_name == "Random") and math.random(1, 100) <= math.clamp(tonumber(head_hit_chance) or 0, 0, 100) then
        return fc("Head") or fc("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    if     part_name == "Head"             then return fc("Head")
    elseif part_name == "HumanoidRootPart" then return fc("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif part_name == "Torso"            then return fc("Torso") or fc("UpperTorso")
    elseif part_name == "UpperTorso"       then return fc("UpperTorso")
    elseif part_name == "LowerTorso"       then return fc("LowerTorso")
    elseif part_name == "Closest" then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return fc("HumanoidRootPart") end
        local cam_pos, cam_look = cam.CFrame.Position, cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local best, best_d = nil, math.huge
        for _, part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local d = 1 - cam_look:Dot((part.Position - cam_pos).Unit)
                if d < best_d then best_d = d; best = part end
            end
        end
        return best or fc("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif part_name == "Random" then
        local list = {}
        for _, part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(list, part) end
        end
        if #list > 0 then return list[math.random(1, #list)] end
    end
    return fc("HumanoidRootPart")
end

--// aim assist lerp stuff
local function get_aim_lerp_alpha(smoothness, curve, dt)
    smoothness = math.clamp(tonumber(smoothness) or 2, 0.1, 10)
    curve = curve or "Linear"
    local speed = 6 / smoothness
    if curve == "Instant" or curve == "Snap" then
        return 1
    elseif curve == "Expo" then
        return 1 - math.exp(-(4 / smoothness) * dt)
    elseif curve == "EaseIn" then
        local t = math.clamp(speed * dt, 0, 1)
        return t * t
    elseif curve == "EaseOut" then
        local t = math.clamp(speed * dt, 0, 1)
        return 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t)
    elseif curve == "EaseInOut" then
        local t = math.clamp(speed * dt, 0, 1)
        if t < 0.5 then
            return 2 * t * t
        end
        return 1 - ((-2 * t + 2) ^ 2) / 2
    elseif curve == "Cubic" then
        local t = math.clamp(speed * dt, 0, 1)
        return t * t * t
    end
    return math.clamp(speed * dt, 0, 1)
end

--// sample a target's position from an os.clock timestamp using a buffer of {t, pos} samples sorted oldest to newest
local function sample_delayed_position(history, target_time, fallback_pos)
    local n = #history
    if n == 0 then return fallback_pos end
    if target_time <= history[1].t then return history[1].pos end
    if target_time >= history[n].t then return history[n].pos end
    for i = n - 1, 1, -1 do
        local a = history[i]
        local b = history[i + 1]
        if target_time >= a.t and target_time <= b.t then
            local span = b.t - a.t
            local alpha = span > 0 and (target_time - a.t) / span or 0
            return a.pos:Lerp(b.pos, alpha)
        end
    end
    return fallback_pos
end

local function get_fov_color(st, default_color)
    if typeof(st.fov_color) == "Color3" then
        return st.fov_color
    end
    if st.fov_color_r ~= nil or st.fov_color_g ~= nil or st.fov_color_b ~= nil then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.clamp(tonumber(st.fov_color_r) or 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(st.fov_color_g) or 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(st.fov_color_b) or 255, 0, 255)
        )
    end
    return default_color
end

--// prefer real colourwell, else fallback sliders wrapped in a set_visible handle
local function add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if type(section.add_color_picker) == "function" then
        return section:add_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    if type(section.add_color) == "function" then
        return section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    default_color = typeof(default_color) == "Color3" and default_color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local opts = {
        section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_r", text .. " r", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.R * 255 + 0.5)),
        section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_g", text .. " g", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.G * 255 + 0.5)),
        section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_b", text .. " b", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.B * 255 + 0.5)),
    }
    local handle = {}
    function handle:set_visible(visible)
        for _, opt in ipairs(opts) do
            if opt and opt.set_visible then opt:set_visible(visible) end
        end
        return self
    end
    return handle
end

--// silent aim statics
local silent_ray_params = RaycastParams.new()
local silent_offsets = {
    Vector3.new(0, 12, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 16, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 20, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 24, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 28, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 32, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 36, 0),
    Vector3.new(0, 40, 0),
}

local calculate_silent_point = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(origin, target_pos, target_char)
    silent_ray_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { local_player.Character or workspace, target_char }
    silent_ray_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

    if not workspace:Raycast(origin, target_pos - origin, silent_ray_params) then
        return origin
    end

    for _, offset in ipairs(silent_offsets) do
        local scan_pos = origin + offset
        if not workspace:Raycast(scan_pos, target_pos - scan_pos, silent_ray_params) then
            return scan_pos
        end
    end

    return nil
end)

local get_silent_target_part = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(st)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil, nil end
    local center = fov_center(false)
    local radius = st.fov or st.fov_radius or 100
    local part_name = st.hit_part or "Head"
    local best_part, best_char, best_dist = nil, nil, math.huge

    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= local_player and plr.Character and is_valid_enemy(plr.Character)
            and (st.katana_check ~= true or not is_katana_deflecting(plr.Character)) then
            local char = plr.Character
            local part = hit_part_from_name(char, part_name, st.head_hit_chance)
            if part and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                local passes_visible_check = (not st.visible_check) or has_line_of_sight(part)
                if passes_visible_check then
                    local scr, on = world_to_screen(part.Position, cam)
                    if on then
                        local dx = scr.X - center.X
                        local dy = scr.Y - center.Y
                        local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                        if dist <= radius and dist < best_dist then
                            best_dist = dist
                            best_part = part
                            best_char = char
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return best_part, best_char
end)

local build_silent_raycast_result = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(from, range, st)
    local target_part, target_char = get_silent_target_part(st)
    if not target_part or not target_char then return end
    local origin = from

    if st.manipulation ~= false then
        origin = calculate_silent_point(from, target_part.Position, target_char)
        if not origin then return end
    end

    local delta = target_part.Position - origin
    local dist = delta.Magnitude
    if dist <= 0 then return end
    local dir = delta.Unit
    local hit_pos = target_part.Position

    if type(range) == "number" and dist > range then
        dist = range
        hit_pos = origin + dir * range
    end

    return {
        Position = hit_pos,
        Distance = dist,
        Instance = target_part,
        Material = target_part.Material,
        Normal = -dir,
    }
end)

local silent_item_denylist = {
    Flamethrower = true,
    Grenade = true,
    Medkit = true,
    Molotov = true,
    SmokeGrenade = true,
    Flashbang = true,
    Warper = true,
    Warpstone = true,
    Grappler = true,
    Satchel = true,
}

local function get_equipped_item()
    local local_fighter = mechanics_controller.LocalFighter
    return local_fighter and local_fighter.EquippedItem or nil
end

local function get_item_field(item, key)
    if not item then return nil end
    if item[key] ~= nil then return item[key] end
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        return item:Get(key)
    end)
    if ok then return value end
    return nil
end

local function silent_item_allowed(item)
    local name = tostring(get_item_field(item, "Name") or item.Name or "")
    if silent_item_denylist[name] then return false end
    local info = get_item_field(item, "Info")
    if type(info) == "table" then
        local item_type = tostring(info.ItemType or info.Type or info.Class or "")
        if item_type == "Melee" or item_type == "Throwable" or item_type == "Consumable" then
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end

local function should_silent_raycast(range)
    local item = get_equipped_item()
    if not item or not silent_item_allowed(item) then return false end
    if type(range) ~= "number" then return false end
    return range >= 50
end

local function is_within_aim_viewport(part, cam, st)
    if not part or not cam then return false end
    local part_screen_pos, on_screen = world_to_screen(part.Position, cam)
    if not on_screen then return false end

    local margin = math.clamp(tonumber(st.edge_margin) or 25, 0, 200)
    local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
    return part_screen_pos.X >= margin and part_screen_pos.X <= viewport.X - margin
        and part_screen_pos.Y >= margin and part_screen_pos.Y <= viewport.Y - margin
end

--// combat class defined here

local Combat = {}
Combat.__index = Combat

function Combat.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        --//hit sounds
        hit_assets = {},
        hit_sounds = {},
        --//fov drawings
        fov_circles = {},
        fov_draw_conn = nil,
        edge_margin_box = nil,
        --//triggerbot
        triggerbot_sec = nil,
        next_shoot_time = 0,
        trigger_miss_target = nil,
        trigger_miss_position = nil,
        trigger_miss_side = 0,
        trigger_miss_until = 0,
        --//silent aim
        silent_sec = nil,
        silent_utility = nil,
        silent_modules_warned = false,
        silent_raycast_hooked = false,
        --//bullet tracers
        tracer_entries = {},
        tracer_conn = nil,
        tracer_effect = nil,
        original_tracer_play = nil,
        tracer_play_wrapper = nil,
        --//aim assist
        aim_sec = nil,
        aim_locked_target = nil,
        aim_smooth_cf = nil,
        aim_last_cam_cf = nil,
        aim_overshoot_offset = nil,
        aim_overshoot_until = 0,
        cam_controller = nil,
        aim_target_history = {},
        aim_next_switch_time = 0, -- os.clock
        aim_assist_using_bind = false,
        aim_assist_conn = nil,
    }, Combat)
end

function Combat:desc(id)
    local descriptions = self.ui and self.ui.descriptions or {}
    return descriptions[id]
end

function Combat:set_option_visible(option, visible)
    self.helpers.set_visible(option, visible)
end

--// hit sounds

function Combat:ensure_hit_sound_dir()
    if type(makefolder) ~= "function" then return end
    if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder("daydreamer") then pcall(makefolder, "daydreamer") end
    if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder("daydreamer/rivals") then pcall(makefolder, "daydreamer/rivals") end
    if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(HIT_SOUND_DIR) then pcall(makefolder, HIT_SOUND_DIR) end
end

function Combat:list_hit_sound_files()
    local files = {}
    if type(listfiles) ~= "function" or type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(HIT_SOUND_DIR) then
        return files
    end
    local ok, entries = pcall(listfiles, HIT_SOUND_DIR)
    if not ok or type(entries) ~= "table" then return files end
    for _, path in ipairs(entries) do
        local filename = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)$")
        if filename and filename:lower():match("%.(mp3|ogg|wav)$") then
            files[#files + 1] = filename
        end
    end
    table.sort(files, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
    return files
end

function Combat:get_hit_sound_asset(filename, refresh)
    if type(filename) ~= "string" or filename == "" then return nil, "enter a sound filename" end
    local rbx_id = filename:match("^%s*rbxassetid://(%d+)%s*$")
    if rbx_id then return "rbxassetid://" .. rbx_id end
    local name = filename:match("([^/\\]+)$")
    if not name or not (name:lower():match("%.mp3$") or name:lower():match("%.ogg$") or name:lower():match("%.wav$")) then
        return nil, "use an .mp3, .ogg, or .wav file"
    end
    local path = HIT_SOUND_DIR .. "/" .. name
    if refresh then self.hit_assets[path] = nil end
    if self.hit_assets[path] then return self.hit_assets[path] end
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(path) then return nil, name .. " was not found" end

    local loader = getcustomasset or env.getcustomasset or getsynasset or env.getsynasset
        or getcustomassetasync or env.getcustomassetasync
    if type(loader) ~= "function" then return nil, "your executor does not support custom assets" end
    local ok, asset = pcall(loader, path)
    if not ok or type(asset) ~= "string" or asset == "" then return nil, "failed to load " .. name end
    self.hit_assets[path] = asset
    return asset
end

function Combat:selected_sound(state, event)
    return state and state[event .. "_file"]
end

function Combat:play_sound_event(event, refresh, force)
    local state = self.ui.state.hit_sounds
    if not state or (state.enabled ~= true and not force) then return end
    local asset, err = self:get_hit_sound_asset(self:selected_sound(state, event), refresh)
    if not asset then
        self.ui:notify({ title = "Hit sounds", text = err, duration = 4 })
        return false
    end
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Name = "DaydreamerHitSound"
    sound.SoundId = asset
    sound.Volume = math.clamp((tonumber(state.volume) or 70) / 100, 0, 1.5)
    sound.PlaybackSpeed = math.clamp((tonumber(state.pitch) or 100) / 100, 0.5, 2)
    sound.Parent = sound_service
    self.hit_sounds[sound] = true
    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        self.hit_sounds[sound] = nil
        sound:Destroy()
    end)
    sound:Play()
    task.delay(10, function()
        if sound.Parent then
            self.hit_sounds[sound] = nil
            sound:Destroy()
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function Combat:should_replace_sound(event)
    local state = self.ui.state.hit_sounds
    if not state or state.enabled ~= true then return false end
    return self:play_sound_event(event, false, false) == true
end

function Combat:install_sound_hooks()
    local item_module = player_scripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem
    local viewmodel_module = item_module and item_module:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
    local ok, viewmodel = pcall(require, viewmodel_module)
    local item_ok, client_item = pcall(require, item_module)
    if not ok or not item_ok or type(viewmodel) ~= "table" or type(client_item) ~= "table"
        or type(viewmodel.PlayHitmarkerSound) ~= "function" or type(client_item.CreateSound) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local originals = {
        hitmarker = viewmodel.PlayHitmarkerSound,
        equip = viewmodel.Equip,
        animation = viewmodel.PlayAnimation,
        create_sound = client_item.CreateSound,
    }
    local runtime = { event = nil, reload_pending_until = 0 }
    local combat = self

    local hitmarker_wrapper = function(self_obj, ...)
        if combat:should_replace_sound("hit") then return end
        runtime.event = "hit"
        local result = originals.hitmarker(self_obj, ...)
        runtime.event = nil
        return result
    end
    local equip_wrapper = function(self_obj, ...)
        runtime.event = "equip"
        local result = originals.equip(self_obj, ...)
        runtime.event = nil
        return result
    end
    local animation_wrapper = function(self_obj, name, ...)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or not name:lower():find("reload", 1, true) then
            return originals.animation(self_obj, name, ...)
        end
        runtime.event = "reload"
        local result = originals.animation(self_obj, name, ...)
        runtime.event = nil
        -- RIVALS creates the reload sound just after starting its animation.
        -- Consume this marker on one sound only so later shots stay shots.
        runtime.reload_pending_until = os.clock() + 0.75
        return result
    end
    local create_sound_wrapper = function(self_obj, ...)
        local owner = self_obj.ClientFighter and self_obj.ClientFighter.Player
        if owner ~= local_player then return originals.create_sound(self_obj, ...) end
        local event = runtime.event
        if event == "reload" then
            runtime.reload_pending_until = 0
        elseif not event and os.clock() < runtime.reload_pending_until then
            event = "reload"
            runtime.reload_pending_until = 0
        end
        event = event or "shot"
        if combat:should_replace_sound(event) then return nil end
        return originals.create_sound(self_obj, ...)
    end

    viewmodel.PlayHitmarkerSound = hitmarker_wrapper
    if type(originals.equip) == "function" then viewmodel.Equip = equip_wrapper end
    if type(originals.animation) == "function" then viewmodel.PlayAnimation = animation_wrapper end
    client_item.CreateSound = create_sound_wrapper
    env.daydreamer_rivals_hitsound_cleanup = function()
        if viewmodel.PlayHitmarkerSound == hitmarker_wrapper then viewmodel.PlayHitmarkerSound = originals.hitmarker end
        if viewmodel.Equip == equip_wrapper then viewmodel.Equip = originals.equip end
        if viewmodel.PlayAnimation == animation_wrapper then viewmodel.PlayAnimation = originals.animation end
        if client_item.CreateSound == create_sound_wrapper then client_item.CreateSound = originals.create_sound end
        for sound in combat.hit_sounds do pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(combat.hit_sounds)
    end
    return true
end

--// misc.lua mounts this into its own tab via env.daydreamer_rivals_mount_weapon_sounds
function Combat:mount_weapon_sounds(target_tab)
    local ui = self.ui
    local hit_sec = target_tab:add_toggle("hit_sounds", "Weapon Sounds", "replace local hit, weapon, reload, and equip sounds with files you add")
    local hit_folder_row = hit_sec:add_status_row("folder", "sound folder")
    hit_folder_row:set_value(HIT_SOUND_DIR)
    local sound_dropdowns = {}
    local sound_layout_orders = {}
    local sound_events = {"hit", "shot", "reload", "equip"}
    local available_sound_files = self:list_hit_sound_files()

    local function refresh_sound_lists()
        available_sound_files = self:list_hit_sound_files()
        for _, event in ipairs(sound_events) do
            local custom_file_id = event .. "_file"
            local previous = ui.state.hit_sounds and ui.state.hit_sounds[custom_file_id]
            if sound_dropdowns[event] then sound_dropdowns[event]:destroy() end
            sound_dropdowns[event] = hit_sec:add_dropdown(custom_file_id, event .. " sound file", available_sound_files)
            sound_dropdowns[event].frame.LayoutOrder = sound_layout_orders[event]
            if previous and table.find(available_sound_files, previous) then
                ui:set_value("hit_sounds." .. custom_file_id, previous, true)
            end
        end
    end

    for _, event in ipairs(sound_events) do
        local custom_file_id = event .. "_file"
        sound_dropdowns[event] = hit_sec:add_dropdown(custom_file_id, event .. " sound file", available_sound_files)
        sound_layout_orders[event] = sound_dropdowns[event].frame.LayoutOrder
        hit_sec:add_button("test_" .. event, "play " .. event .. " sound", "test " .. event)
    end
    hit_sec:add_button("refresh_lists", "refresh lists", "refresh sound file lists")
    ui:on_change("hit_sounds.refresh_lists", refresh_sound_lists)
    hit_sec:add_slider("volume", "volume", 0, 150, "%", 70)
    hit_sec:add_slider("pitch", "pitch", 50, 200, "%", 100)
    for _, event in ipairs(sound_events) do
        ui:on_change("hit_sounds.test_" .. event, function()
            self:play_sound_event(event, true, true)
        end)
    end
end

function Combat:setup_hit_sounds()
    self:ensure_hit_sound_dir()
    self:install_sound_hooks()
    env.daydreamer_rivals_mount_weapon_sounds = function(target_tab)
        return self:mount_weapon_sounds(target_tab)
    end
end

--// fov drawings

function Combat:should_draw_any_fov()
    local ui = self.ui
    local s = ui and ui.state and ui.state.silent_aim
    if s then
        local module_on = s.menu_enabled == true or s.enabled == true
        if module_on and s.draw_fov == true then return true end
    end
    local a = ui and ui.state and ui.state.aim_assist
    if a then
        local module_on = a.menu_enabled == true or a.enabled == true
        if module_on and a.draw_fov == true then return true end
        if module_on and a.draw_edge_margin == true then return true end
    end
    return false
end

function Combat:update_fov_circle(name, center_fn)
    local entry = self.fov_circles[name]
    local st = self.ui.state[name]
    if not entry then return end
    local module_on = st and (st.menu_enabled == true or st.enabled == true)
    local draw = module_on and st.draw_fov == true
    if draw and not entry.circle then
        entry.circle = make_drawing_circle()
    end
    if not entry.circle then return end
    entry.circle.Visible = draw
    if not draw then return end
    entry.circle.Position = center_fn()
    entry.circle.Radius = math.max(1, tonumber(st.fov or st.fov_radius) or entry.default_radius)
    entry.circle.Color = get_fov_color(st, entry.default_color)
end

function Combat:update_edge_margin_box()
    local st = self.ui.state.aim_assist
    local module_on = st and (st.menu_enabled == true or st.enabled == true)
    local draw = module_on and st.draw_edge_margin == true
    if draw and not self.edge_margin_box then self.edge_margin_box = make_drawing_square() end
    if not self.edge_margin_box then return end
    self.edge_margin_box.Visible = draw
    if not draw then return end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then self.edge_margin_box.Visible = false; return end
    local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
    local margin = math.clamp(tonumber(st.edge_margin) or 25, 0, 200)
    self.edge_margin_box.Position = Vector2.new(margin, margin)
    self.edge_margin_box.Size = Vector2.new(math.max(1, viewport.X - margin * 2), math.max(1, viewport.Y - margin * 2))
    self.edge_margin_box.Color = get_fov_color(st, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
end

function Combat:start_fov_drawings()
    if not self:should_draw_any_fov() then
        if self.fov_draw_conn then
            self.fov_draw_conn:Disconnect()
            self.fov_draw_conn = nil
        end
        -- the loop is dead now, so hide the drawings ourselves or they linger at
        -- their last position
        for _, entry in pairs(self.fov_circles) do
            if entry.circle then entry.circle.Visible = false end
        end
        if self.edge_margin_box then self.edge_margin_box.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    if self.fov_draw_conn then self.fov_draw_conn:Disconnect() end
    self.fov_draw_conn = run_service.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        self:update_fov_circle("silent_aim", function()
            return fov_center(false)
        end)
        self:update_fov_circle("aim_assist", function()
            return fov_center(false)
        end)
        self:update_edge_margin_box()
    end))
end

function Combat:register_fov_circle(name, default_radius, default_color)
    self.fov_circles[name] = {
        circle = nil,
        default_radius = default_radius,
        default_color = default_color,
    }
    if self:should_draw_any_fov() then
        self:start_fov_drawings()
    end
end

function Combat:cleanup_fov_drawings()
    if self.fov_draw_conn then
        self.fov_draw_conn:Disconnect()
        self.fov_draw_conn = nil
    end
    for _, entry in pairs(self.fov_circles) do
        remove_drawing(entry.circle)
        entry.circle = nil
    end
    remove_drawing(self.edge_margin_box)
    self.edge_margin_box = nil
end

--// triggerbot

function Combat:build_triggerbot()
    local triggerbot_sec = self.tab:add_toggle("triggerbot", "Triggerbot", self:desc("triggerbot"))
    triggerbot_sec:add_slider("reaction_time", "reaction time", 0, 500, "ms", 0)
    triggerbot_sec:add_slider("miss_chance", "miss chance", 0, 100, "%", 0)
    triggerbot_sec:add_switch("katana_check", "katana deflect check", false)
    triggerbot_sec:add_switch("flashbang_check", "flashbang check", true)
    triggerbot_sec:add_switch("scope_check", "scope check", true)
    self.triggerbot_sec = triggerbot_sec
end

function Combat:install_triggerbot()
    local combat = self
    local ui = self.ui

    mechanics_controller._ToggleAutoShoot = function(p1)
        p1._auto_shoot_hash = p1._auto_shoot_hash + 1
        local current_hash = p1._auto_shoot_hash

        if p1._auto_shoot_connection then
            p1._auto_shoot_connection:Disconnect()
            p1._auto_shoot_connection = nil
        end

        local is_shooting = false

        p1._auto_shoot_connection = run_service.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if p1._auto_shoot_hash ~= current_hash then return end
            if not (p1.LocalFighter and p1.LocalFighter.EquippedItem and p1:IsAlive()) then return end
            local st = ui.state.triggerbot
            if not (st and st.enabled) then
                if is_shooting then
                    is_shooting = false
                    p1:EquippedItemInput("FinishShooting")
                end
                combat.next_shoot_time = 0
                return
            end

            --// flashbang check
            if st.flashbang_check ~= false and local_player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("FlashbangGui") then
                combat.next_shoot_time = 0
                return
            end

            --// get the game's built-in auto shoot reach
            local weapon_reach = p1.LocalFighter.EquippedItem:GetAutoShootReach()

            --// random scope check stuff
            if st.scope_check ~= false then
                if not weapon_reach or weapon_reach <= 0 then
                    if is_shooting then
                        is_shooting = false
                        p1:EquippedItemInput("FinishShooting")
                    end
                    combat.next_shoot_time = 0
                    return
                end
            else
                if not weapon_reach or weapon_reach <= 0 then
                    weapon_reach = 100
                end
            end

            local current_camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not current_camera then
                if is_shooting then
                    is_shooting = false
                    p1:EquippedItemInput("FinishShooting")
                end
                return
            end
            trigger_filter[1] = local_player.Character or workspace
            trigger_ray_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = trigger_filter
            local active_ray = workspace:Raycast(current_camera.CFrame.Position, current_camera.CFrame.LookVector * weapon_reach, trigger_ray_params)

            if active_ray and active_ray.Instance then
                local hit_model = active_ray.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

                --// simple visibility check pls dont kill performance
                if hit_model and hit_model:HasTag("Entity") and is_valid_enemy(hit_model)
                    and (st.katana_check ~= true or not is_katana_deflecting(hit_model)) then
                    local now = os.clock()
                    local delay = (st.reaction_time or 0) / 1000
                    if delay > 0 then
                        if combat.next_shoot_time == 0 then
                            combat.next_shoot_time = now + delay
                            return
                        elseif now < combat.next_shoot_time then
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    combat.next_shoot_time = 0

                    if not is_shooting then
                        local miss_chance = math.clamp(tonumber(st.miss_chance) or 0, 0, 100)
                        if miss_chance > 0 and math.random(1, 100) <= miss_chance then
                            combat.trigger_miss_target = hit_model
                            combat.trigger_miss_position = active_ray.Position
                            combat.trigger_miss_side = math.random(0, 1) == 0 and -1 or 1
                            combat.trigger_miss_until = os.clock() + 0.15
                        end
                        is_shooting = true
                        p1:EquippedItemInput("StartShooting")

                        if p1.LocalFighter.EquippedItem.Name ~= "Flamethrower" then
                            task.delay(0.01, function()
                                p1:EquippedItemInput("FinishShooting")
                                is_shooting = false
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end
            end

            combat.next_shoot_time = 0
            combat.trigger_miss_target = nil
            combat.trigger_miss_position = nil
            combat.trigger_miss_until = 0
            if is_shooting then
