                is_shooting = false
                p1:EquippedItemInput("FinishShooting")
            end
        end))
    end

    -- now trigger the hook if player is already spawned
    pcall(function()
        if mechanics_controller.LocalFighter then
            mechanics_controller:_ToggleAutoShoot()
        end
    end)
end

--// silent

function Combat:build_silent_aim()
    local ui = self.ui
    local silent_sec = self.tab:add_toggle("silent_aim", "Silent Aim", self:desc("silent_aim"))
    silent_sec:add_slider("fov", "fov", 10, 750, "px", 100)
    silent_sec:add_switch("draw_fov", "draw fov", false)
    local silent_fov_color = add_color_option(silent_sec, "fov_color", "fov color", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    silent_sec:add_slider("hit_chance", "hit chance", 0, 100, "%", 100)
    silent_sec:add_switch("katana_check", "katana deflect check", false)
    silent_sec:add_switch("visible_check", "visible check", false)
    silent_sec:add_switch("manipulation", "manipulation", true)
    silent_sec:add_dropdown("hit_part", "hit part", {
        "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Random", "Closest",
    })
    local silent_head_hit_chance = silent_sec:add_slider("head_hit_chance", "head hit chance", 0, 100, "%", 0)
    self.silent_sec = silent_sec

    local function update_silent_head_option(value)
        self:set_option_visible(silent_head_hit_chance, value == "Random" or value == "Closest")
    end
    update_silent_head_option(ui.state.silent_aim.hit_part)
    ui:on_change("silent_aim.hit_part", update_silent_head_option)

    local function update_silent_fov_color_option(value)
        self:set_option_visible(silent_fov_color, value == true)
    end
    update_silent_fov_color_option(ui.state.silent_aim.draw_fov)
    ui:on_change("silent_aim.draw_fov", update_silent_fov_color_option)
    ui:on_change("silent_aim.draw_fov", function() self:start_fov_drawings() end)
    ui:on_change("silent_aim.enabled", function() self:start_fov_drawings() end)
end

function Combat:get_silent_utility()
    if self.silent_utility then return self.silent_utility end
    local modules = replicated_storage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
    if not modules then
        if not self.silent_modules_warned then
            self.silent_modules_warned = true
        end
        return nil
    end
    local ok, mod = pcall(require, modules:FindFirstChild("Utility"))
    if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.Raycast) == "function" then
        self.silent_utility = mod
        return self.silent_utility
    end
    if not self.silent_modules_warned then
        self.silent_modules_warned = true
    end
    return nil
end

function Combat:install_silent_raycast()
    if self.silent_raycast_hooked then return end
    local utility = self:get_silent_utility()
    if not utility then return end
    local combat = self
    local ui = self.ui
    local original_raycast = utility.Raycast
    self.silent_raycast_hooked = true
    utility.Raycast = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(hook_self, from, to, range, ignore, mode, debug)
        if combat.trigger_miss_target and combat.trigger_miss_position and os.clock() <= combat.trigger_miss_until
            and type(range) == "number" and range > 0 then
            local miss_position = combat.trigger_miss_position
            combat.trigger_miss_target = nil
            combat.trigger_miss_position = nil
            combat.trigger_miss_until = 0

            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local right = cam and cam.CFrame.RightVector
            local delta = right and (miss_position + right * (TRIGGER_MISS_OFFSET * combat.trigger_miss_side) - from)
            if delta and delta.Magnitude > 0 then
                return original_raycast(hook_self, from, from + delta.Unit * range, range, ignore, mode, debug)
            end
        end

        local st = ui.state.silent_aim
        if st and st.enabled and should_silent_raycast(range) then
            local chance = math.clamp(tonumber(st.hit_chance) or 100, 0, 100)
            if chance > 0 and math.random(1, 100) <= chance then
                local result = build_silent_raycast_result(from, range, st)
                if result then
                    return result
                end
            end
        end
        return original_raycast(hook_self, from, to, range, ignore, mode, debug)
    end)
end

function Combat:start_silent_raycast_installer()
    task.spawn(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        for _ = 1, 120 do
            self:install_silent_raycast()
            if self.silent_raycast_hooked then return end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end))
end

--// aim assist --------------------------------------------------------------

function Combat:build_aim_assist()
    local ui = self.ui
    local aim_sec = self.tab:add_toggle("aim_assist", "Aim Assist", self:desc("aim_assist"))
    aim_sec:add_slider("fov", "fov", 10, 1000, "px", 500)
    aim_sec:add_switch("draw_fov", "draw fov", false)
    --aim_sec:add_switch("draw_edge_margin", "draw edge margin", false)
    local aim_fov_color = add_color_option(aim_sec, "fov_color", "fov color", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    aim_sec:add_slider("smoothness", "smoothness", 1, 100, "", 20)
    aim_sec:add_slider("reaction_time", "reaction time", 0, 500, "ms", 0)
    aim_sec:add_slider("target_switch_delay", "target switch delay", 0, 500, "ms", 0)
    --aim_sec:add_slider("edge_margin", "edge margin", 0, 200, "px", 25)
    aim_sec:add_switch("sticky_target", "sticky target", false)
    aim_sec:add_switch("katana_check", "katana deflect check", false)
    local aim_overshoot_chance = aim_sec:add_slider("overshoot_chance", "overshoot chance", 0, 100, "%", 0)
    local aim_overshoot_pixels = aim_sec:add_slider("overshoot_pixels", "overshoot distance", 1, 100, "px", 15)

    aim_sec:add_dropdown("aim_method", "aim method", {
        "Linear", "Snap", "Expo", "EaseIn", "EaseOut", "EaseInOut", "Cubic",
    })
    aim_sec:add_dropdown("aim_part", "aim part", {
        "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Random", "Closest",
    })
    local aim_head_hit_chance = aim_sec:add_slider("head_hit_chance", "head hit chance", 0, 100, "%", 0)
    aim_sec:add_switch("visible_check", "visibility check", false)
    aim_sec:add_switch("prediction", "prediction", false)
    local aim_prediction_x = aim_sec:add_slider("prediction_strength_x", "prediction strength x", 0, 100, "%", 50)
    local aim_prediction_y = aim_sec:add_slider("prediction_strength_y", "prediction strength y", 0, 100, "%", 50)
    aim_sec:add_switch("jitter", "jitter", false)
    local aim_jitter_x = aim_sec:add_slider("jitter_strength_x", "jitter strength x", 0, 200, "px", 0)
    local aim_jitter_y = aim_sec:add_slider("jitter_strength_y", "jitter strength y", 0, 200, "px", 0)
    self.aim_sec = aim_sec

    local function update_aim_head_option(value)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_head_hit_chance, value == "Random" or value == "Closest")
    end
    update_aim_head_option(ui.state.aim_assist.aim_part)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.aim_part", update_aim_head_option)

    local function update_aim_prediction_options(value)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_prediction_x, value == true)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_prediction_y, value == true)
    end
    update_aim_prediction_options(ui.state.aim_assist.prediction)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.prediction", update_aim_prediction_options)

    local function update_aim_jitter_options(value)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_jitter_x, value == true)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_jitter_y, value == true)
    end
    update_aim_jitter_options(ui.state.aim_assist.jitter)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.jitter", update_aim_jitter_options)

    local function update_aim_overshoot_options(value)
        local visible = (tonumber(value) or 0) > 0
        self:set_option_visible(aim_overshoot_pixels, visible)
    end
    update_aim_overshoot_options(ui.state.aim_assist.overshoot_chance)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.overshoot_chance", update_aim_overshoot_options)

    local function update_aim_fov_color_option(value)
        self:set_option_visible(aim_fov_color, value == true)
    end
    update_aim_fov_color_option(ui.state.aim_assist.draw_fov)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.draw_fov", update_aim_fov_color_option)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.draw_fov", function() self:start_fov_drawings() end)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.enabled", function() self:start_fov_drawings() end)
    ui:on_change("aim_assist.draw_edge_margin", function() self:start_fov_drawings() end)
end

function Combat:clear_aim_lock()
    self.aim_locked_target = nil
    self.aim_smooth_cf = nil
    self.aim_last_cam_cf = nil
    self.aim_overshoot_offset = nil
    self.aim_overshoot_until = 0
    self.aim_target_history = {}
end

function Combat:is_within_aim_distance(part, cam)
    if not part or not cam then return false end
    local esp = self.ui.state and self.ui.state.esp
    local cap = math.clamp(tonumber(esp and esp.distance_cap) or DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
    return (part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude <= cap
end

function Combat:roll_aim_overshoot(cam, target, st, now)
    self.aim_overshoot_offset = nil
    self.aim_overshoot_until = 0

    local chance = math.clamp(tonumber(st.overshoot_chance) or 0, 0, 100)
    if chance <= 0 or math.random(1, 100) > chance then return end

    local target_screen_pos, on_screen = world_to_screen(target.Position, cam)
    if not on_screen then return end

    local center = fov_center(false)
    local direction = Vector2.new(target_screen_pos.X - center.X, target_screen_pos.Y - center.Y)
    if direction.Magnitude < 1 then
        local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
        direction = Vector2.new(math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle))
    else
        direction = direction.Unit
    end

    self.aim_overshoot_offset = direction * math.clamp(tonumber(st.overshoot_pixels) or 15, 1, 100)
    self.aim_overshoot_until = now + AIM_OVERSHOOT_DURATION
end

Combat.closest_to_center = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    local st = self.ui.state.aim_assist
    if not st then return nil end
    local radius = st.fov or st.fov_radius or 500
    local target_part_name = st.aim_part or st.target_part or "Head"
    local mp = fov_center(false)
    if not mp then return nil end

    local best, best_dist = nil, radius
    for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= local_player and p.Character and is_valid_enemy(p.Character)
            and (st.katana_check ~= true or not is_katana_deflecting(p.Character)) then
            local part = hit_part_from_name(p.Character, target_part_name, st.head_hit_chance)
            if part and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and self:is_within_aim_distance(part, cam)
                and is_within_aim_viewport(part, cam, st) then
                local passes_visible_check = (not st.visible_check) or has_line_of_sight(part)
                if passes_visible_check then
                    local scr, on = world_to_screen(part.Position, cam)
                    if on then
                        local dx = scr.X - mp.X
                        local dy = scr.Y - mp.Y
                        local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                        if dist < best_dist then
                            best_dist = dist
                            best = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end)

Combat.step_aim_assist = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self, dt)
    dt = dt or (1 / 240)
    local st = self.ui.state.aim_assist
    if not (st and st.enabled) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
        return
    end

    local bind_mode = st.bind_mode or "toggle"

    -- "always" mode: keybind is irrelevant — run unconditionally
    if bind_mode ~= "always" and st.keybind == nil then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
        return
    end

    local function is_bound_input_pressed(keybind)
        local ok_mouse, pressed_mouse = pcall(function()
            if typeof(keybind) == "EnumItem" and string.find(tostring(keybind), "MouseButton") then
                return user_input_service:IsMouseButtonPressed(keybind)
            end
            return false
        end)
        if ok_mouse and pressed_mouse then
            return true
        end

        local ok_key, pressed_key = pcall(function()
            if typeof(keybind) == "EnumItem" then
                return user_input_service:IsKeyDown(keybind)
            end
            return false
        end)
        if ok_key and pressed_key then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    if bind_mode == "hold" then
        if not is_bound_input_pressed(st.keybind) then
            self:clear_aim_lock()
            return
        end
    end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end

    local now = os.clock()

    -- drop the lock if the locked target is invalid before considering a switch
    if self.aim_locked_target and (not self.aim_locked_target.Parent or not self.aim_locked_target:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
    end
    if self.aim_locked_target and not self:is_within_aim_distance(self.aim_locked_target, cam) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
    end
    if self.aim_locked_target and not is_within_aim_viewport(self.aim_locked_target, cam, st) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
    end
    if self.aim_locked_target and st.katana_check == true and is_katana_deflecting(self.aim_locked_target.Parent) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
    end
    if self.aim_locked_target and st.visible_check == true and not has_line_of_sight(self.aim_locked_target) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
    end

    local switch_delay = (tonumber(st.target_switch_delay) or 0) / 1000
    if not self.aim_locked_target then
        self.aim_locked_target = self:closest_to_center()
        self.aim_smooth_cf = cam.CFrame
        self.aim_last_cam_cf = cam.CFrame
        self.aim_target_history = {}
        if not self.aim_locked_target then return end
        self.aim_next_switch_time = now + switch_delay
        self:roll_aim_overshoot(cam, self.aim_locked_target, st, now)
    elseif not st.sticky_target then
        if now >= self.aim_next_switch_time then
            local candidate = self:closest_to_center()
            if candidate and candidate ~= self.aim_locked_target then
                self.aim_locked_target = candidate
                self.aim_target_history = {}
                self.aim_next_switch_time = now + switch_delay
                self:roll_aim_overshoot(cam, self.aim_locked_target, st, now)
            end
        end
    end
    if not self.aim_locked_target.Parent or not self.aim_locked_target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
        return
    end

    --// do not pull through walls if visible check is on
    if st.visible_check == true and not has_line_of_sight(self.aim_locked_target) then
        self:clear_aim_lock()
        return
    end

    local my_char = local_player.Character
    if not my_char then return end
    if not self.cam_controller then
        pcall(function()
            local cm = player_scripts.Controllers:FindFirstChild("CameraController")
            if cm and cm:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                self.cam_controller = require(cm)
            end
        end)
    end
    if not self.cam_controller or type(self.cam_controller.MimicRotation) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    if not self.aim_smooth_cf then
        self.aim_smooth_cf = cam.CFrame
    end
    if self.aim_last_cam_cf then
        local manual_delta = self.aim_last_cam_cf:ToObjectSpace(cam.CFrame)
        self.aim_smooth_cf = self.aim_smooth_cf * manual_delta
    end

    -- record the target's true position for the reaction-time buffer
    local true_pos = self.aim_locked_target.Position
    table.insert(self.aim_target_history, { t = now, pos = true_pos })
    while #self.aim_target_history > 1 and now - self.aim_target_history[1].t > 1 do
        table.remove(self.aim_target_history, 1)
    end

    -- reaction time: aim at where the target was `reaction_time` ms ago, so a
    -- sudden strafe takes that long to register in the aim
    local reaction_delay = (tonumber(st.reaction_time) or 0) / 1000
    local aim_pos = true_pos
    if reaction_delay > 0 then
        aim_pos = sample_delayed_position(self.aim_target_history, now - reaction_delay, true_pos)
    end

    -- prediction: lead the target by its current velocity. a stationary target
    -- has zero velocity, so this is a no-op no matter how high the strength is.
    if st.prediction == true then
        local velocity = Vector3.zero
        local part = self.aim_locked_target
        local ok_vel = pcall(function()
            local asm = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if asm then velocity = asm end
        end)
        if not ok_vel then
            pcall(function() velocity = part.Velocity end)
        end
        local strength_x = (tonumber(st.prediction_strength_x) or 0) / 100
        local strength_y = (tonumber(st.prediction_strength_y) or 0) / 100
        local lead = PREDICT_MAX_LEAD
        aim_pos = aim_pos + Vector3.new(
            velocity.X * strength_x * lead,
            velocity.Y * strength_y * lead,
            velocity.Z * strength_x * lead
        )
    end

    if st.jitter == true then
        local screen_point, on_screen = world_to_screen(aim_pos, cam)
        if on_screen then
            local jitter_x = math.clamp(tonumber(st.jitter_strength_x) or 0, 0, 1005)
            local jitter_y = math.clamp(tonumber(st.jitter_strength_y) or 0, 0, 1005)
            local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(
                screen_point.X + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * jitter_x,
                screen_point.Y + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * jitter_y
            )
            local distance = (aim_pos - ray.Origin).Magnitude
            aim_pos = ray.Origin + ray.Direction * distance
        end
    end

    if self.aim_overshoot_offset and now <= self.aim_overshoot_until then
        local screen_point, on_screen = world_to_screen(aim_pos, cam)
        if on_screen then
            local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(screen_point.X + self.aim_overshoot_offset.X, screen_point.Y + self.aim_overshoot_offset.Y)
            local distance = (aim_pos - ray.Origin).Magnitude
            aim_pos = ray.Origin + ray.Direction * distance
        end
    elseif self.aim_overshoot_offset then
        self.aim_overshoot_offset = nil
        self.aim_overshoot_until = 0
    end

    local look_cf = CFrame.lookAt(cam.CFrame.Position, aim_pos)
    local smoothness = (tonumber(st.smoothness) or 20) / 10
    local alpha = get_aim_lerp_alpha(smoothness, st.aim_method or st.aim_curve, dt)
    self.aim_smooth_cf = self.aim_smooth_cf:Lerp(look_cf, alpha)
    pcall(function()
        self.cam_controller:MimicRotation(self.aim_smooth_cf)
    end)
    self.aim_last_cam_cf = self.aim_smooth_cf
end)

function Combat:enable_aim_assist()
    if self.aim_assist_using_bind then
        pcall(function() run_service:UnbindFromRenderStep(AIM_ASSIST_BIND) end)
        self.aim_assist_using_bind = false
    end
    if self.aim_assist_conn then
        self.aim_assist_conn:Disconnect()
        self.aim_assist_conn = nil
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        run_service:UnbindFromRenderStep(AIM_ASSIST_BIND)
        run_service:BindToRenderStep(AIM_ASSIST_BIND, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function(dt)
            return self:step_aim_assist(dt)
        end)
    end)
    if ok then
        self.aim_assist_using_bind = true
    else
        self.aim_assist_conn = run_service.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
            return self:step_aim_assist(dt)
        end))
    end
end

--// bullet tracer

local function get_tracer_color(st)
    return get_fov_color({
        fov_color = st.color,
        fov_color_r = st.color_r,
        fov_color_g = st.color_g,
        fov_color_b = st.color_b,
    }, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
end

local function remove_tracer_entry(entry)
    for _, line in ipairs(entry.lines or {}) do remove_drawing(line) end
end

function Combat:add_tracer_controls(target_tab)
    local tracer_sec = target_tab:add_toggle("bullet_tracers", "Bullet Tracers", "draws a tracer for fired bullets")
    local tracer_line_type = tracer_sec:add_dropdown("line_type", "line type", {"Solid", "Dashed", "Lightning"})
    tracer_sec:add_slider("duration", "duration", 25, 1500, "ms", 250)
    tracer_sec:add_slider("max_distance", "max distance", 50, 4000, "studs", 2000)
    tracer_sec:add_slider("thickness", "thickness", 1, 8, "px", 2)
    tracer_sec:add_slider("transparency", "transparency", 0, 100, "%", 10)
    tracer_sec:add_switch("local_only", "local shots only", true)
    local tracer_lightning_jaggedness = tracer_sec:add_slider("lightning_jaggedness", "lightning jaggedness", 1, 50, "px", 14)
    add_color_option(tracer_sec, "color", "colour", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))

    self.ui:on_change("bullet_tracers.line_type", function(value)
        self:set_option_visible(tracer_lightning_jaggedness, value == "Lightning")
    end)
end

function Combat:clear_tracers()
    for i = #self.tracer_entries, 1, -1 do
        remove_tracer_entry(self.tracer_entries[i])
        self.tracer_entries[i] = nil
    end
end

function Combat:add_tracer_segment(start_pos, end_pos, st)
    if not start_pos or not end_pos then return end
    if (end_pos - start_pos).Magnitude > (tonumber(st.max_distance) or 2000) then return end

    local entry = {
        start_pos = start_pos,
        end_pos = end_pos,
        created_at = os.clock(),
        duration = math.max(0.025, (tonumber(st.duration) or 250) / 1000),
        line_type = st.line_type or "Solid",
        color = get_tracer_color(st),
        thickness = math.clamp(tonumber(st.thickness) or 2, 1, 8),
        transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(st.transparency) or 10, 0, 100) / 100,
    }
    local count = entry.line_type == "Solid" and 1 or 8
    entry.lines = {}
    entry.lightning_offsets = {}
    local jaggedness = math.clamp(tonumber(st.lightning_jaggedness) or 14, 1, 50)
    for i = 1, count do
        local ok, line = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Line") end)
        if not ok or not line then
            remove_tracer_entry(entry)
            return
        end
        line.Thickness = entry.thickness
        line.Color = entry.color
        entry.lines[i] = line
        entry.lightning_offsets[i] = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * jaggedness
    end
    self.tracer_entries[#self.tracer_entries + 1] = entry
end

function Combat:add_tracers_from_data(data, context)
    local st = self.ui.state.bullet_tracers
    if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
    if st.local_only ~= false and data.IsLocal ~= true then return end
    local muzzle = context and context.MuzzlePosition
    if not muzzle then return end

    local function add_result(result)
        if type(result) ~= "table" or not result.Position then return end
        local start_pos = result.StartPosition
            or (result.LastRaycastResult and result.LastRaycastResult.Position)
            or muzzle
        self:add_tracer_segment(start_pos, result.Position, st)
    end

    for _, group in pairs(data.RaycastResults or {}) do
        if type(group) == "table" and group.Position then
            add_result(group)
        elseif type(group) == "table" then
            for _, result in pairs(group) do add_result(result) end
        end
    end
end

function Combat:update_tracers()
    local now = os.clock()
    for i = #self.tracer_entries, 1, -1 do
        local entry = self.tracer_entries[i]
        local alpha = (now - entry.created_at) / entry.duration
        if alpha >= 1 then
            remove_tracer_entry(entry)
            table.remove(self.tracer_entries, i)
        else
            local from, from_on = world_to_screen(entry.start_pos)
            local to, to_on = world_to_screen(entry.end_pos)
            local visible = from_on and to_on
            local opacity = (1 - entry.transparency) * (1 - alpha)
            if entry.lines then
                for _, line in ipairs(entry.lines) do line.Visible = visible end
                if visible then
                    local start_2d = Vector2.new(from.X, from.Y)
                    local end_2d = Vector2.new(to.X, to.Y)
                    local delta = end_2d - start_2d
                    if entry.line_type == "Dashed" then
                        for j, line in ipairs(entry.lines) do
                            local from_alpha = (j - 1) / #entry.lines
                            local to_alpha = math.min(1, from_alpha + 0.55 / #entry.lines)
                            line.From = start_2d + delta * from_alpha
                            line.To = start_2d + delta * to_alpha
                            line.Transparency = opacity
                        end
                    elseif entry.line_type == "Lightning" then
                        local length = delta.Magnitude
                        local unit = length > 0 and delta.Unit or Vector2.new(1, 0)
                        local perpendicular = Vector2.new(-unit.Y, unit.X)
                        local previous = start_2d
                        for j, line in ipairs(entry.lines) do
                            local point
                            if j == #entry.lines then
                                point = end_2d
                            else
                                point = start_2d + delta * (j / #entry.lines) + perpendicular * (entry.lightning_offsets[j] or 0)
                            end
                            line.From = previous
                            line.To = point
                            line.Transparency = opacity
                            previous = point
                        end
                    else
                        local line = entry.lines[1]
                        line.From = start_2d
                        line.To = end_2d
                        line.Transparency = opacity
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function Combat:install_bullet_tracers()
    if self.tracer_play_wrapper then return end
    local modules = player_scripts:FindFirstChild("Modules")
    local mod = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("TracerEffect")
    local ok, effect = pcall(require, mod)
    if not ok or type(effect) ~= "table" or type(effect.Play) ~= "function" then return end
    self.tracer_effect = effect
    self.original_tracer_play = effect.Play
    local original_play = effect.Play
    self.tracer_play_wrapper = function(self_obj, data, properties, context)
        local result = original_play(self_obj, data, properties, context)
        pcall(function() self:add_tracers_from_data(data, context) end)
        return result
    end
    effect.Play = self.tracer_play_wrapper
    self.tracer_conn = run_service.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        return self:update_tracers()
    end))
end

function Combat:cleanup_tracers()
    if self.tracer_conn then self.tracer_conn:Disconnect(); self.tracer_conn = nil end
    self:clear_tracers()
    if self.tracer_effect and self.tracer_play_wrapper and self.tracer_effect.Play == self.tracer_play_wrapper then
        self.tracer_effect.Play = self.original_tracer_play
    end
    self.tracer_effect = nil
    self.tracer_play_wrapper = nil
    self.original_tracer_play = nil
end

function Combat:setup_tracers()
    -- wallhack/render tabs mount the tracer controls through lib
    self.lib.add_bullet_tracer_controls = function(target_tab)
        return self:add_tracer_controls(target_tab)
    end
    env.daydreamer_rivals_tracers_cleanup = function()
        self:cleanup_tracers()
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 120 do
            self:install_bullet_tracers()
            if self.tracer_play_wrapper then return end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

--// layout / lifecycle ------------------------------------------------------

function Combat:arrange_sections()
    local tab = self.tab
    if self.silent_sec.frame and self.aim_sec.frame and tab.left_col and tab.right_col then
        self.silent_sec.frame.Parent = tab.left_col
        self.silent_sec.frame.LayoutOrder = 1
        self.aim_sec.frame.Parent = tab.right_col
        self.aim_sec.frame.LayoutOrder = 1
        if self.triggerbot_sec.frame then
            self.triggerbot_sec.frame.Parent = tab.left_col
            self.triggerbot_sec.frame.LayoutOrder = 2
        end
    end
end

function Combat:install_respawn_cleanup()
    --run cleanup on respawn
    local_player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(LPH_JIT(function()
        self:clear_aim_lock()
        self.aim_smooth_cf = nil
        self.aim_last_cam_cf = nil
        self.silent_utility = nil
        self.silent_modules_warned = false
    end))
end

function Combat:load_ragebot()
    -- ragebot sections live inside the combat tab
    local tab = self.tab
    local ui = self.ui
    local ok, rb = pcall(function()
        -- compiled build: module is embedded as __rv_ragebot
        if type(__dreamer_require) == "function" and type(__dreamer_mods) == "table" and __dreamer_mods["__rv_ragebot"] then
            return __dreamer_require("__rv_ragebot")
        end
        -- try all the same paths init.lua uses
        local dirs = {
            "daydreamer_dev/scripts/Rivals",
            "scripts/Rivals",
        }
        for _, dir in ipairs(dirs) do
            local path = dir .. "/ragebot.lua"
            if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(path) and type(readfile) == "function" then
                local src = readfile(path)
                local fn = assert(loadstring(src, path))
                return fn()
            end
        end
        --// just load the file directly
        for _, dir in ipairs(dirs) do
            local ok2, chunk = pcall(loadfile, dir .. "/ragebot.lua")
            if ok2 and chunk then return chunk() end
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and type(rb) == "function" then
        pcall(rb, tab, ui)
    elseif ok and type(rb) == "table" and type(rb.build) == "function" then
        pcall(rb.build, rb, tab, ui)
    end
end

function Combat:build()
    if shared then
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_fov_cleanup = function()
            self:cleanup_fov_drawings()
        end
    end

    self:setup_hit_sounds()
    self:build_triggerbot()
    self:install_triggerbot()
    self:build_silent_aim()
    self:start_silent_raycast_installer()
    self:build_aim_assist()
    self:register_fov_circle("silent_aim", 100, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    self:register_fov_circle("aim_assist", 500, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    self:setup_tracers()
    self:arrange_sections()
    self:enable_aim_assist()
    self:install_respawn_cleanup()
    self:load_ragebot()
    return self.tab
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("combat")

    --// cleanup stuff
    if shared and type(shared.daydreamer_rivals_fov_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_fov_cleanup)
    end
    if type(env.daydreamer_rivals_hitsound_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(env.daydreamer_rivals_hitsound_cleanup)
    end
    if type(env.daydreamer_rivals_tracers_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(env.daydreamer_rivals_tracers_cleanup)
    end

    local combat = Combat.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.combat = combat
    return combat:build()
end

end)

-- ─── tab: cosmetics ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_cosmetics', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local jit_max = "LPH_" .. "JIT_MAX"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(f) return f end
    env[jit_max] = env[jit_max] or function(f) return f end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(f) return f end
end

local function elevate(fn)
    local genv = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local set_id = genv.setthreadidentity or genv.set_thread_identity or genv.setidentity or genv.setthreadcontext or genv.set_thread_context
    local get_id = genv.getthreadidentity or genv.get_thread_identity or genv.getidentity or genv.getthreadcontext or genv.get_thread_context
    if not set_id then return fn() end
    local ok_id, old_id = pcall(function() return get_id and get_id() end)
    pcall(set_id, 7)
    local res = fn()
    pcall(set_id, (ok_id and type(old_id) == "number") and old_id or 2)
    return res
end

local plrs = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local reps = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local http = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))
local collection_service = cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService"))

local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
if not lp then
    repeat task.wait() until plrs.LocalPlayer
    lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
end

local lps = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 30) or lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
local ctrls = lps and (lps:WaitForChild("Controllers", 30) or lps:FindFirstChild("Controllers"))
local rmods = reps:WaitForChild("Modules", 30) or reps:FindFirstChild("Modules")

local elib_mod = rmods and rmods:WaitForChild("EnumLibrary", 30)
local elib = elib_mod and require(elib_mod)
if elib then elib:WaitForEnumBuilder() end
local clib_mod = rmods and rmods:WaitForChild("CosmeticLibrary", 30)
local clib = clib_mod and require(clib_mod)
local ilib_mod = rmods and rmods:WaitForChild("ItemLibrary", 30)
local ilib = ilib_mod and require(ilib_mod)
local dctrl_mod = ctrls and ctrls:WaitForChild("PlayerDataController", 30)
local dctrl = dctrl_mod and require(dctrl_mod)
local coss = clib and clib.Cosmetics or {}

local cdata = dctrl and dctrl.CurrentData
if dctrl and not cdata then
    task.spawn(function()
        repeat task.wait() until dctrl.CurrentData
        cdata = dctrl.CurrentData
    end)
end

local fctrl
pcall(function()
    if ctrls then
        local fctrl_mod = ctrls:WaitForChild("FighterController", 30)
        if fctrl_mod then fctrl = require(fctrl_mod) end
    end
end)

local rems = reps:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local drems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Data")
local eqrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("EquipCosmetic")
local favrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("FavoriteCosmetic")
local rrems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Replication")
local frems = rrems and rrems:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
local uirem = frems and frems:FindFirstChild("UseItem")

local eqremid = eqrem and tostring(eqrem)
local favremid = favrem and tostring(favrem)
local uiremid = uirem and tostring(uirem)
local idbroken = eqrem and (eqremid == tostring(game) or eqremid == "RemoteEvent")
local cosTypes = { Skin=true, Wrap=true, Charm=true, Finisher=true, KillEffect=true, HitEffect=true, DeathEffect=true }

local function matchrem(self_obj, id)
    if not id then return false end
    if not idbroken then return tostring(self_obj) == id end
    return false
end

local function isEqRemCall(self_obj, args)
    if matchrem(self_obj, eqremid) then return true end
    if not idbroken then return false end
    return type(args[1]) == "string" and type(args[2]) == "string" and cosTypes[args[2]]
end

local function isFavRemCall(self_obj, args)
    if matchrem(self_obj, favremid) then return true end
    if not idbroken or not favrem then return false end
    return type(args[1]) == "string" and type(args[2]) == "string" and (args[3] == true or args[3] == false or args[3] == nil) and not cosTypes[args[2]]
end

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end

local Cosmetics = {}
Cosmetics.__index = Cosmetics

function Cosmetics.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        equip = {},
        favs = {},
        cwep = nil,
        vprof = lp,
        lwep = nil,
        fcache = {},
        finv = {},
        custom_wraps = {},
        custom_assets = {},
        custom_originals = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
        viewmodels = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    }, Cosmetics)
end

function Cosmetics:banned(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return true end
    return name:find("MISSING_") or name:find("Bubblegum") or name:find("Ragdoll") or name:find("Fall Apart") or name:find("Every Finisher Ever")
end

function Cosmetics:toenum(name)
    if not elib then return nil end
    local ok, id = pcall(elib.ToEnum, elib, name)
    return ok and id or nil
end

function Cosmetics:clonecos(name, ctype, inv, favonly)
    if self:banned(name) then return nil end
    local base = coss[name]
    if not base then return nil end
    local data = table.clone(base)
    data.Name = name
    data.Type = data.Type or ctype
    data.Seed = data.Seed or math.random(1, 1000000)
    local id = self:toenum(name)
    if id then data.Enum = id data.ObjectID = data.ObjectID or id end
    if inv ~= nil then data.Inverted = inv end
    if favonly ~= nil then data.OnlyUseFavorites = favonly end
    return data
end

local savefile = "daydreamer/rivals/cosmetics.json"
local legacyfile = "unlockall/config.json"
local custom_asset_dir = "daydreamer/assets/images"

function Cosmetics:ensurefolder(path)
    if not makefolder then return end
    if isfolder and isfolder(path) then return end
    pcall(function()
        makefolder(path)
    end)
end

function Cosmetics:hasfile(path)
    if not isfile then return false end
    local ok, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
    return ok and exists
end

function Cosmetics:readtext(path)
    if not readfile then return nil end
    local ok, text = pcall(readfile, path)
    return ok and text or nil
end

function Cosmetics:writetext(path, text)
    if not writefile then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        writefile(path, text)
    end)
    return ok
end

function Cosmetics:migratelegacycfg()
    if self:hasfile(savefile) or not self:hasfile(legacyfile) then return end
    local text = self:readtext(legacyfile)
    if text then
        self:ensurefolder("daydreamer")
        self:ensurefolder("daydreamer/rivals")
        self:writetext(savefile, text)
    end
end

function Cosmetics:buildcfg()
    local cfg = { equipped = {}, favorites = self.favs, custom_wraps = self.custom_wraps }
    for wep, cos in self.equip do
        local slot = {}
        cfg.equipped[wep] = slot
        for ctype, data in cos do
            if data and data.Name and not self:banned(data.Name) then
                slot[ctype] = { name = data.Name, seed = data.Seed, inverted = data.Inverted }
            end
        end
    end
    return cfg
end

function Cosmetics:savecfg()
    if not writefile then return end
    local self_ref = self
    pcall(function()
        self_ref:ensurefolder("daydreamer")
        self_ref:ensurefolder("daydreamer/rivals")
        self_ref:writetext(savefile, http:JSONEncode(self_ref:buildcfg()))
    end)
end

function Cosmetics:readcfg(path)
    if not self:hasfile(path) then return nil end
    local text = self:readtext(path)
    if not text then return nil end
    local ok, cfg = pcall(function()
        return http:JSONDecode(text)
    end)
    return ok and cfg or nil
end

function Cosmetics:applycfg(cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return false end
    table.clear(self.equip)
    if cfg.equipped then
        for wep, cos in cfg.equipped do
            self.equip[wep] = {}
            for ctype, data in cos do
                if data and not self:banned(data.name) then
                    local cloned = self:clonecos(data.name, ctype, data.inverted)
                    if cloned then
                        cloned.Seed = data.seed or cloned.Seed
                        self.equip[wep][ctype] = cloned
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    self.favs = cfg.favorites or {}
    self.custom_wraps = type(cfg.custom_wraps) == "table" and cfg.custom_wraps or {}
    table.clear(self.custom_assets)
    return true
end

function Cosmetics:ensurecustomassetdir()
    self:ensurefolder("daydreamer")
    self:ensurefolder("daydreamer/assets")
    self:ensurefolder(custom_asset_dir)
end

function Cosmetics:customassetpath(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil, "enter an image filename" end
    local filename = name:match("([^/\\]+)$")
    if not filename or filename == "" then return nil, "enter an image filename" end
    if not filename:lower():match("%.png$") and not filename:lower():match("%.jpe?g$")
        and not filename:lower():match("%.webp$") then
        return nil, "use a .png, .jpg, .jpeg, or .webp image"
    end
    return custom_asset_dir .. "/" .. filename, filename
end

function Cosmetics:loadcfg()
    self:applycfg(self:readcfg(savefile) or self:readcfg(legacyfile))
end

function Cosmetics:rebuildinv()
    table.clear(self.finv)
    for name in coss do
        if not self:banned(name) then self.finv[name] = true end
    end
    for _, cos in self.equip do
        for _, data in cos do
            if data and data.Name and not self:banned(data.Name) then self.finv[data.Name] = true end
        end
    end
end

function Cosmetics:isenabled()
    local state = self.ui.state.unlockall
    return state and state.enabled
end

function Cosmetics:saferep(key)
    pcall(function()
        if not cdata and dctrl.CurrentData then cdata = dctrl.CurrentData end
        if cdata then cdata:Replicate(key) end
    end)
end

function Cosmetics:getewep()
    if not fctrl then return self.lwep end
    local fighter = fctrl:GetFighter(lp)
    if not fighter or not fighter.Items then return self.lwep end
    for _, item in fighter.Items do
        if item.IsEquipped then
            self.lwep = item.Name
            return item.Name
        end
    end
    return self.lwep
end

local function get_inst_player(inst)
    if not inst then return nil end
    if typeof(inst) == "Instance" and inst:IsA("Player") then return inst end
    if inst.Player and typeof(inst.Player) == "Instance" and inst.Player:IsA("Player") then return inst.Player end
    if inst.ClientFighter and inst.ClientFighter.Player then return inst.ClientFighter.Player end
    if inst.Fighter and inst.Fighter.Player then return inst.Fighter.Player end
    if inst.LocalPlayer then return inst.LocalPlayer end
    return nil
end

function Cosmetics:PlayerDataGet(oget, dctrl_inst, key)
    local data = oget(dctrl_inst, key)
    local owner = get_inst_player(dctrl_inst)
    if owner and owner ~= lp then return data end
    if self:isenabled() and key == "CosmeticInventory" then
        local proxy = {}
        if data then
            for name, value in data do
                if not self:banned(name) then proxy[name] = value end
            end
        end
        for name in self.finv do proxy[name] = true end
        return proxy
    end
    if self:isenabled() and key == "FavoritedCosmetics" then
        local res = data and table.clone(data) or {}
        for wep, fav in self.favs do
            local slot = res[wep] or {}
            res[wep] = slot
            for name, isfav in fav do
                if not self:banned(name) then slot[name] = isfav end
            end
        end
        return res
    end
    return data
end

function Cosmetics:PlayerDataGetWeaponData(ogetwep, dctrl_inst, wname)
    local data = ogetwep(dctrl_inst, wname)
    if not self:isenabled() or not data then return data end
    local owner = get_inst_player(dctrl_inst)
    if owner and owner ~= lp then return data end
    local merged = table.clone(data)
    merged.Name = wname
    local weq = self.equip[wname]
    if weq then
        for ctype, cdata in weq do
            merged[ctype] = cdata
        end
    end
    return merged
end

function Cosmetics:CreateViewModel(ocvm, citem_inst, vmref)
    if not self:isenabled() then return ocvm(citem_inst, vmref) end
    local wname = citem_inst.Name
    local wplr = get_inst_player(citem_inst)
    self.cwep = (wplr == lp) and wname or nil
    if wplr == lp and self.equip[wname] and self.equip[wname].Skin and vmref then
        local skin = self.equip[wname].Skin
        local datakey = citem_inst:ToEnum("Data")
        if vmref[datakey] then
            vmref[datakey][citem_inst:ToEnum("Skin")] = skin
            vmref[datakey][citem_inst:ToEnum("Name")] = skin.Name
        elseif vmref.Data then
            vmref.Data.Skin = skin
            vmref.Data.Name = skin.Name
        end
    end
    local res = ocvm(citem_inst, vmref)
    self.cwep = nil
    return res
end

function Cosmetics:GetWrap(ogw, cvm_inst)
    if not self:isenabled() then return ogw(cvm_inst) end
    local item = cvm_inst.ClientItem
    local wname = item and item.Name
    local wplr = get_inst_player(item) or get_inst_player(cvm_inst)
    local weq = wname and wplr == lp and self.equip[wname]
    return (weq and weq.Wrap) or ogw(cvm_inst)
end

function Cosmetics:getcustomasset(path)
    if type(path) ~= "string" or path == "" then return nil, "choose an image file first" end
    local cached = self.custom_assets[path]
    if cached then return cached end

    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not self:hasfile(path) then
        return nil, "file was not found by your executor"
    end

    local genv = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local loader = getcustomasset or genv.getcustomasset or getsynasset or genv.getsynasset
        or get_custom_asset or genv.get_custom_asset or getcustomassetasync or genv.getcustomassetasync
    if type(loader) ~= "function" then
        return nil, "your executor does not support custom assets"
    end
    local ok, asset = pcall(loader, path)
    if not ok or type(asset) ~= "string" or asset == "" then
        return nil, "failed to load that image as a custom asset"
    end
    self.custom_assets[path] = asset
    return asset
end

function Cosmetics:refreshcustomasset(path)
    self.custom_assets[path] = nil
    return self:getcustomasset(path)
end

function Cosmetics:getcustomwrap(wname)
    local wrap = wname and self.custom_wraps[wname]
    if type(wrap) == "string" then return wrap, 1 end -- pre-scale config format
    if type(wrap) ~= "table" then return nil end
    return wrap.path, math.clamp(tonumber(wrap.scale) or 1, 0.1, 20)
end

function Cosmetics:getinstances(value, found, seen, depth)
    if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
        found[value] = true
        for _, child in value:GetDescendants() do found[child] = true end
        return
    end
    if type(value) ~= "table" or depth >= 2 or seen[value] then return end
    seen[value] = true
    for _, child in value do
        self:getinstances(child, found, seen, depth + 1)
    end
end

function Cosmetics:restorecustomwrap(viewmodel)
    elevate(function()
        local saved = self.custom_originals[viewmodel]
        if not saved then return end
        for _, texture in ipairs(saved.cleanup or {}) do pcall(function() texture:Destroy() end) end
        for inst, props in saved do
            if inst ~= "cleanup" and inst and inst.Parent then
                for prop, value in props do pcall(function() inst[prop] = value end) end
            end
        end
        self.custom_originals[viewmodel] = nil
    end)
end

function Cosmetics:applycustomwrap(viewmodel)
    elevate(function()
        local item = viewmodel and viewmodel.ClientItem
        local wname = item and item.Name
        local owner = get_inst_player(item) or get_inst_player(viewmodel)
        local path, scale
        if owner == lp then path, scale = self:getcustomwrap(wname) end
        self:restorecustomwrap(viewmodel)
        if not path then
            return
        end

        local asset = self:getcustomasset(path)
        if not asset then return end
        local saved = { cleanup = {} }
        self.custom_originals[viewmodel] = saved
        local model = viewmodel.ItemModel
        if not model then return end
        local faces = {
            Enum.NormalId.Top, Enum.NormalId.Bottom, Enum.NormalId.Front,
            Enum.NormalId.Back, Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right,
        }
        for _, inst in model:GetDescendants() do
            if inst:IsA("BasePart") and collection_service:HasTag(inst, "Wrappable") then
                for _, face in ipairs(faces) do
                    local texture = Instance.new("Texture")
                    texture.Name = "DaydreamerCustomWrap"
                    texture.Texture = asset
                    texture.Face = face
                    texture.StudsPerTileU = scale
                    texture.StudsPerTileV = scale
                    texture.ZIndex = 10
                    texture.Parent = inst
                    table.insert(saved.cleanup, texture)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function Cosmetics:refreshcustomwraps()
    pcall(function()
        local fighter = fctrl and fctrl:GetFighter(lp)
        local items = fighter and fighter.Items
        if items then
            for _, item in items do
                if item.ViewModel then self.viewmodels[item.ViewModel] = true end
            end
        end
    end)
    for viewmodel in self.viewmodels do
        if viewmodel and not viewmodel._destroyed and viewmodel._UpdateWrap then
            pcall(function() viewmodel:_UpdateWrap() end)
        end
    end
end

function Cosmetics:ClientViewModelNew(onew, rdata, cliitm)
    if not self:isenabled() then
        local res = onew(rdata, cliitm)
        if res then
            self.viewmodels[res] = true
            self:applycustomwrap(res)
        end
        return res
    end
    local wplr = get_inst_player(cliitm)
    local wname = (wplr == lp and self.cwep) or (cliitm and cliitm.Name)
    if wplr == lp and wname and self.equip[wname] then
        local rcls = require(reps.Modules.ReplicatedClass)
        local datakey = rcls:ToEnum("Data")
        rdata[datakey] = rdata[datakey] or {}
        local cos = self.equip[wname]
        local slot = rdata[datakey]
        if cos.Skin then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Skin")] = cos.Skin end
        if cos.Wrap then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Wrap")] = cos.Wrap end
        if cos.Charm then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Charm")] = cos.Charm end
    end
    local res = onew(rdata, cliitm)
    if res then self.viewmodels[res] = true end
    if wplr == lp and wname and self.equip[wname] and self.equip[wname].Wrap and res._UpdateWrap then
        res:_UpdateWrap()
        task.delay(0.1, function() if not res._destroyed then res:_UpdateWrap() end end)
    end
    return res
end

function Cosmetics:GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(ogvi, ilib_inst, wdata, hires)
    if not self:isenabled() or not wdata then return ogvi(ilib_inst, wdata, hires) end
    local wplr = get_inst_player(wdata)
    if wplr and wplr ~= lp then return ogvi(ilib_inst, wdata, hires) end
    local wname = wdata.Name
    local weq = self.equip[wname]
    if weq and weq.Skin and (wdata.Skin == weq.Skin or self.vprof == lp) then
        local sinfo = ilib_inst.ViewModels[weq.Skin.Name]
        if sinfo then return sinfo[hires and "ImageHighResolution" or "Image"] or sinfo.Image end
    end
    return ogvi(ilib_inst, wdata, hires)
end

function Cosmetics:PlayFinisher(ofin, cent_inst, fname, ...)
    if not self:isenabled() then return ofin(cent_inst, fname, ...) end
    local killer = get_inst_player(cent_inst)
    if killer and killer ~= lp then return ofin(cent_inst, fname, ...) end
    local ewep = self:getewep()
    local tfin = ewep and (self.fcache[ewep] or (self.equip[ewep] and self.equip[ewep].Finisher and self.equip[ewep].Finisher.Name))
    return ofin(cent_inst, tfin or fname, ...)
end

function Cosmetics:namecall_hook(oncall, self_obj, ...)
    local args = { ... }

    if uirem and matchrem(self_obj, uiremid) and fctrl then
        pcall(function()
            local fighter = fctrl:GetFighter(lp)
            if fighter and fighter.Items then
                local id = args[1]
                for _, item in fighter.Items do
                    if item:Get("ObjectID") == id then
                        self.lwep = item.Name
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if not self:isenabled() then return oncall(self_obj, ...) end

    if isEqRemCall(self_obj, args) then
        local wname, ctype, cname, opts = args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4] or {}
        if not cname or cname == "None" or cname == "" then
            self.equip[wname] = self.equip[wname] or {}
            self.equip[wname][ctype] = nil
            if not next(self.equip[wname]) then self.equip[wname] = nil end
            self:rebuildinv()
            task.defer(function()
                self:saferep("WeaponInventory")
                task.wait(0.2)
                self:savecfg()
            end)
            return oncall(self_obj, ...)
        end
        if self:banned(cname) then return oncall(self_obj, ...) end
        local rdata = shared.daydreamer_originals and shared.daydreamer_originals.dctrl_Get and shared.daydreamer_originals.dctrl_Get(dctrl, "CosmeticInventory")
        if rdata and rdata[cname] == true then
            return oncall(self_obj, ...)
        end
        local cloned = self:clonecos(cname, ctype, opts.IsInverted, opts.OnlyUseFavorites)
        self.equip[wname] = self.equip[wname] or {}
        if cloned then self.equip[wname][ctype] = cloned end
        if ctype == "Finisher" then self.fcache[wname] = cname end
        self:rebuildinv()
        task.defer(function()
            self:saferep("WeaponInventory")
            task.wait(0.2)
            self:savecfg()
        end)
        return
    end

    if isFavRemCall(self_obj, args) then
        local fwep, fname, fstate = args[1], args[2], args[3]
        if not fname or fname == "None" or fname == "" then return oncall(self_obj, ...) end
        if self:banned(fname) then return oncall(self_obj, ...) end
        self.favs[fwep] = self.favs[fwep] or {}
        self.favs[fwep][fname] = fstate or nil
        self:savecfg()
        task.spawn(function() self:saferep("FavoritedCosmetics") end)
        return
    end

    return oncall(self_obj, ...)
end

function Cosmetics:setup_hooks()
    local originals = shared.daydreamer_originals
    if not originals then
        originals = {}
        shared.daydreamer_originals = originals
    end

    -- hook dctrl.Get
    if not originals.dctrl_Get and dctrl then
        originals.dctrl_Get = dctrl.Get
        dctrl.Get = function(self_obj, key)
            local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
            if not inst then return originals.dctrl_Get(self_obj, key) end
            return inst:PlayerDataGet(originals.dctrl_Get, self_obj, key)
        end
    end

    -- hook dctrl.GetWeaponData
    if not originals.dctrl_GetWeaponData and dctrl then
        originals.dctrl_GetWeaponData = dctrl.GetWeaponData
        dctrl.GetWeaponData = function(self_obj, wname)
            local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
            if not inst then return originals.dctrl_GetWeaponData(self_obj, wname) end
            return inst:PlayerDataGetWeaponData(originals.dctrl_GetWeaponData, self_obj, wname)
        end
    end

    -- hook namecall
    if hookmetamethod and eqrem then
        if not originals.namecall then
            originals.namecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self_obj, ...)
                if getnamecallmethod() ~= "FireServer" then return originals.namecall(self_obj, ...) end
                local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                if not inst then return originals.namecall(self_obj, ...) end
                return inst:namecall_hook(originals.namecall, self_obj, ...)
            end))
        end
    end

    -- hook citem._CreateViewModel
    local citem
    pcall(function()
        if lps then
            local crc = lps:WaitForChild("Modules", 10)
                and lps.Modules:WaitForChild("ClientReplicatedClasses", 10)
            if crc then
                local cf = crc:WaitForChild("ClientFighter", 10)
                local ci_mod = cf and cf:WaitForChild("ClientItem", 10)
                if ci_mod then citem = require(ci_mod) end
            end
        end
    end)
    if citem and citem._CreateViewModel then
        if not originals.citem_CreateViewModel then
            originals.citem_CreateViewModel = citem._CreateViewModel
            citem._CreateViewModel = function(self_obj, vmref)
                local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                if not inst then return originals.citem_CreateViewModel(self_obj, vmref) end
                return inst:CreateViewModel(originals.citem_CreateViewModel, self_obj, vmref)
            end
        end
    end

    -- hook cvm
    local vmmod
    pcall(function()
        if lps then
            local crc = lps:WaitForChild("Modules", 10)
                and lps.Modules:WaitForChild("ClientReplicatedClasses", 10)
            if crc then
                local cf = crc:WaitForChild("ClientFighter", 10)
                local ci_mod = cf and cf:WaitForChild("ClientItem", 10)
                vmmod = ci_mod and ci_mod:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
            end
        end
    end)
    if vmmod then
        local cvm
        pcall(function() cvm = require(vmmod) end)
        if cvm then
            if cvm.GetWrap then
                if not originals.cvm_GetWrap then
                    originals.cvm_GetWrap = cvm.GetWrap
                    cvm.GetWrap = function(self_obj)
                        local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                        if not inst then return originals.cvm_GetWrap(self_obj) end
                        return inst:GetWrap(originals.cvm_GetWrap, self_obj)
                    end
                end
            end
            if cvm.new then
                if not originals.cvm_new then
                    originals.cvm_new = cvm.new
                    cvm.new = function(rdata, cliitm)
                        local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                        if not inst then return originals.cvm_new(rdata, cliitm) end
                        return inst:ClientViewModelNew(originals.cvm_new, rdata, cliitm)
                    end
                end
            end
            if cvm._UpdateWrap then
                if not originals.cvm_UpdateWrap then
                    originals.cvm_UpdateWrap = cvm._UpdateWrap
                    cvm._UpdateWrap = function(self_obj, ...)
                        local result = originals.cvm_UpdateWrap(self_obj, ...)
                        local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                        if inst then inst:applycustomwrap(self_obj) end
                        return result
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- hook ilib
    if ilib and ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
        if not originals.ilib_GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
            originals.ilib_GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
            ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = function(self_obj, wdata, hires)
                local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                if not inst then return originals.ilib_GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(self_obj, wdata, hires) end
                return inst:GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(originals.ilib_GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData, self_obj, wdata, hires)
            end
        end
    end

    -- hook cent._PlayFinisher
    local cent
    pcall(function()
        if lps then
            local crc = lps:WaitForChild("Modules", 10)
                and lps.Modules:WaitForChild("ClientReplicatedClasses", 10)
            local ce_mod = crc and crc:WaitForChild("ClientEntity", 10)
            if ce_mod then cent = require(ce_mod) end
        end
    end)
    if cent and cent._PlayFinisher then
        if not originals.cent_PlayFinisher then
            originals.cent_PlayFinisher = cent._PlayFinisher
            cent._PlayFinisher = function(self_obj, fname, ...)
                local inst = dreamer.cosmetics
                if not inst then return originals.cent_PlayFinisher(self_obj, fname, ...) end
                return inst:PlayFinisher(originals.cent_PlayFinisher, self_obj, fname, ...)
            end
        end
    end
end

function Cosmetics:build()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local unlockall_sec = tab:add_toggle("unlockall", "Unlock All", desc["unlockall"])
    unlockall_sec:set_bindable(false)
    unlockall_sec:add_button("save_cosmetics", "save all cosmetics now", "save cosmetics")
    unlockall_sec:add_button("export_cosmetics", "export cosmetics to clipboard", "export cosmetics")
    unlockall_sec:add_textbox("import_text", "import cosmetics", "", "paste exported cosmetics here...")
    unlockall_sec:add_button("import_cosmetics", "paste above, then import", "import cosmetics")
    local custom_assets_row = unlockall_sec:add_status_row("custom_assets_location", "image folder")
    custom_assets_row:set_value("daydreamer/assets/images")
    unlockall_sec:add_textbox("custom_wrap_path", "custom wrap filename", "", "example.png from daydreamer/assets/images")
    unlockall_sec:add_slider("custom_wrap_scale", "custom wrap tile size", 0.1, 20, " studs", 1, 0.1)
    unlockall_sec:add_button("apply_custom_wrap", "apply file to equipped gun now", "apply custom wrap")
    unlockall_sec:add_button("refresh_custom_wrap", "reload equipped gun's image file", "refresh custom wrap")
    unlockall_sec:add_button("clear_custom_wrap", "clear equipped gun custom wrap", "clear custom wrap")

    local self_ref = self

    ui:on_change("unlockall.save_cosmetics", function()
        self_ref:savecfg()
        ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "saved all cosmetics successfully", duration = 3 })
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.export_cosmetics", function()
        if not setclipboard then
            ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "clipboard is unavailable on this executor", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return http:JSONEncode(self_ref:buildcfg()) end)
        if ok and encoded then
            setclipboard(encoded)
            ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "copied your cosmetics to clipboard", duration = 3 })
        else
            ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "failed to export cosmetics for some reason", duration = 4 })
        end
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.import_cosmetics", function()
        local text = ui.state.unlockall and ui.state.unlockall.import_text
        if (not text or text:gsub("%s+", "") == "") and getclipboard then
            text = getclipboard()
        end
        if not text or text:gsub("%s+", "") == "" then
            ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "paste an exported cosmetics string into the box first", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        local ok, cfg = pcall(function() return http:JSONDecode(text) end)
        if not ok or type(cfg) ~= "table" or (cfg.equipped == nil and cfg.favorites == nil) then
            ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "that text is not a valid cosmetics export", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        self_ref:applycfg(cfg)
        self_ref:rebuildinv()
        self_ref:savecfg()
        self_ref:saferep("WeaponInventory")
        self_ref:saferep("FavoritedCosmetics")
        table.clear(self_ref.fcache)
        for wname, wdata in self_ref.equip do
            if wdata.Finisher and wdata.Finisher.Name then
                self_ref.fcache[wname] = wdata.Finisher.Name
            end
        end
        if type(ui.set_value) == "function" then
            pcall(function() ui:set_value("unlockall.import_text", "") end)
        end
        ui:notify({ title = "Cosmetics", text = "imported cosmetics successfully!", duration = 3 })
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.apply_custom_wrap", function()
        local state = ui.state.unlockall or {}
        local path, filename_or_err = self_ref:customassetpath(state.custom_wrap_path)
        if not path then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = filename_or_err, duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        local weapon = self_ref:getewep()
        if not weapon then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = "equip a gun first", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        local asset, err = self_ref:refreshcustomasset(path)
        if not asset then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = err, duration = 5 })
            return
        end
        local scale = math.clamp(tonumber(state.custom_wrap_scale) or 1, 0.1, 20)
        self_ref.custom_wraps[weapon] = { path = path, scale = scale }
        self_ref:savecfg()
        self_ref:refreshcustomwraps()
        ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = filename_or_err .. " applied to " .. weapon, duration = 3 })
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.refresh_custom_wrap", function()
        local weapon = self_ref:getewep()
        local path = weapon and self_ref:getcustomwrap(weapon)
        if not path then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = "apply a file to the equipped gun first", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        local asset, err = self_ref:refreshcustomasset(path)
        if not asset then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = err, duration = 5 })
            return
        end
        self_ref:refreshcustomwraps()
        ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = "reloaded for " .. weapon, duration = 3 })
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.custom_wrap_scale", function()
        local weapon = self_ref:getewep()
        local path = weapon and self_ref:getcustomwrap(weapon)
        if not path then return end
        local state = ui.state.unlockall or {}
        self_ref.custom_wraps[weapon] = {
            path = path,
            scale = math.clamp(tonumber(state.custom_wrap_scale) or 1, 0.1, 20),
        }
        self_ref:savecfg()
        self_ref:refreshcustomwraps()
    end)

    ui:on_change("unlockall.clear_custom_wrap", function()
        local weapon = self_ref:getewep()
        if not weapon then
            ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = "equip a gun first", duration = 4 })
            return
        end
        self_ref.custom_wraps[weapon] = nil
        self_ref:savecfg()
        self_ref:refreshcustomwraps()
        ui:notify({ title = "Custom wrap", text = "cleared for " .. weapon, duration = 3 })
    end)

    self:ensurecustomassetdir()
    self:migratelegacycfg()
    self:loadcfg()
    self:rebuildinv()

    for wname, wdata in self.equip do
        if wdata.Finisher and wdata.Finisher.Name then
            self.fcache[wname] = wdata.Finisher.Name
        end
    end

    self:setup_hooks()
    self:refreshcustomwraps()
end

function Cosmetics:install_cleanup()
    local self_ref = self
    if shared then
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_cosmetics_cleanup = function()
            if dreamer.cosmetics == self_ref then
                for viewmodel in self_ref.viewmodels do self_ref:restorecustomwrap(viewmodel) end
                dreamer.cosmetics = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("misc")

    if shared and type(shared.daydreamer_rivals_cosmetics_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_cosmetics_cleanup)
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_cosmetics_cleanup = nil
    end

    local cosmetics = Cosmetics.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.cosmetics = cosmetics
    cosmetics:build()
    cosmetics:install_cleanup()

    return tab
end

end)

-- ─── tab: spoofers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_spoofers', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(f) return f end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(f) return f end
end

local run = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local replicated_storage = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local core_gui = cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui"))
local lp = players.LocalPlayer

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local cfg = dreamer.spoofers
if type(cfg) ~= "table" then
    cfg = {}
    dreamer.spoofers = cfg
end

local defaults = {
    name_enabled = false,
    name_value = "",
    dispname_enabled = false,
    dispname_value = "",
    premium = false,
    verified = false,
    avatar_enabled = false,
    avatar_userid = 0,
    level_enabled = false,
    level = 1000,
    streak_enabled = false,
    streak = 0,
    elo_enabled = false,
    elo = 0,
    device_spoof = false,
    platform = "computer",
}
for key, value in pairs(defaults) do
    if cfg[key] == nil then cfg[key] = value end
end

local device_codes = { mobile = "Touch", controller = "Gamepad", vr = "VR", computer = "MouseKeyboard" }
local platform_images = {
    computer = "rbxassetid://17136633356",
    mobile = "rbxassetid://17136633510",
    controller = "rbxassetid://17136633629",
    vr = "rbxassetid://17136765745",
}

local function setup_hooks()
    if cfg.hooked_index then return end
    if not hookmetamethod or not newcclosure then
        print("functions not available")
        return
    end

    cfg.original_index = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self, key)
        local result = cfg.original_index(self, key)
        if key == "RankedCurrentELO" and result == nil then
            if cfg.elo_enabled and cfg.elo > 0 then
                return cfg.elo
            end
            return 0
        end
        if key == "Level" and cfg.level_enabled and typeof(self) == "userdata" then
            return cfg.level
        end
        return result
    end)))
    cfg.hooked_index = true
end
pcall(setup_hooks)

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.track(self, conn)
    self.conns[#self.conns + 1] = conn
    return conn
end
function fallback_helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn) end
    for i = #conns, 1, -1 do conns[i] = nil end
end

local Spoofers = {}
Spoofers.__index = Spoofers
cfg.Spoofers = Spoofers

function Spoofers.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        cfg = cfg,
        conns = {},
        device_last = 0,
        next_stats_apply = 0,
        next_level_patch = 0,
        next_device_patch = 0,
        next_escape_patch = 0,
        device_loop_token = 0,
    }, Spoofers)
end

function Spoofers:desc(id)
    local descriptions = self.ui and self.ui.descriptions or {}
    return descriptions[id]
end

function Spoofers:track(conn)
    return self.helpers.track(self, conn)
end

function Spoofers:send_device_code()
    local code = device_codes[self.cfg.platform]
    if code then
        pcall(function()
            replicated_storage.Remotes.Replication.Fighter.SetControls:FireServer(code)
        end)
        self.device_last = tick()
    end
end

function Spoofers:apply_identity()
    if self.cfg.name_enabled and self.cfg.name_value ~= "" then
        lp.Name = self.cfg.name_value
        local dname = self.cfg.dispname_enabled and self.cfg.dispname_value ~= "" and self.cfg.dispname_value or self.cfg.name_value
        lp.DisplayName = dname
        if lp.Character then
            lp.Character.Name = self.cfg.name_value
            local hum = lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.DisplayName = dname end
        end
    end
    if self.cfg.avatar_enabled and self.cfg.avatar_userid ~= 0 then
        lp.UserId = self.cfg.avatar_userid
        lp.CharacterAppearanceId = self.cfg.avatar_userid
    end
end

function Spoofers:char_appearance()
    if not self.cfg.avatar_enabled or self.cfg.avatar_userid == 0 then return end
    local plr = players:FindFirstChild(self.cfg.name_value) or lp
    if not plr.Character then return end
    local ok, appearance = pcall(function()
        return players:GetCharacterAppearanceAsync(self.cfg.avatar_userid)
    end)
    if not ok or not appearance then return end
    for _, v in ipairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("Accessory") or v:IsA("Shirt") or v:IsA("Pants") or v:IsA("BodyColors") then
            v:Destroy()
        end
    end
    for _, v in ipairs(appearance:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("Shirt") or v:IsA("Pants") or v:IsA("BodyColors") then
            v.Parent = plr.Character
        elseif v:IsA("Accessory") then
            plr.Character.Humanoid:AddAccessory(v)
        end
    end
    local parent = plr.Character.Parent
    plr.Character.Parent = nil
    plr.Character.Parent = parent
end

function Spoofers:apply_stats()
    if not (self.cfg.level_enabled or self.cfg.streak_enabled or self.cfg.elo_enabled) then return end
    pcall(function()
        local ls = lp:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
        if self.cfg.level_enabled then
            lp:SetAttribute("Level", self.cfg.level)
            if ls then
                local lv = ls:FindFirstChild("Level")
                if lv then lv.Value = self.cfg.level end
            end
        end
        if self.cfg.streak_enabled then
            lp:SetAttribute("StatisticDuelsWinStreak", self.cfg.streak)
            if ls then
                local ws = ls:FindFirstChild("Win Streak")
                if ws then ws.Value = self.cfg.streak end
            end
        end
        if self.cfg.elo_enabled and self.cfg.elo > 0 then
            lp:SetAttribute("DisplayELO", self.cfg.elo)
        end
    end)
end

function Spoofers:patch_level()
    if not self.cfg.level_enabled then return end
    pcall(function()
        local ls = lp:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
        if ls then
            local lv = ls:FindFirstChild("Level")
            if lv and lv.Value ~= self.cfg.level then
                lv.Value = self.cfg.level
            end
        end
    end)
end

function Spoofers:patch_device_gui()
    if not self.cfg.device_spoof then return end
    local name = lp.Name
    local img = platform_images[self.cfg.platform] or platform_images.computer
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return end

    local plr_inst = players:FindFirstChild(name)
    local char = plr_inst and plr_inst.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local ctrl = hrp
        and hrp:FindFirstChild("Nametag")
        and hrp.Nametag:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        and hrp.Nametag.Frame:FindFirstChild("Player")
        and hrp.Nametag.Frame.Player:FindFirstChild("Controls")
    if ctrl then ctrl.Image = img end

    local mf = pg:FindFirstChild("MainGui") and pg.MainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
    if not mf then return end

    local sb = mf:FindFirstChild("DuelInterfaces")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces:FindFirstChild("DuelInterface")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface:FindFirstChild("Scoreboard")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface.Scoreboard:FindFirstChild("Container")
    if sb then
        for _, v in ipairs(sb:GetDescendants()) do
            if v.Name == "Username" and v.Text:find("@" .. name) then
                pcall(function() v.Parent.Container.TeammateSlot.Container.Controls.Image = img end)
            end
        end
    end

    local top = mf:FindFirstChild("DuelInterfaces")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces:FindFirstChild("DuelInterface")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface:FindFirstChild("Top")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface.Top:FindFirstChild("Scores")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface.Top.Scores:FindFirstChild("Teams")
    if top then
        for _, v in ipairs(top:GetDescendants()) do
            if v.Name == "Headshot" and v.Image:find(tostring(self.cfg.avatar_userid)) then
                pcall(function() v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Controls").Image = img end)
            end
        end
    end

    local results = mf:FindFirstChild("DuelInterfaces")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces:FindFirstChild("DuelInterface")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface:FindFirstChild("FinalResults")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface.FinalResults:FindFirstChild("Winners")
        and mf.DuelInterfaces.DuelInterface.FinalResults.Winners:FindFirstChild("Players")
    if results then
        for _, v in ipairs(results:GetDescendants()) do
            if v.Name == "Username" and v.Text:find("@" .. name) then
                pcall(function() v.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Controls").Image = img end)
            end
        end
    end
end

function Spoofers:patch_escape_menu()
    if not self.cfg.name_enabled and not self.cfg.avatar_enabled then return end
    pcall(function()
        local escape = core_gui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui") and core_gui.RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("SettingsHub")
        if not escape then return end
        for _, v in ipairs(escape:GetDescendants()) do
            if self.cfg.name_enabled and self.cfg.name_value ~= "" then
                if v:IsA("TextLabel") and v.Text == lp.Name then
                    v.Text = self.cfg.name_value
                end
                if v:IsA("TextLabel") and v.Text == "@" .. lp.Name then
                    v.Text = "@" .. self.cfg.name_value
                end
                if v:IsA("TextLabel") and (v.Text == lp.DisplayName or v.Text == "@" .. lp.DisplayName) then
                    local dname = self.cfg.dispname_enabled and self.cfg.dispname_value ~= "" and self.cfg.dispname_value or self.cfg.name_value
                    v.Text = v.Text:sub(1, 1) == "@" and "@" .. dname or dname
                end
            end
            if self.cfg.avatar_enabled and self.cfg.avatar_userid ~= 0 then
                if v:IsA("ImageLabel") and v.Image:find(tostring(lp.UserId)) then
                    v.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. self.cfg.avatar_userid .. "&width=150&height=150&format=png"
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function Spoofers:start_loops()
    self:track(lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        self:apply_identity()
        self:char_appearance()
    end))

    self:track(run.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now < self.next_stats_apply then return end
        self.next_stats_apply = now + 0.2
        self:apply_stats()
    end)))

    self:track(run.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now >= self.next_level_patch then
            self.next_level_patch = now + 0.2
            self:patch_level()
        end
