        if now >= self.next_device_patch then
            self.next_device_patch = now + 0.35
            self:patch_device_gui()
        end
        if now >= self.next_escape_patch then
            self.next_escape_patch = now + 0.5
            self:patch_escape_menu()
        end
    end)))

    self.device_loop_token = self.device_loop_token + 1
    local token = self.device_loop_token
    task.spawn(function()
        while token == self.device_loop_token do
            task.wait(15)
            if token ~= self.device_loop_token then break end
            if self.cfg.device_spoof and tick() - self.device_last > 14 then
                self:send_device_code()
            end
        end
    end)
end

function Spoofers:build()
    local tab = self.tab
    local ui = self.ui

    local name_sec = tab:add_toggle("name_enabled", "Name Spoof", self:desc("name_spoof"))
    name_sec:add_textbox("name_value", "username", "", "enter username...")
    name_sec:add_switch("dispname_enabled", "spoof display name", false)
    name_sec:add_textbox("dispname_value", "display name", "", "enter display name...")
    local dispname_frame = name_sec.options_frame and name_sec.options_frame:FindFirstChild("dispname_value")
    if dispname_frame then dispname_frame.Visible = false end
    name_sec:add_switch("badges_premium", "premium", false)
    name_sec:add_switch("badges_verified", "verified", false)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.name_enabled = val == true
        self:apply_identity()
    end)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.name_value", function(val)
        self.cfg.name_value = val or ""
        self:apply_identity()
    end)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.dispname_enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.dispname_enabled = val == true
        self:apply_identity()
    end)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.dispname_value", function(val)
        self.cfg.dispname_value = val or ""
        self:apply_identity()
    end)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.badges_premium", function(val)
        self.cfg.premium = val == true
    end)
    ui:on_change("name_enabled.badges_verified", function(val)
        self.cfg.verified = val == true
    end)

    local avatar_sec = tab:add_toggle("avatar_enabled", "Avatar Spoof", self:desc("avatar_spoof"))
    avatar_sec:add_textbox("avatar_userid", "user id", "", "enter user id...")
    ui:on_change("avatar_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.avatar_enabled = val == true
        self:apply_identity()
        if val then self:char_appearance() end
    end)
    ui:on_change("avatar_enabled.avatar_userid", function(val)
        self.cfg.avatar_userid = tonumber(val) or 0
        self:apply_identity()
        if self.cfg.avatar_enabled then self:char_appearance() end
    end)

    local level_sec = tab:add_toggle("level_enabled", "Level Spoof", self:desc("level"))
    level_sec:add_textbox("level_value", "value", "1000", "enter level...")
    ui:on_change("level_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.level_enabled = val == true
    end)
    ui:on_change("level_enabled.level_value", function(val)
        self.cfg.level = tonumber(val) or 1000
    end)

    local elo_sec = tab:add_toggle("elo_enabled", "ELO Spoof", self:desc("elo"))
    elo_sec:add_textbox("elo_value", "value", "0", "enter elo...")
    ui:on_change("elo_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.elo_enabled = val == true
    end)
    ui:on_change("elo_enabled.elo_value", function(val)
        self.cfg.elo = tonumber(val) or 0
    end)

    local ws_sec = tab:add_toggle("streak_enabled", "Win Streak Spoof", self:desc("winstreak"))
    ws_sec:add_textbox("streak_value", "value", "0", "enter streak...")
    ui:on_change("streak_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.streak_enabled = val == true
    end)
    ui:on_change("streak_enabled.streak_value", function(val)
        self.cfg.streak = tonumber(val) or 0
    end)

    local device_sec = tab:add_toggle("device_enabled", "Device Spoof", self:desc("device_spoof"))
    device_sec:add_dropdown("platform_type", "platform", {"computer", "mobile", "controller", "vr"})
    ui:on_change("device_enabled.enabled", function(val)
        self.cfg.device_spoof = val == true
        if val then self:send_device_code() end
    end)
    ui:on_change("device_enabled.platform_type", function(val)
        self.cfg.platform = val or "computer"
        if self.cfg.device_spoof then self:send_device_code() end
    end)
end

function Spoofers:cleanup()
    self.device_loop_token = self.device_loop_token + 1
    self.helpers.disconnect_all(self.conns)
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("spoofers")
    if dreamer.spoofers_instance and type(dreamer.spoofers_instance.cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(function() dreamer.spoofers_instance:cleanup() end)
    end
    local spoofers = Spoofers.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.spoofers_instance = spoofers
    spoofers:start_loops()
    spoofers:build()
    return tab
end

end)

-- ─── tab: wallhack ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_wallhack', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(fn) return fn end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(fn) return fn end
end

local cloneref = cloneref or function(o) return o end
local players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local run_service = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local workspace = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
local local_player = players.LocalPlayer

--// resolve fighter controller
local fc
local function get_fc()
    if not fc then
        local ok, result = pcall(require, local_player.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController)
        if ok then fc = result end
    end
    return fc
end

local font_regular = Font.new("rbxassetid://16658221428", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
local font_bold = Font.new("rbxassetid://12187363368", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)

local math_min, math_max, math_floor, math_clamp = math.min, math.max, math.floor, math.clamp
local math_sqrt, math_atan2, math_deg = math.sqrt, math.atan2, math.deg

local COLOR_BLACK = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local COLOR_WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 800
local MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 250
local MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 2000

local function get_esp_distance_cap(state)
    local esp = state and state.esp
    return math_clamp(tonumber(esp and esp.distance_cap) or DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
end

local function get_feature_distance_cap(state, feature_state)
    return math_clamp(
        tonumber(feature_state and feature_state.distance_cap) or DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP,
        MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP,
        MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP
    )
end

local R6_SKELETON_BONES = {
    { {"Head", oy = -0.5},         {"Torso", oy = 0.5} },
    { {"Torso", oy = 0.5},         {"Torso", oy = -0.5} },

    { {"Torso", ox = -0.25, oy = -0.5}, {"Left Leg",  oy = -0.5} },

}

if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then

    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn) end
    for i = #conns, 1, -1 do conns[i] = nil end
end
function fallback_helpers.get_hui()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local success, result = pcall(gethui)
        if success and result then return result end
    end
    local core_gui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local roblox_gui = core_gui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
    if roblox_gui then return roblox_gui end
    return core_gui
end
function fallback_helpers.is_enemy(player, local_plr)
    if not player or player == local_plr then return false end
    if player.Team ~= nil and player.Team == local_plr.Team then return false end
    if player:GetAttribute("TeamID") ~= nil and player:GetAttribute("TeamID") == local_plr:GetAttribute("TeamID") then return false end
    return true
end
function fallback_helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if type(section.add_color_picker) == "function" then
        return section:add_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    if type(section.add_color) == "function" then
        return section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    default_color = typeof(default_color) == "Color3" and default_color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_r", text .. " r", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.R * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_g", text .. " g", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.G * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_b", text .. " b", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.B * 255 + 0.5))
    return nil
end

local Wallhack = {}
Wallhack.__index = Wallhack

function Wallhack.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        Targets = {},
        Connections = {},
        Holder = nil,
    }, Wallhack)
end

function Wallhack:read_color(opt_id, key, default)
    local color = self.helpers.read_color and self.helpers.read_color(self.ui, opt_id, key)
    if color then return color end
    local state = self.ui.state[opt_id]
    if state then
        if typeof(state[key]) == "Color3" then return state[key] end
        if state[key .. "_r"] ~= nil or state[key .. "_g"] ~= nil or state[key .. "_b"] ~= nil then
            return Color3.fromRGB(
                math_clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_r"]) or (default.R * 255), 0, 255),
                math_clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_g"]) or (default.G * 255), 0, 255),
                math_clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_b"]) or (default.B * 255), 0, 255)
            )
        end
    end
    return default
end

function Wallhack:add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if self.helpers.add_color_option then
        return self.helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    return fallback_helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
end

function Wallhack:build_box_objects(holder)
    local objects = {}
    local parent_gui = holder

    --// bounding box target frame
    local target_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    target_holder.Visible = false
    target_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    target_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    target_holder.Parent = parent_gui
    objects.TargetHolder = target_holder

    -- glow
    local box_glow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    box_glow.Image = "rbxassetid://110204605000367"
    box_glow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    box_glow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(Vector2.new(21, 21), Vector2.new(79, 79))
    box_glow.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
    box_glow.ImageTransparency = 1
    box_glow.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated
    box_glow.Visible = true
    box_glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box_glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -21, 0, -21)
    box_glow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    box_glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box_glow.Parent = target_holder
    objects.BoxGlow = box_glow

    local box_glow_grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    box_glow_grad.Rotation = 90
    box_glow_grad.Parent = box_glow
    objects.BoxGlowGradient = box_glow_grad

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 21)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 20)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 21)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 20)
    padding.Parent = box_glow

    -- 2D Box Outer Outline (UIStroke wrapper)
    local box_outline_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    box_outline_holder.Visible = false
    box_outline_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box_outline_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box_outline_holder.Parent = box_glow
    objects.BoxOutlineHolder = box_outline_holder

    local box_outline = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    box_outline.Thickness = 3
    box_outline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    box_outline.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
    box_outline.Parent = box_outline_holder
    objects.BoxOutline = box_outline

    local box_outline_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    box_outline_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    box_outline_corner.Parent = box_outline_holder
    objects.BoxOutlineCorner = box_outline_corner

    -- 2D Box Inner Line
    local box_inline_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    box_inline_holder.Visible = false
    box_inline_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box_inline_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box_inline_holder.Parent = box_glow
    objects.BoxInlineHolder = box_inline_holder

    local box_inline = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    box_inline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    box_inline.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
    box_inline.Parent = box_inline_holder
    objects.BoxInline = box_inline

    local box_inline_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    box_inline_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    box_inline_corner.Parent = box_inline_holder
    objects.BoxInlineCorner = box_inline_corner

    -- Box Fill
    local box_fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    box_fill.Visible = false
    box_fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    box_fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box_fill.Parent = box_glow
    objects.BoxFill = box_fill

    local box_fill_grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    box_fill_grad.Rotation = 270
    box_fill_grad.Parent = box_fill
    objects.BoxFillGradient = box_fill_grad

    local box_fill_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    box_fill_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    box_fill_corner.Parent = box_fill
    objects.BoxFillCorner = box_fill_corner

    --//corner line wrapper
    local corner_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    corner_holder.Visible = false
    corner_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    corner_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner_holder.Parent = box_glow
    objects.CornerHolder = corner_holder

    for i = 1, 8 do
        local line = Instance.new("Frame")
        line.Visible = false
        line.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        line.BorderSizePixel = 0
        line.Parent = corner_holder
        objects["Line_" .. i] = line

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        stroke.Parent = line
        objects["LineStroke_" .. i] = stroke

        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
        corner.Parent = line
        objects["LineCorner_" .. i] = corner
    end

    --// skeleton bones
    local skeleton_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    skeleton_holder.Visible = false
    skeleton_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    skeleton_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    skeleton_holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
    skeleton_holder.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    skeleton_holder.Parent = parent_gui
    objects.SkeletonHolder = skeleton_holder

    for i = 1, SKELETON_BONE_COUNT do
        local bone = Instance.new("Frame")
        bone.Visible = false
        bone.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        bone.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bone.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bone.Parent = skeleton_holder
        objects["Bone_" .. i] = bone

        local bone_stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        bone_stroke.Thickness = 1
        bone_stroke.Color = COLOR_BLACK
        bone_stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        bone_stroke.Parent = bone
        objects["BoneStroke_" .. i] = bone_stroke
    end

    --// layout dirs
    local directions = {"Top", "Bottom", "Left", "Right"}
    for _, dir in ipairs(directions) do
        local bar_holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar_holder.Visible = false
        bar_holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar_holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar_holder.Parent = target_holder
        objects[dir .. "BarHolder"] = bar_holder

        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = bar_holder

        if dir == "Top" then
            layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
            layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
            bar_holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
            bar_holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, -5)
            bar_holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 4, 0, 0)
            bar_holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        elseif dir == "Bottom" then
            layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
            layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
            bar_holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, -2, 1, 3)
            bar_holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 4, 0, 0)
            bar_holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        elseif dir == "Left" then
            layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
            layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
            bar_holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            bar_holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, -2)
            bar_holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 4)
            bar_holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        elseif dir == "Right" then
            layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
            layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
            bar_holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, 8, 0, -2)
            bar_holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 4)
            bar_holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        end
    end

    -- Ammo bar structure (bottom bar)
    do
        local parent_holder = objects.BottomBarHolder

        local outline = Instance.new("Frame")
        outline.Visible = false
        outline.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        outline.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        outline.BorderSizePixel = 0
        outline.Parent = parent_holder
        objects.AmmoBarOutline = outline

        local outline_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        outline_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2)
        outline_corner.Parent = outline
        objects.AmmoBarOutlineCorner = outline_corner

        local bar_fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar_fill.Visible = true
        bar_fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        bar_fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bar_fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar_fill.Parent = outline
        objects.AmmoBar = bar_fill

        local bar_fill_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        bar_fill_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2)
        bar_fill_corner.Parent = bar_fill
        objects.AmmoBarCorner = bar_fill_corner

        local bar_grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        bar_grad.Rotation = 0
        bar_grad.Parent = bar_fill
        objects.AmmoBarGradient = bar_grad

        local bar_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        bar_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar_text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bar_text.TextSize = 10
        bar_text.FontFace = font_regular
        bar_text.ZIndex = 10
        bar_text.Visible = false
        bar_text.Parent = outline
        objects.AmmoBarText = bar_text

        local text_stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        text_stroke.Thickness = 1
        text_stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        text_stroke.Parent = bar_text
    end

    -- healthbar structures
    do
        local parent_holder = objects.LeftBarHolder

        local outline = Instance.new("Frame")
        outline.Visible = false
        outline.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        outline.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        outline.BorderSizePixel = 0
        outline.Parent = parent_holder
        objects.HealthBarOutline = outline

        local outline_stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        outline_stroke.Thickness = 1
        outline_stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        outline_stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        outline_stroke.Parent = outline
        objects.HealthBarOutlineStroke = outline_stroke

        local outline_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        outline_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
        outline_corner.Parent = outline
        objects.HealthBarOutlineCorner = outline_corner

        local bar_fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar_fill.Visible = true
        bar_fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        bar_fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bar_fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar_fill.Parent = outline
        objects.HealthBar = bar_fill

        local bar_fill_corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        bar_fill_corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
        bar_fill_corner.Parent = bar_fill
        objects.HealthBarCorner = bar_fill_corner

        local bar_grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        bar_grad.Rotation = 90
        bar_grad.Parent = bar_fill
        objects.HealthBarGradient = bar_grad

        local bar_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        bar_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar_text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bar_text.TextSize = 10
        bar_text.FontFace = font_regular
        bar_text.ZIndex = 10
        bar_text.Visible = false
        bar_text.Parent = outline
        objects.HealthBarText = bar_text

        local text_stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        text_stroke.Thickness = 1
        text_stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        text_stroke.Parent = bar_text
    end

    return objects
end

function Wallhack:setup_highlight(target_info, char)
    if target_info.Objects.Highlight then
        pcall(function() target_info.Objects.Highlight:Destroy() end)
    end
    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Enabled = false
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee = char
    highlight.Parent = char
    target_info.Objects.Highlight = highlight
end

function Wallhack:disconnect_character_cache(target_info)
    local conns = target_info.CharacterCacheConnections
    if not conns then return end
    for _, conn in pairs(conns) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    target_info.CharacterCacheConnections = nil
end

function Wallhack:remove_box_part(parts, part)
    for i = #parts, 1, -1 do
        if parts[i] == part then
            table.remove(parts, i)
            return
        end
    end
end

function Wallhack:rebuild_character_cache(target_info, character)
    local box_parts = {}
    local parts_by_name = {}
    local is_dyn_box_fn = self.helpers.is_dynamic_box_part or fallback_helpers.is_dynamic_box_part
    if character then
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                parts_by_name[child.Name] = child
                if is_dyn_box_fn(child) then
                    box_parts[#box_parts + 1] = child
                end
            end
        end
    end
    target_info.CachedCharacter = character
    target_info.BoxParts = box_parts
    target_info.PartsByName = parts_by_name
    local get_skel_bones_fn = self.helpers.get_skeleton_bones or fallback_helpers.get_skeleton_bones
    target_info.SkeletonBones = character and get_skel_bones_fn(character, parts_by_name) or R6_SKELETON_BONES
end

function Wallhack:track_character_cache(target_info, character)
    self:disconnect_character_cache(target_info)
    self:rebuild_character_cache(target_info, character)
    if not character then return end

    local conns = {}
    local self_ref = self
    local is_dyn_box_fn = self.helpers.is_dynamic_box_part or fallback_helpers.is_dynamic_box_part
    local get_skel_bones_fn = self.helpers.get_skeleton_bones or fallback_helpers.get_skeleton_bones

    conns.ChildAdded = character.ChildAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(child)
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            target_info.PartsByName[child.Name] = child
            if is_dyn_box_fn(child) then
                target_info.BoxParts[#target_info.BoxParts + 1] = child
            end
            target_info.SkeletonBones = get_skel_bones_fn(character, target_info.PartsByName)
        end
    end))
    conns.ChildRemoved = character.ChildRemoved:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(child)
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            if target_info.PartsByName[child.Name] == child then
                target_info.PartsByName[child.Name] = nil
            end
            self_ref:remove_box_part(target_info.BoxParts, child)
            target_info.SkeletonBones = get_skel_bones_fn(character, target_info.PartsByName)
        end
    end))
    target_info.CharacterCacheConnections = conns
end

function Wallhack:create_target_gui(player)
    local target_info = {
        Objects = {},
        CharacterObjects = {},
        Connections = {}
    }

    local objects = self:build_box_objects(self.Holder)
    target_info.Objects = objects

    local character = player.Character
    target_info.CharacterObjects.Character = character
    if character then
        target_info.CharacterObjects.HumanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        self:track_character_cache(target_info, character)
        self:setup_highlight(target_info, character)
    end

    local self_ref = self
    target_info.Connections.CharacterAdded = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(char)
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Character = char
        target_info.CharacterObjects.HumanoidRootPart = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        self_ref:track_character_cache(target_info, char)
        self_ref:setup_highlight(target_info, char)
    end))

    target_info.Remove = function()
        for _, conn in pairs(target_info.Connections) do
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
        target_info.Connections = {}
        self_ref:disconnect_character_cache(target_info)
        for _, obj in pairs(objects) do
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
        target_info.Objects = {}
    end

    self.Targets[player] = target_info
end

function Wallhack:update_target(player, target_info, ctx)
    local objects = target_info.Objects
    local char_objs = target_info.CharacterObjects
    local character = char_objs.Character

    local target_holder = objects.TargetHolder
    local highlight = objects.Highlight

    target_holder.Visible = false
    if highlight then highlight.Enabled = false end
    local skeleton_holder_reset = objects.SkeletonHolder
    if skeleton_holder_reset then skeleton_holder_reset.Visible = false end

    local esp_st = self.ui.state.esp
    local chams_st = self.ui.state.chams
    local bars_st = self.ui.state.bars

    local esp_enabled = esp_st and esp_st.enabled
    local chams_enabled = chams_st and chams_st.enabled
    local bars_enabled = bars_st and bars_st.enabled

    if not (esp_enabled or chams_enabled or bars_enabled) then
        return
    end

    if not character or not character.Parent then
        return
    end
    if target_info.CachedCharacter ~= character then
        self:rebuild_character_cache(target_info, character)
    end

    local root_part = char_objs.HumanoidRootPart
    if not root_part or not root_part.Parent then
        char_objs.HumanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        return
    end

    local humanoid = char_objs.Humanoid
    if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
        char_objs.Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        return
    end

    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end

    if not ctx then return end
    local camera_pos
    local distance
    if ctx.is_preview ~= true then
        camera_pos = ctx.camera_position or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position)
        distance = camera_pos and (root_part.Position - camera_pos).Magnitude
    end

    local is_enemy_check = self.helpers.is_enemy or fallback_helpers.is_enemy
    local is_enemy_player = is_enemy_check(player, local_player)

    local force = ctx.is_preview == true
    local esp_show = esp_enabled and (force or not esp_st.enemy_only or is_enemy_player)
    local in_esp_distance = force or not distance or distance <= get_esp_distance_cap(self.ui.state)
    local in_chams_distance = force or not distance or distance <= get_feature_distance_cap(self.ui.state, chams_st)
    local in_bars_distance = force or not distance or distance <= get_feature_distance_cap(self.ui.state, bars_st)
    esp_show = esp_show and in_esp_distance
    local chams_show = chams_enabled and in_chams_distance and (force or not chams_st.enemy_only or is_enemy_player)
    local bars_show = bars_enabled and in_bars_distance and (force or not esp_st.enemy_only or is_enemy_player)

    -- Update Chams
    if chams_show and highlight then
        local outline_enabled = chams_st.outline == true
        local outline_color = self:read_color("chams", "outline_color", Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
        local outline_transparency = outline_enabled and (chams_st.outline_transparency / 100) or 1
        local target_visible = chams_st.visible == true and chams_st.invisible ~= true
        if chams_st.visible and chams_st.invisible then
            local now = ctx.now or os.clock()
            if (target_info.NextLineOfSightAt or 0) <= now then
                local camera_pos = ctx.camera_position or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position)
                local has_los_fn = self.helpers.has_line_of_sight or fallback_helpers.has_line_of_sight
                target_info.LineOfSight = has_los_fn(character, root_part, camera_pos, local_player.Character)
                target_info.NextLineOfSightAt = now + 0.12
            end
            target_visible = target_info.LineOfSight == true
        end

        if chams_st.visible and (target_visible or not chams_st.invisible) then
            highlight.Enabled = true
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
            highlight.Adornee = character
            highlight.FillColor = self:read_color("chams", "visible_color", Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 140))
            highlight.FillTransparency = (chams_st.visible_transparency or 50) / 100
            highlight.OutlineColor = outline_color
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = outline_transparency
        elseif chams_st.invisible then
            highlight.Enabled = true
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Adornee = character
            highlight.FillColor = self:read_color("chams", "fill_color", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
            highlight.FillTransparency = chams_st.fill_transparency / 100
            highlight.OutlineColor = outline_color
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = outline_transparency
        end
    end

    if not (esp_show or bars_show) then
        return
    end

    local calc_box_fn = self.helpers.calculate_box or fallback_helpers.calculate_box
    local box_width, box_height, box_pos_x, box_pos_y, on_screen = calc_box_fn(character, root_part, esp_st.dynamic_box, ctx.project, ctx.viewport_size, target_info.BoxParts)
    if not on_screen then
        return
    end

    if esp_show or bars_show then
        target_holder.Visible = true
        target_holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math_floor(box_pos_x), math_floor(box_pos_y))
        target_holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math_floor(box_width), math_floor(box_height))
    end

    -- Bounding Box drawing
    local box_type = esp_show and esp_st.box_type or "Disabled"
    local box_glow = objects.BoxGlow
    local box_outline_holder = objects.BoxOutlineHolder
    local box_inline_holder = objects.BoxInlineHolder
    local box_fill = objects.BoxFill
    local corner_holder = objects.CornerHolder

    box_glow.ImageTransparency = 1
    box_outline_holder.Visible = false
    box_inline_holder.Visible = false
    box_fill.Visible = false
    corner_holder.Visible = false
    for i = 1, 8 do
        objects["Line_" .. i].Visible = false
    end

    local skeleton_holder = objects.SkeletonHolder
    skeleton_holder.Visible = false
    for i = 1, SKELETON_BONE_COUNT do
        objects["Bone_" .. i].Visible = false
    end

    if esp_show and box_type ~= "Disabled" then
        local box_color = self:read_color("esp", "box_color", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
        local stroke_enabled = esp_st.stroke_enabled ~= false
        local stroke_thickness = esp_st.stroke_thickness
        local box_thickness = esp_st.box_thickness
        local round_corners = esp_st.round_corners
        local corner_radius = esp_st.corner_radius

        if esp_st.glow then
            box_glow.ImageTransparency = 0
            objects.BoxGlowGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(self:read_color("esp", "glow_color", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157)))
            objects.BoxGlowGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new((100 - (esp_st.glow_intensity or 35)) / 100)
        end

        if box_type == "Full Box" then
            box_outline_holder.Visible = true
            box_inline_holder.Visible = true

            box_outline_holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(box_width, box_height)
            box_inline_holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(box_width, box_height)

            local box_outline = objects.BoxOutline
            local box_inline = objects.BoxInline

            box_outline.Thickness = stroke_enabled and (box_thickness + stroke_thickness * 2) or 0
            box_inline.Thickness = box_thickness
            box_inline.Color = box_color

            local radius = round_corners and UDim.new(0, corner_radius) or UDim.new(0, 0)
            local join_mode = round_corners and Enum.LineJoinMode.Round or Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter

            objects.BoxOutlineCorner.CornerRadius = radius
            objects.BoxInlineCorner.CornerRadius = radius

            box_outline.LineJoinMode = join_mode
            box_inline.LineJoinMode = join_mode

            if esp_st.fill then
                box_fill.Visible = true
                box_fill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(box_width, box_height)
                objects.BoxFillCorner.CornerRadius = radius
                objects.BoxFillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(self:read_color("esp", "fill_color", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157)))
                objects.BoxFillGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.85)
            end
        elseif box_type == "Corners Only" then
            corner_holder.Visible = true
            corner_holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(box_width, box_height)

            local Layout = {
                {UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, box_thickness), Vector2.new(0, 0)},
                {UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0, box_thickness, 0.3, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0)},

                {UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, box_thickness), Vector2.new(1, 0)},
                {UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0, box_thickness, 0.3, 0), Vector2.new(1, 0)},

                {UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, box_thickness), Vector2.new(0, 1)},
                {UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, box_thickness, 0.3, 0), Vector2.new(0, 1)},

                {UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, box_thickness), Vector2.new(1, 1)},
                {UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, box_thickness, 0.3, 0), Vector2.new(1, 1)},
            }

            local line_radius = round_corners and UDim.new(1, 0) or UDim.new(0, 0)

            for i = 1, 8 do
                local line = objects["Line_" .. i]
                local stroke = objects["LineStroke_" .. i]
                local line_corner = objects["LineCorner_" .. i]
                local lay = Layout[i]

                line.Position = lay[1]
                line.Size = lay[2]
                line.AnchorPoint = lay[3]
                line.BackgroundColor3 = box_color
                line.Visible = true

                if line_corner then
                    line_corner.CornerRadius = line_radius
                end

                if stroke then
                    stroke.Thickness = stroke_enabled and stroke_thickness or 0
                    stroke.Color = COLOR_BLACK
                    stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
                end
            end
        end

        if esp_st.interior_glow then
            box_fill.Visible = true
            box_fill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(box_width, box_height)
            local radius = round_corners and UDim.new(0, corner_radius) or UDim.new(0, 0)
            objects.BoxFillCorner.CornerRadius = radius
            local igc1 = self:read_color("esp", "interior_glow_color_1", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
            local igc2 = self:read_color("esp", "interior_glow_color_2", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
            objects.BoxFillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(igc1, igc2)
            
            local intensity = esp_st.interior_glow_intensity or 50
            local factor = intensity / 100
            local t1 = 1 - (0.5 * factor)
            local t2 = 1 - (0.1 * factor)
            objects.BoxFillGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(t1, t2)
        end
    end

    -- Skeleton drawing
    if esp_show and esp_st.skeleton then
        skeleton_holder.Visible = true
        local skel_color = self:read_color("esp", "skeleton_color", COLOR_WHITE)
        local skel_thickness = esp_st.skeleton_thickness or 1
        local stroke_on = esp_st.stroke_enabled ~= false
        local bone_idx = 0
        local parts_by_name = target_info.PartsByName
        local resolve_joint_fn = self.helpers.resolve_joint or fallback_helpers.resolve_joint
        local get_skel_bones_fn = self.helpers.get_skeleton_bones or fallback_helpers.get_skeleton_bones
        for _, pair in ipairs(target_info.SkeletonBones or get_skel_bones_fn(character, parts_by_name)) do
            if bone_idx >= SKELETON_BONE_COUNT then break end
            local pa = resolve_joint_fn(character, pair[1], parts_by_name)
            local pb = resolve_joint_fn(character, pair[2], parts_by_name)
            if pa and pb then
                local ax, ay, a_on = ctx.project(pa)
                local bx, by, b_on = ctx.project(pb)
                if a_on and b_on then
                    bone_idx = bone_idx + 1
                    local bone = objects["Bone_" .. bone_idx]
                    local dx, dy = bx - ax, by - ay
                    local length = math_sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                    bone.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math_floor(length), skel_thickness)
                    bone.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math_floor((ax + bx) / 2), math_floor((ay + by) / 2))
                    bone.Rotation = math_deg(math_atan2(dy, dx))
                    bone.BackgroundColor3 = skel_color
                    bone.Visible = true
                    local bone_stroke = objects["BoneStroke_" .. bone_idx]
                    if bone_stroke then
                        bone_stroke.Thickness = stroke_on and 1 or 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Bars drawing
    local health_bar_outline = objects.HealthBarOutline
    local ammo_bar_outline = objects.AmmoBarOutline

    health_bar_outline.Visible = false
    ammo_bar_outline.Visible = false
    objects.BottomBarHolder.Visible = false

    if bars_show then
        local health_bar_enabled = bars_st.health_bar
        local font_size = esp_st and esp_st.font_size or 12

        if health_bar_enabled then
            local health = humanoid.Health
            local max_health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            local health_ratio = math_clamp(health / max_health, 0, 1)

            health_bar_outline.Visible = true
            objects.LeftBarHolder.Visible = true

            local radius_val = bars_st.health_bar_corner_radius or 2
            objects.HealthBarOutlineCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius_val)
            objects.HealthBarCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius_val)

            local health_stroke = objects.HealthBarOutlineStroke
            if health_stroke then
                health_stroke.Thickness = (bars_st.health_bar_stroke ~= false) and 1 or 0
            end

            local bar_thickness = bars_st.health_bar_thickness or 3
            health_bar_outline.Size = UDim2.new(0, bar_thickness, 1, 0)
            local bar_fill = objects.HealthBar
            bar_fill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, health_ratio, 0)
            bar_fill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
            bar_fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)

            local mode = bars_st.health_color_mode
            local c1 = self:read_color("bars", "health_color_1", Color3.fromRGB(131, 245, 78))
            local c2 = self:read_color("bars", "health_color_2", Color3.fromRGB(252, 71, 77))

            local grad = objects.HealthBarGradient
            if mode == "Static" then
                grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1)
            elseif mode == "Gradient" then
                grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2)
            elseif mode == "Reactive" then
                local c = c2:Lerp(c1, health_ratio)
                grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(c)
            end

            local health_text = objects.HealthBarText
            local show_text = (bars_st.health_number ~= false) and (health < max_health)
            if show_text then
                health_text.Text = tostring(math_floor(health))
                health_text.Visible = true
                health_text.TextSize = font_size
                health_text.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1 - health_ratio, -6)
                health_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            else
                health_text.Visible = false
            end
        end

        if bars_st.ammo_bar then
            local current_ammo, max_ammo = 0, 1
            local fighter_ctrl = get_fc()
            if fighter_ctrl then
                local fighter = fighter_ctrl:GetFighter(player)
                if fighter then
                    local item = fighter.EquippedItem
                    if item then
                        current_ammo = rawget(item.Data, "Ammo") or 0
                        max_ammo = rawget(item.Info, "MaxAmmo") or 1
                    end
                end
            end
            local ammo_ratio = math_clamp(current_ammo / math_max(max_ammo, 1), 0, 1)

            ammo_bar_outline.Visible = true
            objects.BottomBarHolder.Visible = true

            local radius_val = bars_st.health_bar_corner_radius or 2
            objects.AmmoBarOutlineCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            objects.AmmoBarCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            local bar_thickness = bars_st.health_bar_thickness or 3
            ammo_bar_outline.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, bar_thickness)
            local bar_fill = objects.AmmoBar
            bar_fill.Size = UDim2.new(ammo_ratio, 0, 1, 0)
            bar_fill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            bar_fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

            objects.AmmoBarGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(self:read_color("bars", "ammo_color", Color3.fromRGB(52, 131, 235)))

            local ammo_text = objects.AmmoBarText
            if max_ammo > 0 and current_ammo < max_ammo then
                ammo_text.Text = tostring(math_floor(current_ammo)) .. "/" .. tostring(math_floor(max_ammo))
                ammo_text.Visible = true
                ammo_text.TextSize = font_size
                ammo_text.Position = UDim2.new(ammo_ratio, 3, 0.5, 0)
                ammo_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            else
                ammo_text.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
end

function Wallhack:Init()
    local get_hui_fn = self.helpers.get_hui or fallback_helpers.get_hui
    local holder = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    holder.Name = "\n"
    holder.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets
    holder.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    holder.ResetOnSpawn = false
    holder.DisplayOrder = 10000
    holder.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    holder.Parent = get_hui_fn()
    self.Holder = holder

    local self_ref = self
    for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= local_player then
            self:create_target_gui(p)
        end
    end

    self.Connections.PlayerAdded = players.PlayerAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(p)
        if p ~= local_player then
            self_ref:create_target_gui(p)
        end
    end))
    self.Connections.PlayerRemoving = players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(p)
        local target = self_ref.Targets[p]
        if target then
            target.Remove()
            self_ref.Targets[p] = nil
        end
    end))

    local get_camera = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        return workspace.CurrentCamera
    end)

    local live_ctx
    live_ctx = {
        holder = self.Holder,
        is_preview = false,
        viewport_size = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        camera_position = nil,
        now = 0,
        _cam = nil,
        project = function(world_pos)
            local cam = live_ctx._cam
            if not cam then return 0, 0, false, 0 end
            local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world_pos)
            return sp.X, sp.Y, on, sp.Z
        end,
    }
    self.Connections.PreRender = run_service.PreRender:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local cam = get_camera()
        if not cam then return end
        live_ctx._cam = cam
        live_ctx.viewport_size = cam.ViewportSize
        live_ctx.camera_position = cam.CFrame.Position
        live_ctx.now = os.clock()
        for p, target in pairs(self_ref.Targets) do
            local success, err = pcall(function() self_ref:update_target(p, target, live_ctx) end)
            if not success then
                warn("update target failed for some random reason: ", err)
            end
        end
    end))
end

function Wallhack:Unload()
    for _, conn in pairs(self.Connections) do
        conn:Disconnect()
    end
    self.Connections = {}

    for p, target in pairs(self.Targets) do
        target.Remove()
    end
    self.Targets = {}

    if self.Holder then
        pcall(function() self.Holder:Destroy() end)
        self.Holder = nil
    end
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("render")
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    --// ui ctrls
    local esp_sec = tab:add_toggle("esp", "Player ESP", desc["esp"] or "Draws boxes around players")
    esp_sec:add_switch("enemy_only", "team check", true)
    esp_sec:add_slider("distance_cap", "distance cap", MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, " studs", DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
    esp_sec:add_dropdown("box_type", "box type", {"Full Box", "Corners Only", "Disabled"})
    esp_sec:add_switch("dynamic_box", "dynamic box", true)
    esp_sec:add_switch("round_corners", "rounded corners", false)
    esp_sec:add_switch("glow", "box glow", false)
    esp_sec:add_slider("glow_intensity", "box glow intensity", 0, 100, "%", 45)
    esp_sec:add_switch("interior_glow", "interior glow", false)
    local interior_glow_intensity_slider = esp_sec:add_slider("interior_glow_intensity", "int glow intensity", 0, 100, "%", 50)
    esp_sec:add_switch("fill", "box fill", false)
    esp_sec:add_switch("stroke_enabled", "box stroke", false)
    esp_sec:add_slider("stroke_thickness", "stroke thickness", 1, 5, "px", 1)
    esp_sec:add_slider("box_thickness", "box thickness", 1, 5, "px", 1)
    esp_sec:add_slider("corner_radius", "corner radius", 1, 20, "px", 8)
    esp_sec:add_slider("font_size", "font size", 8, 24, "px", 12)
    esp_sec:add_switch("skeleton", "skeleton", false)
    esp_sec:add_slider("skeleton_thickness", "skeleton thickness", 1, 5, "px", 1)

    if type(lib.add_bullet_tracer_controls) == "function" then
        lib.add_bullet_tracer_controls(tab)
    end

    --// chams stuff
    local chams_sec = tab:add_toggle("chams", "Player Chams", desc["chams"] or "Highlights players through walls")
    chams_sec:add_switch("enemy_only", "team check", true)
    chams_sec:add_slider("distance_cap", "distance cap", MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, " studs", DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
    chams_sec:add_switch("invisible", "invisible chams", true)
    local cham_fill_color_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(chams_sec, "fill_color", "invisible colour", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local cham_fill_trans_slider = chams_sec:add_slider("fill_transparency", "invisible transparency", 0, 100, "%", 50)
    chams_sec:add_switch("visible", "visible chams", false)
    local cham_visible_color_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(chams_sec, "visible_color", "visible colour", Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 140))
    local cham_visible_trans_slider = chams_sec:add_slider("visible_transparency", "visible transparency", 0, 100, "%", 50)
    chams_sec:add_switch("outline", "render outline", true)
    local cham_outline_color_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(chams_sec, "outline_color", "outline colour", Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
    local cham_outline_trans_slider = chams_sec:add_slider("outline_transparency", "outline transparency", 0, 100, "%", 50)

    --// health bar stuff
    local bars_sec = tab:add_toggle("bars", "Player Bars", desc["bars"] or "Shows player health bars")
    bars_sec:add_slider("distance_cap", "distance cap", MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, " studs", DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
    bars_sec:add_switch("health_bar", "health bar", true)
    bars_sec:add_switch("health_bar_stroke", "health bar stroke", true)
    bars_sec:add_slider("health_bar_corner_radius", "corner radius", 1, 10, "px", 2)
    bars_sec:add_slider("health_bar_thickness", "thickness", 1, 10, "px", 3)
    bars_sec:add_switch("health_number", "health number", true)
    bars_sec:add_switch("ammo_bar", "ammo bar", true)
    local ammo_color_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(bars_sec, "ammo_color", "ammo colour", Color3.fromRGB(52, 131, 235))
    bars_sec:add_dropdown("health_color_mode", "colour mode", {"Static", "Gradient", "Reactive"})
    local health_color_1_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(bars_sec, "health_color_1", "Health Color 1", Color3.fromRGB(131, 245, 78))
    local health_color_2_opt = fallback_helpers.add_color_option(bars_sec, "health_color_2", "Health Color 2", Color3.fromRGB(252, 71, 77))

    if shared.daydreamer_rivals_wallhack_cleanup then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_wallhack_cleanup)
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_wallhack_cleanup = nil
    end

    local wallhack = Wallhack.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.wallhack_instance = wallhack

    local box_color_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "box_color", "box colour", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local glow_color_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "glow_color", "glow colour", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local interior_glow_color_1_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "interior_glow_color_1", "int glow colour 1", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local interior_glow_color_2_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "interior_glow_color_2", "int glow colour 2", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local fill_color_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "fill_color", "fill colour", Color3.fromRGB(216, 126, 157))
    local skeleton_color_opt = wallhack:add_color_option(esp_sec, "skeleton_color", "skeleton colour", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))

    local function set_option_visible(option, visible)
        if option and option.set_visible then
            option:set_visible(visible)
        end
    end

    local function update_glow_color_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(glow_color_opt, val == true)
    end
    local function update_interior_glow_color_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(interior_glow_color_1_opt, val == true)
        set_option_visible(interior_glow_color_2_opt, val == true)
        if interior_glow_intensity_slider and interior_glow_intensity_slider.set_visible then
            interior_glow_intensity_slider:set_visible(val == true)
        end
    end
    local function update_fill_color_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(fill_color_opt, val == true)
    end
    local function update_skeleton_color_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(skeleton_color_opt, val == true)
    end

    ui:on_change("esp.glow", update_glow_color_vis)
    ui:on_change("esp.interior_glow", update_interior_glow_color_vis)
    ui:on_change("esp.fill", update_fill_color_vis)
    ui:on_change("esp.skeleton", update_skeleton_color_vis)

    local function update_chams_invisible_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(cham_fill_color_opt, val == true)
        if cham_fill_trans_slider and cham_fill_trans_slider.set_visible then
            cham_fill_trans_slider:set_visible(val == true)
        end
    end
    ui:on_change("chams.invisible", update_chams_invisible_vis)

    local function update_chams_visible_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(cham_visible_color_opt, val == true)
        if cham_visible_trans_slider and cham_visible_trans_slider.set_visible then
            cham_visible_trans_slider:set_visible(val == true)
        end
    end
    ui:on_change("chams.visible", update_chams_visible_vis)

    local function update_chams_outline_vis(val)
        set_option_visible(cham_outline_color_opt, val == true)
        if cham_outline_trans_slider and cham_outline_trans_slider.set_visible then
            cham_outline_trans_slider:set_visible(val == true)
        end
    end
    ui:on_change("chams.outline", update_chams_outline_vis)

    local function update_health_color_mode_vis(val)
        if val == "Static" then
            set_option_visible(health_color_1_opt, true)
            set_option_visible(health_color_2_opt, false)
        else
            set_option_visible(health_color_1_opt, true)
            set_option_visible(health_color_2_opt, true)
        end
    end
    ui:on_change("bars.health_color_mode", update_health_color_mode_vis)

    task.spawn(function()
        update_glow_color_vis(ui.state.esp.glow)
        update_interior_glow_color_vis(ui.state.esp.interior_glow)
        update_fill_color_vis(ui.state.esp.fill)
        update_skeleton_color_vis(ui.state.esp.skeleton)
        update_chams_invisible_vis(ui.state.chams.invisible)
        update_chams_visible_vis(ui.state.chams.visible)
        update_chams_outline_vis(ui.state.chams.outline)
        update_health_color_mode_vis(ui.state.bars.health_color_mode)
    end)

    --// expose a preview renderer
    ui.rivals_esp_renderers = ui.rivals_esp_renderers or {}
    table.insert(ui.rivals_esp_renderers, {
        id = "wallhack",
        create = function(ctx)
            local inst = dreamer.wallhack_instance
            if inst then
                return {
                    Objects = inst:build_box_objects(ctx.holder),
                    CharacterObjects = {},
                    Connections = {},
                    Remove = function() end,
                }
            end
            return {}
        end,
        update = function(player, target, ctx, dt)
            local inst = dreamer.wallhack_instance
            if inst then
                inst:update_target(player, target, ctx, dt)
            end
        end,
    })

    wallhack:Init()

    shared.daydreamer_rivals_wallhack_cleanup = function()
        wallhack:Unload()
    end

    return tab
end

end)

-- ─── tab: nametags ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_nametags', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(fn) return fn end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(fn) return fn end
end

local cloneref = cloneref or function(o) return o end
local players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local run_service = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local workspace = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
local local_player = players.LocalPlayer
local font_google_sans        = Font.new("rbxassetid://16658221428", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
local font_google_sans_medium = Font.new("rbxassetid://16658221428", Enum.FontWeight.Medium,  Enum.FontStyle.Normal)

local math_min, math_max, math_floor, math_clamp, math_exp = math.min, math.max, math.floor, math.clamp, math.exp
local DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 800
local MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 250
local MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP = 2000

local function get_esp_distance_cap(state)
    local esp = state and state.esp
    return math_clamp(tonumber(esp and esp.distance_cap) or DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
end

local function get_nametag_distance_cap(state)
    local nametags = state and state.nametags
    return math_clamp(
        tonumber(nametags and nametags.distance_cap) or get_esp_distance_cap(state),
        MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP,
        MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP
    )
end

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn) end
    for i = #conns, 1, -1 do conns[i] = nil end
end
function fallback_helpers.get_hui()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local success, result = pcall(gethui)
        if success and result then return result end
    end
    local core_gui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local roblox_gui = core_gui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
    if roblox_gui then return roblox_gui end
    return core_gui
end
function fallback_helpers.is_enemy(player, local_plr)
    if not player or player == local_plr then return false end
    if player.Team ~= nil and player.Team == local_plr.Team then return false end
    if player:GetAttribute("TeamID") ~= nil and player:GetAttribute("TeamID") == local_plr:GetAttribute("TeamID") then return false end
    return true
end
function fallback_helpers.estimate_text_width(text, font_size)
    return math.floor(#text * font_size * 0.55)
end
function fallback_helpers.color_to_rgb_string(color)
    return string.format("%d,%d,%d", math.floor(color.R * 255), math.floor(color.G * 255), math.floor(color.B * 255))
end

local fallback_weapon_cache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function fallback_helpers.get_equipped_weapon_name(player, workspace_ref)
    if not player then return "none" end
    local now = os.clock()
    local cached = fallback_weapon_cache[player]
    if cached and now - cached.t < 0.15 then return cached.name end

    local function finish(name)
        fallback_weapon_cache[player] = { t = now, name = name }
        return name
    end

    local ws = workspace_ref or workspace
    local viewModels = ws:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not viewModels then return finish("none") end
    local playerName = player.Name

    local function weaponFromModelName(modelName)
        local dash = modelName:find("-", 1, true)
        if not dash then return nil end
        local owner = modelName:sub(1, dash - 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if owner == playerName then
            return modelName:sub(dash + 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        end
        return nil
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(viewModels:GetChildren()) do
        local w = weaponFromModelName(child.Name)
        if w then return finish(w) end
    end

    local fp = viewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    if fp then
        for _, child in ipairs(fp:GetChildren()) do
            local w = weaponFromModelName(child.Name)
            if w then return finish(w) end
        end
    end

    return finish("none")
end

function fallback_helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if type(section.add_color_picker) == "function" then
        return section:add_color_picker(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    if type(section.add_color) == "function" then
        return section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    default_color = typeof(default_color) == "Color3" and default_color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_r", text .. " r", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.R * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_g", text .. " g", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.G * 255 + 0.5))
    section:add_slider(sub_id .. "_b", text .. " b", 0, 255, "", math.floor(default_color.B * 255 + 0.5))
    return nil
end

local NametagESP = {}
NametagESP.__index = NametagESP

function NametagESP.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        Targets = {},
        Connections = {},
        Holder = nil,
    }, NametagESP)
end

function NametagESP:read_color(key, default)
    local color = self.helpers.read_color and self.helpers.read_color(self.ui, "nametags", key)
    if color then return color end
    local state = self.ui.state.nametags
    if state then
        if typeof(state[key]) == "Color3" then return state[key] end
        if state[key .. "_r"] ~= nil or state[key .. "_g"] ~= nil or state[key .. "_b"] ~= nil then
            return Color3.fromRGB(
                math.clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_r"]) or (default.R * 255), 0, 255),
                math.clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_g"]) or (default.G * 255), 0, 255),
                math.clamp(tonumber(state[key .. "_b"]) or (default.B * 255), 0, 255)
            )
        end
    end
    return default
end

function NametagESP:add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if self.helpers.add_color_option then
        return self.helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    return fallback_helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
end

function NametagESP:build_nametag_objects(holder)
    local objects = {}
    local parent_gui = holder

    --// pillbox
    local bubble_frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    bubble_frame.Visible = false
    bubble_frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    bubble_frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
    bubble_frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bubble_frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 30)
    bubble_frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    bubble_frame.ClipsDescendants = false
    bubble_frame.Parent = parent_gui
    objects.BubbleFrame = bubble_frame

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9999)
    corner.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.Corner = corner

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingLeft  = UDim.new(0, 5)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    padding.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.Padding = padding

    local list_layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    list_layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    list_layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    list_layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    list_layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    list_layout.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.ListLayout = list_layout

    local name_label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    name_label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    name_label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    name_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    name_label.RichText = true
    name_label.FontFace = font_google_sans_medium
    name_label.ZIndex = 2
    name_label.LayoutOrder = 1
    name_label.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.NameLabel = name_label

    local div1 = Instance.new("Frame")
    div1.BorderSizePixel = 0
    div1.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 10)
    div1.ZIndex = 2
    div1.LayoutOrder = 2
    div1.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.Divider1 = div1

    local health_label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    health_label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    health_label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    health_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    health_label.RichText = true
    health_label.FontFace = font_google_sans
    health_label.ZIndex = 2
    health_label.LayoutOrder = 3
    health_label.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.HealthLabel = health_label

    local div2 = Instance.new("Frame")
    div2.BorderSizePixel = 0
    div2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 10)
    div2.ZIndex = 2
    div2.LayoutOrder = 4
    div2.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.Divider2 = div2

    local distance_label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    distance_label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    distance_label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    distance_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    distance_label.RichText = true
    distance_label.FontFace = font_google_sans
    distance_label.ZIndex = 2
    distance_label.LayoutOrder = 5
    distance_label.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.DistanceLabel = distance_label

    local equipped_container = Instance.new("Frame")
    equipped_container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    equipped_container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    equipped_container.ClipsDescendants = true
    equipped_container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    equipped_container.LayoutOrder = 6
    equipped_container.Parent = bubble_frame
    objects.EquippedContainer = equipped_container

    local eq_layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    eq_layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    eq_layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    eq_layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    eq_layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    eq_layout.Parent = equipped_container

    local div3 = Instance.new("Frame")
    div3.BorderSizePixel = 0
    div3.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 10)
    div3.ZIndex = 2
    div3.LayoutOrder = 1
    div3.Parent = equipped_container
    objects.Divider3 = div3

    local equipped_label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    equipped_label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    equipped_label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    equipped_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    equipped_label.RichText = true
    equipped_label.FontFace = font_google_sans
    equipped_label.ZIndex = 2
    equipped_label.LayoutOrder = 2
    equipped_label.Parent = equipped_container
    objects.EquippedLabel = equipped_label

    return objects
end

function NametagESP:create_nametag(player)
    local target_info = {
        Objects            = {},
        CharacterObjects   = {},
        Connections        = {},
        EquippedTransition = 0,
        DisplayedHealth    = 150,
        DisplayedDistance  = 0,
        CachedName         = player.Name,
        CachedDisplayName  = player.DisplayName,
    }

    local character = player.Character
    target_info.CharacterObjects.Character = character
    if character then
        target_info.CharacterObjects.HumanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    end

    target_info.Connections.CharacterAdded = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(char)
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Character = char
        target_info.CharacterObjects.HumanoidRootPart = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        target_info.CharacterObjects.Head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 10)
        target_info.DisplayedHealth = 150
    end))

    local objects = self:build_nametag_objects(self.Holder)
    target_info.Objects = objects

    local self_ref = self
    target_info.Remove = function()
        for _, conn in pairs(target_info.Connections) do
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
        target_info.Connections = {}
        for _, obj in pairs(target_info.Objects) do
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
        target_info.Objects = {}
    end

    self.Targets[player] = target_info
end

function NametagESP:update_target(player, target_info, ctx, dt)
    local objects = target_info.Objects
    local char_objs = target_info.CharacterObjects
    local character = char_objs.Character

    local bubble_frame = objects.BubbleFrame
    if not bubble_frame then return end

    bubble_frame.Visible = false

    local state = self.ui.state.nametags
    local enabled = state and state.enabled
    if not enabled then return end

    if not character or not character.Parent then return end

    local root_part = char_objs.HumanoidRootPart
    if not root_part or not root_part.Parent then
        char_objs.HumanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        return
    end

    local humanoid = char_objs.Humanoid
    if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
        char_objs.Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        return
    end

    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

    if not ctx then return end

    -- Team check
    local is_enemy_check = self.helpers.is_enemy or fallback_helpers.is_enemy
    if not (ctx and ctx.is_preview) and state.enemy_only and not is_enemy_check(player, local_player) then
        return
    end

    local head = char_objs.Head
    if not head or not head.Parent then
        char_objs.Head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        head = char_objs.Head
    end
    local head_pos = head and head.Position or root_part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

    local vertical_offset = state.vertical_offset or 3
    local target_pos = head_pos + Vector3.new(0, vertical_offset, 0)

    if ctx.is_preview ~= true then
        local camera_pos = ctx.camera_position or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position)
        if camera_pos and (target_pos - camera_pos).Magnitude > get_nametag_distance_cap(self.ui.state) then
            return
        end
    end

    local screen_x, screen_y, on_screen = ctx.project(target_pos)
    if not on_screen then return end

    bubble_frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(screen_x), math.floor(screen_y))

    --// smooth ticking
    local lerp_fast = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-10 * dt), 0, 1)
    local lerp_slow = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-6  * dt), 0, 1)

    local actual_distance = ctx.distance_override
        or (target_pos - (ctx.camera_position or workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position)).Magnitude
    target_info.DisplayedDistance = target_info.DisplayedDistance +
        (actual_distance - target_info.DisplayedDistance) * lerp_slow

    local actual_health = humanoid.Health
    target_info.DisplayedHealth = target_info.DisplayedHealth +
        (actual_health - target_info.DisplayedHealth) * lerp_fast

    local distance = target_info.DisplayedDistance

    --// colours
    local name_color     = self:read_color("name_color",     Color3.fromRGB(30,  30,  30))
    local dist_color     = self:read_color("dist_color",     Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    local equipped_color = self:read_color("equipped_color", Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120))

    local to_rgb_string = self.helpers.color_to_rgb_string or fallback_helpers.color_to_rgb_string
    local name_rgb = to_rgb_string(name_color)
    local dist_rgb = to_rgb_string(dist_color)
    local equipped_rgb = to_rgb_string(equipped_color)

    --// size font based on distance
    local base_font_size = state.font_size or 13
    local final_font_size = base_font_size
    if state.scale_with_distance then
        local scale_factor = math.clamp(35 / math.max(1, distance), 0.6, 1.2)
        final_font_size = math.floor(base_font_size * scale_factor)
    end
    local name_label = objects.NameLabel
    local display_name = state.use_display_name and target_info.CachedDisplayName or target_info.CachedName
    name_label.Text = string.format("<font color=\"rgb(%s)\">%s</font>", name_rgb, display_name)
    name_label.TextSize = final_font_size
    local est_width = self.helpers.estimate_text_width or fallback_helpers.estimate_text_width
    local name_w = est_width(display_name, final_font_size)
    name_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(name_w, final_font_size + 2)

    local health_label = objects.HealthLabel
    local show_health = state.show_health
    local health_w = 0
    if show_health then
        local health = target_info.DisplayedHealth
        local hp_ratio   = math.clamp(health / 150, 0, 1)
        local col_low    = self:read_color("health_low_color",  Color3.fromRGB(220, 60,  60))
        local col_mid    = self:read_color("health_mid_color",  Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 40))
        local col_high   = self:read_color("health_high_color", Color3.fromRGB(80,  200, 80))
        local health_color
        if hp_ratio <= 0.5 then
            health_color = col_low:Lerp(col_mid, hp_ratio * 2)
        else
            health_color = col_mid:Lerp(col_high, (hp_ratio - 0.5) * 2)
        end
        local health_rgb = to_rgb_string(health_color)

        if state.show_decimal then
            local int_part = math.floor(health)
            local dec_part = math.floor((health - int_part) * 100)

            health_label.Text = string.format("<font color=\"rgb(%s)\">%d<font size=\"%d\">.%02d</font></font>",
                health_rgb,
                int_part,
                math.max(6, math_floor(final_font_size * 0.75)),
                dec_part
            )
            health_w = est_width("000.00", final_font_size)
        else
            health_label.Text = string.format("<font color=\"rgb(%s)\">%d</font>",
                health_rgb,
                math_floor(health)
            )
            health_w = est_width("000", final_font_size)
        end
        health_label.TextSize = final_font_size
        health_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(health_w, final_font_size + 2)
        health_label.Visible = true
        objects.Divider1.Visible = true
    else
        health_label.Visible = false
        objects.Divider1.Visible = false
    end

    local distance_label = objects.DistanceLabel
    local show_distance = state.show_distance
    local dist_w = 0
    if show_distance then
        distance_label.Text = string.format("<font color=\"rgb(%s)\">%dm</font>",
            dist_rgb,
            math_floor(distance)
        )
        distance_label.TextSize = final_font_size
        dist_w = est_width("000m", final_font_size)
        distance_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(dist_w, final_font_size + 2)
        distance_label.Visible = true
        objects.Divider2.Visible = true
    else
        distance_label.Visible = false
        objects.Divider2.Visible = false
    end

    local equipped_container = objects.EquippedContainer
    local equipped_label = objects.EquippedLabel

    local equipped_weapon_name = ctx.weapon_override
    if equipped_weapon_name == nil then
        local get_wep_name = self.helpers.get_equipped_weapon_name or fallback_helpers.get_equipped_weapon_name
        equipped_weapon_name = get_wep_name(player, workspace)
    end
    local has_weapon = equipped_weapon_name ~= nil and equipped_weapon_name ~= ""
    local is_equipped_enabled = state.show_equipped and has_weapon
    local target_trans = is_equipped_enabled and 1 or 0

    local lerp_speed = 15
    local step = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-lerp_speed * dt), 0, 1)
    target_info.EquippedTransition = target_info.EquippedTransition + (target_trans - target_info.EquippedTransition) * step

    local eq_container_width = 0
    if target_info.EquippedTransition > 0.005 then
        equipped_label.Text = string.format("<font color=\"rgb(%s)\">%s</font>",
            equipped_rgb,
            tostring(equipped_weapon_name)
        )
        equipped_label.TextSize = final_font_size

        local eq_text_w = est_width(tostring(equipped_weapon_name), final_font_size)
        equipped_label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(eq_text_w, final_font_size + 2)

        local full_container_width = 13 + eq_text_w
        eq_container_width = math_floor(full_container_width * target_info.EquippedTransition)

        equipped_container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(eq_container_width, final_font_size + 4)
        equipped_container.Visible = true

        equipped_label.TextTransparency = 1 - target_info.EquippedTransition
        objects.Divider3.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - target_info.EquippedTransition
    else
        equipped_container.Visible = false
        equipped_container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    end
    local divider_color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
    local div_h = math.max(6, final_font_size - 4)

    local divs = {objects.Divider1, objects.Divider2, objects.Divider3}
    for _, div in ipairs(divs) do
        div.BackgroundColor3 = divider_color
        div.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, div_h)
    end
    local padding_left  = 5
    local padding_right = 5
    local item_gap = 6

    local total_width = padding_left + name_w
    if show_health then
        total_width = total_width + item_gap + 1 + item_gap + health_w
    end
    if show_distance then
        total_width = total_width + item_gap + 1 + item_gap + dist_w
    end
    if target_info.EquippedTransition > 0.005 then
        total_width = total_width + item_gap + eq_container_width
    end
    total_width = total_width + padding_right
    local vert_size = state.vertical_size or 6
    local total_height = vert_size * 2 + final_font_size + 2
    bubble_frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math_floor(total_width), math_floor(total_height))
    bubble_frame.Visible = true
    local padding_obj = objects.Padding
    padding_obj.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, vert_size)
    padding_obj.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, vert_size)
    local bg_color = self:read_color("bg_color", Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240))
    local bg_trans = (state.bg_transparency or 15) / 100
    bubble_frame.BackgroundColor3 = bg_color
    bubble_frame.BackgroundTransparency = bg_trans
end

function NametagESP:Init()
    local get_hui_fn = self.helpers.get_hui or fallback_helpers.get_hui
    local holder = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    holder.Name = "\n"
    holder.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets
    holder.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    holder.ResetOnSpawn = false
    holder.DisplayOrder = 10001
    holder.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    holder.Parent = get_hui_fn()
    self.Holder = holder

    local self_ref = self
    for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= local_player then
            self:create_nametag(p)
        end
    end

    self.Connections.PlayerAdded = players.PlayerAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(p)
        if p ~= local_player then
            self_ref:create_nametag(p)
        end
    end))

    self.Connections.PlayerRemoving = players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(LPH_JIT(function(p)
        local target = self_ref.Targets[p]
        if target then
            target.Remove()
            self_ref.Targets[p] = nil
        end
    end))

    local get_camera = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        return workspace.CurrentCamera
    end)

    local live_ctx
    live_ctx = {
        holder = self.Holder,
        is_preview = false,
