        distance_override = nil,
        weapon_override = nil,
        camera_position = nil,
        _cam = nil,
        project = function(world_pos)
            local cam = live_ctx._cam
            if not cam then return 0, 0, false, 0 end
            local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world_pos)
            return sp.X, sp.Y, on, sp.Z
        end,
    }
    self.Connections.PreRender = run_service.PreRender:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
        local cam = get_camera()
        if not cam then return end
        live_ctx._cam = cam
        live_ctx.camera_position = cam.CFrame.Position
        for p, target in pairs(self_ref.Targets) do
            local success, err = pcall(function() self_ref:update_target(p, target, live_ctx, dt) end)
            if not success then
                warn(err)
            end
        end
    end))
end

function NametagESP:Unload()
    for _, conn in pairs(self.Connections) do
        conn:Disconnect()
    end
    self.Connections = {}

    for p, target in pairs(self.Targets) do
        target.Remove()
    end
    self.Targets = {}

    if self.Holder then
        pcall(function() self.Holder:Destroy() end)
        self.Holder = nil
    end
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("render")
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    --// UI Configuration Section
    local sec = tab:add_toggle("nametags", "Player Nametags", desc["nametags"] or "Draws pillbox tags above players")
    sec:add_switch("enemy_only", "team check", true)
    sec:add_slider("distance_cap", "distance cap", MIN_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, MAX_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP, " studs", DEFAULT_ESP_DISTANCE_CAP)
    sec:add_switch("show_health", "show health", true)
    sec:add_switch("show_decimal", "show health decimal", true)
    sec:add_switch("show_distance", "show distance", true)
    sec:add_switch("show_equipped", "show equipped item", true)
    sec:add_switch("scale_with_distance", "scale with distance", true)
    sec:add_switch("use_display_name", "use display name", false)

    -- re-injection guard
    if shared.daydreamer_rivals_nametags_cleanup then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_nametags_cleanup)
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_nametags_cleanup = nil
    end

    local nametags = NametagESP.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.nametags_instance = nametags

    local bg_color_opt = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "bg_color", "bg colour", Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240))
    local name_color_opt = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "name_color", "name colour", Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30))
    local health_low_color_opt  = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "health_low_color",  "health colour (low)",  Color3.fromRGB(220, 60,  60))
    local health_mid_color_opt  = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "health_mid_color",  "health colour (mid)",  Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 40))
    local health_high_color_opt = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "health_high_color", "health colour (full)", Color3.fromRGB(80,  200, 80))
    local dist_color_opt     = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "dist_color",     "distance colour",     Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
    local equipped_color_opt = nametags:add_color_option(sec, "equipped_color", "equipped item colour", Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120))

    sec:add_slider("bg_transparency", "bg transparency", 0, 100, "%", 15)
    sec:add_slider("font_size", "font size", 8, 24, "px", 13)
    sec:add_slider("vertical_size", "vertical padding", 2, 20, "px", 6)
    sec:add_slider("vertical_offset", "height offset", 0, 10, " studs", 3)

    ui.rivals_esp_renderers = ui.rivals_esp_renderers or {}
    table.insert(ui.rivals_esp_renderers, {
        id = "nametags",
        create = function(ctx)
            local inst = dreamer.nametags_instance
            if inst then
                return {
                    Objects = inst:build_nametag_objects(ctx.holder),
                    CharacterObjects = {},
                    Connections = {},
                    EquippedTransition = 0,
                    DisplayedHealth = 150,
                    DisplayedDistance = 0,
                    CachedName = "heartskyoto",
                    CachedDisplayName = "heartskyoto",
                    Remove = function() end,
                }
            end
            return {}
        end,
        update = function(player, target, ctx, dt)
            local inst = dreamer.nametags_instance
            if inst then
                inst:update_target(player, target, ctx, dt)
            end
        end,
    })

    nametags:Init()

    shared.daydreamer_rivals_nametags_cleanup = function()
        nametags:Unload()
    end

    return tab
end

end)

-- ─── tab: worldoptions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_worldoptions', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(f) return f end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(f) return f end
end

local Lighting = cloneref(game:GetService("Lighting"))
local RunService = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local workspace_svc = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))

local dreamer = ((getgenv and getgenv()) or _G).dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    ((getgenv and getgenv()) or _G).dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn) end
    for i = #conns, 1, -1 do conns[i] = nil end
end
function fallback_helpers.parse_color(str)
    if typeof and typeof(str) == "Color3" then return str end
    if type(str) ~= "string" then return nil end
    return nil
end
function fallback_helpers.asset_id() return nil end
function fallback_helpers.read_color() return nil end
function fallback_helpers.own_effect() return nil end
function fallback_helpers.destroy_named() end
function fallback_helpers.snapshot() end
function fallback_helpers.restore() end
function fallback_helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    if type(section.add_color) == "function" then
        return section:add_color(sub_id, text, default_color)
    end
    return nil
end
function fallback_helpers.skybox_presets() return {} end
function fallback_helpers.skybox_preset_list()
    return { "default", "custom" }
end

local Worldoptions = {}
Worldoptions.__index = Worldoptions

function Worldoptions.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    local presets = helpers.skybox_presets and helpers.skybox_presets() or {}
    local preset_list = helpers.skybox_preset_list and helpers.skybox_preset_list() or { "default", "custom" }
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        -- per-effect state lives here so build() can wire on_change without globals
        skyboxes = presets,
        skybox_names = preset_list,
        conns = {},
        cleanups = {},
        motion_blur = {
            conn = nil,
            fx = nil,
            previous_look_vector = nil,
        },
        fov = {
            conn = nil,
            saved_fov = nil,
        },
        sky = {
            saved = {},
            snapped_instance = nil,
            loop_active = false,
            created_instance = nil,
        },
        atmosphere = {
            saved = {},
            created = false,
        },
        lighting = {
            saved = {},
            snapped = false,
        },
    }, Worldoptions)
end

function Worldoptions:safe_set(obj, prop, value)
    self.helpers.safe_set(self.ui, obj, prop, value)
end

function Worldoptions:state(id, key)
    local sec = self.ui.state and self.ui.state[id]
    return sec and sec[key]
end

function Worldoptions:is_on(id)
    local sec = self.ui.state and self.ui.state[id]
    return sec and sec.enabled == true
end

function Worldoptions:read_color(id, key)
    return self.helpers.read_color(self.ui, id, key)
end

function Worldoptions:add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
    return self.helpers.add_color_option(section, sub_id, text, default_color)
end

function Worldoptions:own_effect(class, name)
    return self.helpers.own_effect(Lighting, class, name)
end

function Worldoptions:destroy_named(name)
    return self.helpers.destroy_named(Lighting, name)
end

function Worldoptions:track(conn)
    return self.helpers.track(self, conn)
end

function Worldoptions:register_cleanup(toggle_id, apply_fn)
    local self_ref = self
    self.cleanups[#self.cleanups + 1] = function()
        local sec = self_ref.ui.state and self_ref.ui.state[toggle_id]
        if sec then sec.enabled = false end
        pcall(apply_fn)
    end
end

--// colour correction
function Worldoptions:build_color_correction()
    local ui = self.ui
    local CC_NAME = "daydreamer_color_correction"
    local cc = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_colorcorrection", "Colour Correction", "saturation / contrast / brightness / tint")

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_colorcorrection") then
            self:destroy_named(CC_NAME)
            return
        end
        local fx = self:own_effect("ColorCorrectionEffect", CC_NAME)
        if not fx then return end

        local saturation = self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "use_saturation") ~= false and (self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "saturation") or 0) / 100 or 0
        self:safe_set(fx, "Saturation", saturation)

        local contrast = self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "use_contrast") ~= false and (self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "contrast") or 0) / 100 or 0
        self:safe_set(fx, "Contrast", contrast)

        local brightness = self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "use_brightness") ~= false and (self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "brightness") or 0) / 100 or 0
        self:safe_set(fx, "Brightness", brightness)

        local tint = self:read_color("wc_colorcorrection", "tint")
        if tint then
            self:safe_set(fx, "TintColor", self:state("wc_colorcorrection", "use_tint") ~= false and tint or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
        end
    end

    cc:add_switch("use_saturation", "enable saturation", true)
    cc:add_slider("saturation", "saturation", -100, 100, "%", 0)
    cc:add_switch("use_contrast", "enable contrast", true)
    cc:add_slider("contrast", "contrast", -100, 100, "%", 0)
    cc:add_switch("use_brightness", "enable brightness", true)
    cc:add_slider("brightness", "brightness", -100, 100, "%", 0)
    cc:add_switch("use_tint", "enable tint", true)
    self:add_color_option(cc, "tint", "tint color", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))

    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.use_saturation", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.use_contrast", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.use_brightness", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.use_tint", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.saturation", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.contrast", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.brightness", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_colorcorrection.tint", apply)
end

--// atmosphere
function Worldoptions:build_atmosphere()
    local ui = self.ui
    local ATMO_PROPS = { "Density", "Offset", "Haze", "Glare", "Color", "Decay" }
    local saved = self.atmosphere.saved

    local atmo = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_atmosphere", "Atmosphere", "control how atmosphere behaves")

    local function get_atmosphere(create)
        local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if existing then return existing end
        if create then
            local ok, made = pcall(Instance.new, "Atmosphere")
            if ok and made then
                made.Name = "daydreamer_atmosphere"
                made.Parent = Lighting
                self.atmosphere.created = true
                return made
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_atmosphere") then
            local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if self.atmosphere.created and existing and existing.Name == "daydreamer_atmosphere" then
                pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end)
                self.atmosphere.created = false
            elseif existing then
                if self:is_on("wc_skybox") then
                    --// dont remove this restore otherwise for some stupid reason it'll break if skies change
                    self.helpers.restore(saved, existing, ATMO_PROPS)
                    self:safe_set(existing, "Density", 0)
                    self:safe_set(existing, "Haze", 0)
                else
                    self.helpers.restore(saved, existing, ATMO_PROPS)
                end
            end
            if not self:is_on("wc_skybox") then
                for k in pairs(saved) do saved[k] = nil end
            end
            return
        end

        local obj = get_atmosphere(true)
        if not obj then return end
        if not self.atmosphere.created then
            self.helpers.snapshot(saved, obj, ATMO_PROPS)
        end

        local density_val = self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_density") ~= false and (self:state("wc_atmosphere", "density") or 0) / 100 or saved["Density"]
        if density_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Density", density_val) end

        local offset_val = self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_offset") ~= false and (self:state("wc_atmosphere", "offset") or 0) / 100 or saved["Offset"]
        if offset_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Offset", offset_val) end

        local haze_val = self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_haze") ~= false and (self:state("wc_atmosphere", "haze") or 0) or saved["Haze"]
        if haze_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Haze", haze_val) end

        local glare_val = self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_glare") ~= false and (self:state("wc_atmosphere", "glare") or 0) or saved["Glare"]
        if glare_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Glare", glare_val) end

        if self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_color") ~= false then
            local color = self:read_color("wc_atmosphere", "color")
            if color then self:safe_set(obj, "Color", color) end
        else
            if saved["Color"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Color", saved["Color"]) end
        end

        if self:state("wc_atmosphere", "use_decay") ~= false then
            local decay = self:read_color("wc_atmosphere", "decay")
            if decay then self:safe_set(obj, "Decay", decay) end
        else
            if saved["Decay"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(obj, "Decay", saved["Decay"]) end
        end
    end

    self.apply_atmosphere = apply

    atmo:add_switch("use_density", "enable density", true)
    atmo:add_slider("density", "density", 0, 100, "%", 30)
    atmo:add_switch("use_offset", "enable offset", true)
    atmo:add_slider("offset", "offset", -100, 100, "%", 25)
    atmo:add_switch("use_haze", "enable haze", true)
    atmo:add_slider("haze", "haze", 0, 10, "", 0)
    atmo:add_switch("use_glare", "enable glare", true)
    atmo:add_slider("glare", "glare", 0, 10, "", 0)
    atmo:add_switch("use_color", "enable color", true)
    self:add_color_option(atmo, "color", "color", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    atmo:add_switch("use_decay", "enable decay", true)
    self:add_color_option(atmo, "decay", "decay", Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 110))

    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_density", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_offset", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_haze", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_glare", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_color", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.use_decay", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.density", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.offset", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.haze", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.glare", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.color", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_atmosphere.decay", apply)
    self:register_cleanup("wc_atmosphere", apply)
end

--// override world lighting
function Worldoptions:build_lighting()
    local ui = self.ui
    local LIGHT_PROPS = {
        "Brightness", "ExposureCompensation", "ClockTime", "GlobalShadows",
        "FogStart", "FogEnd", "FogColor", "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient",
    }
    local saved = self.lighting.saved

    local light = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_lighting", "Lighting", "control lighting")

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_lighting") then
            if self.lighting.snapped then
                self.helpers.restore(saved, Lighting, LIGHT_PROPS)
                self.lighting.snapped = false
                for k in pairs(saved) do saved[k] = nil end
            end
            return
        end

        if not self.lighting.snapped then
            self.helpers.snapshot(saved, Lighting, LIGHT_PROPS)
            self.lighting.snapped = true
        end

        local brightness_val = self:state("wc_lighting", "use_brightness") ~= false and (self:state("wc_lighting", "brightness") or 2) or saved["Brightness"]
        if brightness_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "Brightness", brightness_val) end

        local exposure_val = self:state("wc_lighting", "use_exposure") ~= false and (self:state("wc_lighting", "exposure") or 0) or saved["ExposureCompensation"]
        if exposure_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "ExposureCompensation", exposure_val) end

        local clock_val = self:state("wc_lighting", "use_clock_time") ~= false and (self:state("wc_lighting", "clock_time") or 14) or saved["ClockTime"]
        if clock_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "ClockTime", clock_val) end

        local shadows_val = self:state("wc_lighting", "use_shadows") ~= false and (self:state("wc_lighting", "global_shadows") == true) or saved["GlobalShadows"]
        if shadows_val ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "GlobalShadows", shadows_val) end

        if self:state("wc_lighting", "use_fog") ~= false then
            self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogStart", self:state("wc_lighting", "fog_start") or 0)
            self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogEnd", self:state("wc_lighting", "fog_end") or 100000)
            local fog = self:read_color("wc_lighting", "fog_color")
            if fog then self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogColor", fog) end
        else
            if saved["FogStart"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogStart", saved["FogStart"]) end
            if saved["FogEnd"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogEnd", saved["FogEnd"]) end
            if saved["FogColor"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "FogColor", saved["FogColor"]) end
        end

        if self:state("wc_lighting", "use_ambient") ~= false then
            local amb = self:read_color("wc_lighting", "ambient")
            if amb then self:safe_set(Lighting, "Ambient", amb) end
            local outdoor = self:read_color("wc_lighting", "outdoor_ambient")
            if outdoor then self:safe_set(Lighting, "OutdoorAmbient", outdoor) end
        else
            if saved["Ambient"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "Ambient", saved["Ambient"]) end
            if saved["OutdoorAmbient"] ~= nil then self:safe_set(Lighting, "OutdoorAmbient", saved["OutdoorAmbient"]) end
        end
    end

    light:add_switch("use_brightness", "enable brightness", true)
    light:add_slider("brightness", "brightness", 0, 10, "", 2)
    light:add_switch("use_exposure", "enable exposure", true)
    light:add_slider("exposure", "exposure", -5, 5, "", 0)
    light:add_switch("use_clock_time", "enable clock time", true)
    light:add_slider("clock_time", "clock time", 0, 24, "h", 14)
    light:add_switch("use_shadows", "enable shadows", true)
    light:add_switch("global_shadows", "global shadows", true)
    light:add_switch("use_fog", "enable fog", true)
    light:add_slider("fog_start", "fog start", 0, 2000, "", 0)
    light:add_slider("fog_end", "fog end", 0, 10000, "", 10000)
    self:add_color_option(light, "fog_color", "fog color", Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192))
    light:add_switch("use_ambient", "enable ambient", true)
    self:add_color_option(light, "ambient", "ambient", Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
    self:add_color_option(light, "outdoor_ambient", "outdoor ambient", Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128))

    for _, key in ipairs({
        "enabled", "use_brightness", "use_exposure", "use_clock_time", "use_shadows", "use_fog", "use_ambient",
        "brightness", "exposure", "clock_time", "global_shadows",
        "fog_start", "fog_end", "fog_color", "ambient", "outdoor_ambient",
    }) do
        ui:on_change("wc_lighting." .. key, apply)
    end
    self:register_cleanup("wc_lighting", apply)
end

--// custom skybox --------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_skybox()
    local ui = self.ui
    local self_ref = self
    local SKY_PROPS = {
        "SkyboxBk", "SkyboxDn", "SkyboxFt", "SkyboxLf", "SkyboxRt", "SkyboxUp",
        "StarCount", "SunTextureId", "SunAngularSize", "MoonTextureId", "MoonAngularSize",
    }
    local FACE_PROPS = {
        SkyboxBk = true, SkyboxDn = true, SkyboxFt = true,
        SkyboxLf = true, SkyboxRt = true, SkyboxUp = true
    }
    local saved_sky = self.sky.saved
    local apply

    local sky = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_skybox", "Custom Skies", "apply a custom skybox")

    local function get_sky(create)
        local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
        if existing then return existing end
        if create then
            local ok, made = pcall(Instance.new, "Sky")
            if ok and made then
                made.Name = "daydreamer_sky"
                made.Parent = Lighting
                self.sky.created_instance = made
                return made
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function snap_sky(obj)
        if not obj then return end
        if self.sky.snapped_instance == obj then return end
        for k in pairs(saved_sky) do saved_sky[k] = nil end
        self.helpers.snapshot(saved_sky, obj, SKY_PROPS)
        self.sky.snapped_instance = obj
    end

    local function restore_sky(obj, only_missing)
        if not obj then return end
        for _, prop in ipairs(SKY_PROPS) do
            if not only_missing or only_missing[prop] == nil then
                local value = saved_sky[prop]
                if value ~= nil and obj[prop] ~= value then
                    self_ref:safe_set(obj, prop, value)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function selected_props()
        local choice = self:state("wc_skybox", "preset")
        if choice == "custom" then
            local raw = self:state("wc_skybox", "custom_id")
            if type(raw) == "string" and raw ~= "" then
                local ids = {}
                for id in raw:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                    local clean = self.helpers.asset_id(id)
                    if clean then
                        table.insert(ids, clean)
                    end
                end
                if #ids > 0 then
                    local bk = ids[1]
                    local dn = ids[2] or bk
                    local ft = ids[3] or bk
                    local lf = ids[4] or bk
                    local rt = ids[5] or bk
                    local up = ids[6] or bk
                    return {
                        SkyboxBk = bk, SkyboxDn = dn, SkyboxFt = ft,
                        SkyboxLf = lf, SkyboxRt = rt, SkyboxUp = up,
                    }
                end
            end
            return nil, "enter at least one valid skybox asset ID"
        end
        return self.skyboxes[choice], "no faces set for '" .. tostring(choice) .. "'"
    end

    local function start_sky_loop()
        if self.sky.loop_active then return end
        self.sky.loop_active = true
        task.spawn(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            while self.sky.loop_active and self_ref:is_on("wc_skybox") do
                apply()
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end))
    end

    local function stop_sky_loop()
        self.sky.loop_active = false
    end

    apply = function()
        if not self_ref:is_on("wc_skybox") then
            stop_sky_loop()
            if self.sky.snapped_instance then
                if self.sky.created_instance == self.sky.snapped_instance then
                    pcall(function() self.sky.snapped_instance:Destroy() end)
                else
                    restore_sky(self.sky.snapped_instance)
                end
                self.sky.snapped_instance = nil
                for k in pairs(saved_sky) do saved_sky[k] = nil end
            end
            self.sky.created_instance = nil
            -- Since skybox was disabled, re-apply atmosphere to restore default density if atmosphere override is off
            if self_ref.apply_atmosphere then
                self_ref:apply_atmosphere()
            end
            return
        end

        start_sky_loop()

        -- Make sure atmosphere is updated (to set density/haze to 0 if atmosphere override is off)
        if self_ref.apply_atmosphere then
            self_ref:apply_atmosphere()
        end

        local choice = self_ref:state("wc_skybox", "preset")
        local obj = get_sky(true)
        if not obj then return end
        snap_sky(obj)

        if choice == "default" or choice == nil then
            restore_sky(obj)
            return
        end
        local props, err = selected_props()
        if type(props) ~= "table" then
            restore_sky(obj)
            if self.sky.last_error ~= err and type(ui.notify) == "function" then
                ui:notify({ title = "skybox", text = err, duration = 3 })
            end
            self.sky.last_error = err
            return
        end
        self.sky.last_error = nil
        restore_sky(obj, props)
        for _, prop in ipairs(SKY_PROPS) do
            local val = props[prop]
            if val ~= nil then
                local is_face = FACE_PROPS[prop]
                if is_face then
                    local id = self_ref.helpers.asset_id(val)
                    if id and obj[prop] ~= id then self_ref:safe_set(obj, prop, id) end
                else
                    if obj[prop] ~= val then self_ref:safe_set(obj, prop, val) end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    self.apply_skybox = apply

    sky:add_dropdown("preset", "preset", self.skybox_names)
    sky:add_textbox("custom_id", "custom skybox ID(s)", "", "single ID or comma list")
    sky:add_button("apply", "apply selected skybox", "apply")

    ui:on_change("wc_skybox.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_skybox.preset", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_skybox.custom_id", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_skybox.apply", apply)
    self:register_cleanup("wc_skybox", apply)
end

--// bloom ----------------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_bloom()
    local ui = self.ui
    local BLOOM_NAME = "daydreamer_bloom"
    local bloom = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_bloom", "Bloom", "glow around bright areas")

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_bloom") then
            self:destroy_named(BLOOM_NAME)
            return
        end
        local fx = self:own_effect("BloomEffect", BLOOM_NAME)
        if not fx then return end
        self:safe_set(fx, "Intensity", (self:state("wc_bloom", "intensity") or 0) / 10)
        self:safe_set(fx, "Size", self:state("wc_bloom", "size") or 24)
        self:safe_set(fx, "Threshold", (self:state("wc_bloom", "threshold") or 0) / 10)
    end

    bloom:add_slider("intensity", "intensity", 0, 100, "", 10)
    bloom:add_slider("size", "size", 0, 56, "", 24)
    bloom:add_slider("threshold", "threshold", 0, 100, "", 10)

    ui:on_change("wc_bloom.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_bloom.intensity", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_bloom.size", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_bloom.threshold", apply)
end

--// sun rays -------------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_sunrays()
    local ui = self.ui
    local SUN_NAME = "daydreamer_sunrays"
    local sun = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_sunrays", "Sun Rays", "control how sun rays behave")

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_sunrays") then
            self:destroy_named(SUN_NAME)
            return
        end
        local fx = self:own_effect("SunRaysEffect", SUN_NAME)
        if not fx then return end
        self:safe_set(fx, "Intensity", (self:state("wc_sunrays", "intensity") or 0) / 100)
        self:safe_set(fx, "Spread", (self:state("wc_sunrays", "spread") or 0) / 100)
    end

    sun:add_slider("intensity", "intensity", 0, 100, "%", 25)
    sun:add_slider("spread", "spread", 0, 100, "%", 100)

    ui:on_change("wc_sunrays.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_sunrays.intensity", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_sunrays.spread", apply)
end

--// depth of field -------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_dof()
    local ui = self.ui
    local DOF_NAME = "daydreamer_dof"
    local dof = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_dof", "Depth of Field", "blur things outside the focus range")

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_dof") then
            self:destroy_named(DOF_NAME)
            return
        end
        local fx = self:own_effect("DepthOfFieldEffect", DOF_NAME)
        if not fx then return end
        self:safe_set(fx, "FocusDistance", self:state("wc_dof", "focus_distance") or 50)
        self:safe_set(fx, "InFocusRadius", self:state("wc_dof", "in_focus_radius") or 10)
        self:safe_set(fx, "NearIntensity", (self:state("wc_dof", "near_intensity") or 0) / 100)
        self:safe_set(fx, "FarIntensity", (self:state("wc_dof", "far_intensity") or 0) / 100)
    end

    dof:add_slider("focus_distance", "focus distance", 0, 500, " studs", 50)
    dof:add_slider("in_focus_radius", "in-focus radius", 0, 200, " studs", 10)
    dof:add_slider("near_intensity", "near intensity", 0, 100, "%", 0)
    dof:add_slider("far_intensity", "far intensity", 0, 100, "%", 75)

    ui:on_change("wc_dof.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_dof.focus_distance", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_dof.in_focus_radius", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_dof.near_intensity", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_dof.far_intensity", apply)
end

--// motion blur ----------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_motion_blur()
    local ui = self.ui
    local self_ref = self
    local MBNAME = "dreamermb"
    local mb = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_motionblur", "Motion Blur", "adds motion blur to your game")
    local Blur_Intensity_Multiplier = 5

    local function clear()
        if self.motion_blur.conn then
            pcall(function() self.motion_blur.conn:Disconnect() end)
            self.motion_blur.conn = nil
        end
        if self.motion_blur.fx then
            pcall(function() self.motion_blur.fx:Destroy() end)
            self.motion_blur.fx = nil
        end
        self.motion_blur.previous_look_vector = nil
        self:destroy_named(MBNAME)
    end

    local function apply()
        if not self:is_on("wc_motionblur") then
            clear()
            return
        end

        if self.motion_blur.conn then return end

        local camera = workspace_svc.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        self.motion_blur.previous_look_vector = camera.CFrame.LookVector
        self.motion_blur.fx = self:own_effect("DepthOfFieldEffect", MBNAME)
        if not self.motion_blur.fx then return end
        self:safe_set(self.motion_blur.fx, "FarIntensity", 0)
        self:safe_set(self.motion_blur.fx, "FocusDistance", 10)
        self:safe_set(self.motion_blur.fx, "InFocusRadius", 10)
        self:safe_set(self.motion_blur.fx, "NearIntensity", 0)

        self.motion_blur.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local cam = workspace_svc.CurrentCamera
            if not cam or not self_ref.motion_blur.fx then return end

            local current_look_vector = cam.CFrame.LookVector
            local camera_blur_magnitude = (current_look_vector - self_ref.motion_blur.previous_look_vector).Magnitude
            local slider_val = self_ref:state("wc_motionblur", "intensity") or 0

            local multiplier = Blur_Intensity_Multiplier * (slider_val / 30)

            self_ref:safe_set(self_ref.motion_blur.fx, "FarIntensity", math.clamp(math.abs(camera_blur_magnitude) * multiplier, 0, 1))
            self_ref:safe_set(self_ref.motion_blur.fx, "NearIntensity", 0)

            self_ref.motion_blur.previous_look_vector = current_look_vector
        end))

        ui:track_connection(self.motion_blur.conn)
    end

    mb:add_slider("intensity", "intensity", 0, 100, "%", 25)

    ui:on_change("wc_motionblur.enabled", apply)
    ui:on_change("wc_motionblur.intensity", apply)
end

--// fov changer ----------------------------------------------------------------
function Worldoptions:build_fov()
    local ui = self.ui
    local self_ref = self
    local fov = self.tab:add_toggle("wc_fov", "FOV Changer", "override your camera field of view")

    local function stop()
        if self.fov.conn then
            pcall(function() self.fov.conn:Disconnect() end)
            self.fov.conn = nil
        end
        local cam = workspace_svc.CurrentCamera
        if cam and self.fov.saved_fov then self:safe_set(cam, "FieldOfView", self.fov.saved_fov) end
        self.fov.saved_fov = nil
    end

    local function start()
        if self.fov.conn then return end
        local cam = workspace_svc.CurrentCamera
        if cam and self.fov.saved_fov == nil then
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return cam.FieldOfView end)
            if ok then self.fov.saved_fov = v end
        end
        self.fov.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local camera = workspace_svc.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then return end
            self_ref:safe_set(camera, "FieldOfView", self_ref:state("wc_fov", "fov") or 70)
        end))
        ui:track_connection(self.fov.conn)
    end

    local function apply()
        if self:is_on("wc_fov") then start() else stop() end
    end

    fov:add_slider("fov", "fov", 1, 120, "", 70)

    ui:on_change("wc_fov.enabled", apply)
    self:register_cleanup("wc_fov", apply)
end

function Worldoptions:install_cleanup()
    local self_ref = self
    if shared then
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_worldoptions_cleanup = function()
            for _, fn in ipairs(self_ref.cleanups) do pcall(fn) end
            for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if type(child.Name) == "string" and child.Name:sub(1, 11) == "daydreamer_" then
                    pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function Worldoptions:build()
    self:build_color_correction()
    self:build_atmosphere()
    self:build_lighting()
    self:build_skybox()
    self:build_bloom()
    self:build_sunrays()
    self:build_dof()
    self:build_motion_blur()
    self:build_fov()
    self:install_cleanup()
    return self.tab
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("world")

    -- re-injection guard: tear down the previous load's effects + cleanup registrations
    -- so reloading the hub doesn't stack duplicate cleanups or strand owned Lighting
    -- children. matches the pattern in misc.lua / nametags.lua / wallhack.lua.
    if shared and type(shared.daydreamer_rivals_worldoptions_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_worldoptions_cleanup)
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_worldoptions_cleanup = nil
    end

    local worldoptions = Worldoptions.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.worldoptions = worldoptions
    return worldoptions:build()
end

end)

-- ─── tab: misc ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__def('tab_misc', function()
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    local env = getfenv()
    local jit = "LPH_" .. "JIT"
    local nov = "LPH_" .. "NO_VIRTUALIZE"
    env[jit] = env[jit] or function(f) return f end
    env[nov] = env[nov] or function(f) return f end
end

local players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local run = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local http = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))
local lp = players.LocalPlayer

--// ffa drops are flat BaseParts parented straight to workspace, named "_drop", with a
--// "Health" or "Ammo" child marking what they give
local DROP_NAME = "_drop"

--//resolve and cache the camera controller only once
local camctrl = nil
local function get_camctrl()
    if camctrl then return camctrl end
    local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
    local ctrls = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
    local inst = ctrls and ctrls:FindFirstChild("CameraController")
    if inst and inst:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, inst)
        if ok and type(mod) == "table" then camctrl = mod end
    end
    return camctrl
end

local function my_char()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return nil end
    return char, hrp, hum
end

local function cam_yaw()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return 0 end
    local _, y = cam.CFrame:ToOrientation()
    return y
end

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local dreamer = env.dreamer
if type(dreamer) ~= "table" then
    dreamer = {}
    env.dreamer = dreamer
end

local fallback_helpers = {}
function fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn)
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
end
function fallback_helpers.disconnect_all(conns)
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns or {}) do fallback_helpers.disconnect(conn) end
    for i = #conns, 1, -1 do conns[i] = nil end
end
function fallback_helpers.enabled(ui, id)
    local st = ui and ui.state and ui.state[id]
    return st and (st.enabled == true or st.menu_enabled == true) or false
end
function fallback_helpers.set_visible(opt, v)
    if opt and opt.set_visible then opt:set_visible(v == true) end
end
function fallback_helpers.status(row, text, color)
    if row then row:set_value(text, color) end
end
function fallback_helpers.short_name(name, max_len)
    name = tostring(name or "?")
    max_len = max_len or 12
    if #name > max_len then return string.sub(name, 1, max_len) .. "..." end
    return name
end

local Misc = {}
Misc.__index = Misc

function Misc.new(ui, tab, lib)
    local helpers = type(lib) == "table" and type(lib.helpers) == "table" and lib.helpers or fallback_helpers
    return setmetatable({
        ui = ui,
        tab = tab,
        lib = lib or {},
        helpers = helpers,
        --//mod
        mod_conns = {},
        mod_cancel = false,
        mod_running = false,
        mod_action_taken = false,
        mod_last_sig = nil,
        mod_staff_ids = {},
        mod_fetched_staff_ids = false,
        mod_loop_token = 0,
        mod_status = nil,
        mod_detail = nil,
        --//animation player
        anim_track = nil,
        anim_obj = nil,
        anim_stopped_conn = nil,
        anim_cleaning = false,
        anim_retry_token = 0,
        anim_scan_elapsed = 0,
        anim_status = nil,
        --//stretched res
        res_conn = nil,
        was_forcing = false,
        tp_conn = nil,
        --anti aim
        spin_angle = 0,
        jitter_flip = false,
        aa_touched_autorotate = false,
        char_conn = nil,
        aa_conn = nil,
        anim_conn = nil,
        --anti tripmine
        tripmine_hrp = nil,
        tripmine_refresh_conn = nil,
        tripmine_detonate_conn = nil,
        --auto collect buffs
        collect_conn = nil,
        collect_status = nil,
        collect_elapsed = 0,
        collect_count = 0,
    }, Misc)
end

function Misc:is_mod_detector_enabled()
    return self.helpers.enabled(self.ui, "moderator_detector")
end

function Misc:is_staff_role(role)
    if type(role) ~= "string" then return false end
    local r = string.lower(role)
    return string.find(r, "mod", 1, true)
        or string.find(r, "staff", 1, true)
        or string.find(r, "admin", 1, true)
        or string.find(r, "contributor", 1, true)
        or string.find(r, "script", 1, true)
        or string.find(r, "build", 1, true)
end

function Misc:get_role(plr)
    if game.CreatorType ~= Enum.CreatorType.Group then return nil end
    local ok, role = pcall(function()
        return plr:GetRoleInGroup(game.CreatorId)
    end)
    return ok and role or nil
end

function Misc:http_json(url)
    local ok, body = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(url)
    end)
    if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" then return nil end
    local ok_json, data = pcall(function()
        return http:JSONDecode(body)
    end)
    return ok_json and data or nil
end

function Misc:fetch_staff_ids_once()
    if self.mod_fetched_staff_ids then return end
    self.mod_fetched_staff_ids = true
    if game.CreatorType ~= Enum.CreatorType.Group then return end
    local gid = tonumber(game.CreatorId)
    if not gid then return end

    local self_ref = self
    task.spawn(function()
        local roles = self_ref:http_json(("https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles"):format(gid))
        if not (roles and type(roles.roles) == "table") then return end
        for _, role in ipairs(roles.roles) do
            if self_ref.mod_cancel then return end
            if role.id and self_ref:is_staff_role(role.name) then
                local cursor = ""
                repeat
                    local url = ("https://groups.roproxy.com/v1/groups/%d/roles/%d/users?limit=100&cursor=%s")
                        :format(gid, role.id, cursor)
                    local page = self_ref:http_json(url)
                    if not (page and type(page.data) == "table") then break end
                    for _, user in ipairs(page.data) do
                        local uid = tonumber(user.userId or user.id)
                        if uid then self_ref.mod_staff_ids[uid] = true end
                    end
                    cursor = page.nextPageCursor or ""
                    task.wait(0.1)
                until cursor == "" or self_ref.mod_cancel
            end
        end
        if self_ref:is_mod_detector_enabled() then self_ref:run_mod_detection() end
    end)
end

function Misc:collect_friend_staff()
    local found = {}
    local seen = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if self.mod_cancel then break end
        local ok, pages = pcall(function()
            return players:GetFriendsAsync(plr.UserId)
        end)
        if ok and pages then
            while not self.mod_cancel do
                local page_ok, page = pcall(function()
                    return pages:GetCurrentPage()
                end)
                if not page_ok or type(page) ~= "table" then break end
                for _, friend in ipairs(page) do
                    local uid = tonumber(friend.Id or friend.UserId)
                    if uid and self.mod_staff_ids[uid] and not seen[uid] then
                        seen[uid] = true
                        found[#found + 1] = self.helpers.short_name(friend.Username or friend.Name or uid, 12)
                    end
                end
                if pages.IsFinished then break end
                local adv_ok = pcall(function()
                    pages:AdvanceToNextPageAsync()
                end)
                if not adv_ok then break end
                task.wait()
            end
        end
    end
    return found
end

function Misc:run_mod_detection()
    if self.mod_running or not self:is_mod_detector_enabled() then return end
    self.mod_running = true

    local staff = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        local role = self:get_role(plr)
        if self:is_staff_role(role) then
            self.mod_staff_ids[plr.UserId] = true
            staff[#staff + 1] = self.helpers.short_name(plr.Name, 12)
        end
    end

    local st = self.ui.state.moderator_detector or {}
    local friend_staff = {}
    if st.check_friends == true then
        friend_staff = self:collect_friend_staff()
    end

    local detected = (#staff > 0) or (#friend_staff > 0)
    local sig = table.concat(staff, ",") .. "|" .. table.concat(friend_staff, ",")
    if detected then
        local detail = (#staff > 0 and ("mods: " .. table.concat(staff, ", ")) or "")
        if #friend_staff > 0 then
            detail = detail .. (#detail > 0 and " | " or "") .. "friends: " .. table.concat(friend_staff, ", ")
        end
        self.helpers.status(self.mod_status, "detected", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120))
        self.helpers.status(self.mod_detail, detail, Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 150))

        if sig ~= self.mod_last_sig then
            self.mod_last_sig = sig
            local behaviour = tostring(st.behaviour or "warn"):lower()
            if behaviour == "kick" and not self.mod_action_taken then
                self.mod_action_taken = true
                lp:Kick("daydreamer: moderator detected")
            elseif behaviour == "unload" and not self.mod_action_taken then
                self.mod_action_taken = true
                if type(self.ui.unload) == "function" then self.ui:unload() end
            elseif type(self.ui.notify) == "function" then
                self.ui:notify({
                    title = "Moderator Detector",
                    text = detail,
                    duration = 8,
                })
            end
        end
    else
        self.mod_last_sig = nil
        self.mod_action_taken = false
        self.helpers.status(self.mod_status, "clear", Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 170))
        self.helpers.status(self.mod_detail, ("players: %d"):format(#players:GetPlayers()), Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    end

    self.mod_running = false
end

function Misc:start_mod_detector()
    self.mod_cancel = false
    self.mod_loop_token = self.mod_loop_token + 1
    self.mod_action_taken = false
    self.helpers.status(self.mod_status, "scanning", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 140))
    self.helpers.status(self.mod_detail, "checking roles", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    self:fetch_staff_ids_once()
    task.spawn(function() self:run_mod_detection() end)
end

function Misc:stop_mod_detector()
    self.mod_cancel = true
    self.mod_loop_token = self.mod_loop_token + 1
    self.mod_running = false
    self.mod_action_taken = false
    self.mod_last_sig = nil
    self.helpers.status(self.mod_status, "off", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    self.helpers.status(self.mod_detail, "--", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
end

--// animation player methods
function Misc:is_anim_enabled()
    return self.helpers.enabled(self.ui, "animation_player")
end

function Misc:clean_anim_track(fade)
    self.anim_cleaning = true
    self.helpers.disconnect(self.anim_stopped_conn)
    self.anim_stopped_conn = nil
    if self.anim_track then
        local track = self.anim_track
        self.anim_track = nil
        pcall(function() track:Stop(fade or 0.12) end)
        pcall(function() track:Destroy() end)
    end
    if self.anim_obj then
        local obj = self.anim_obj
        self.anim_obj = nil
        pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
    end
    self.anim_cleaning = false
end

function Misc:normalize_anim_id(value)
    local raw = tostring(value or "")
    local id = raw:match("(%d+)")
    if not id or id == "" then return nil end
    return "rbxassetid://" .. id, id
end

function Misc:get_animator()
    local _, _, hum = my_char()
    if not hum then return nil end
    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then
        animator = Instance.new("Animator")
        animator.Parent = hum
    end
    return animator
end

function Misc:queue_anim_retry(delay_sec)
    self.anim_retry_token = self.anim_retry_token + 1
    local token = self.anim_retry_token
    local self_ref = self
    task.delay(delay_sec or 0.35, function()
        if token ~= self_ref.anim_retry_token then return end
        if self_ref:is_anim_enabled() and not self_ref.anim_track then
            self_ref:play_animation()
        end
    end)
end

function Misc:stop_competing_tracks(animator)
    local st = self.ui.state.animation_player
    if not (st and st.override_other == true and animator and animator.GetPlayingAnimationTracks) then return end
    local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
        return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
    end)
    if not ok or type(tracks) ~= "table" then return end
    for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
        if track ~= self.anim_track then
            pcall(function() track:Stop(0.08) end)
        end
    end
end

function Misc:play_animation()
    local st = self.ui.state.animation_player
    local asset_id, id = self:normalize_anim_id(st and st.animation_id)
    if not asset_id then
        self:clean_anim_track()
        self.helpers.status(self.anim_status, "missing id", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 120))
        return
    end

    local animator = self:get_animator()
    if not animator then
        self:clean_anim_track()
        self.helpers.status(self.anim_status, "no character", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120))
        return
    end

    self:clean_anim_track()
    self.anim_retry_token = self.anim_retry_token + 1

    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.Name = "daydreamer_animation_player"
    anim.AnimationId = asset_id

    local ok, track = pcall(function()
        return animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
    end)
    if not ok or not track then
        pcall(function() anim:Destroy() end)
        self.helpers.status(self.anim_status, "load failed", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120))
        return
    end

    self.anim_obj = anim
    self.anim_track = track
    pcall(function() track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action end)
    self:stop_competing_tracks(animator)
    pcall(function() track:Play(0.12, 1, 1) end)
    self.helpers.status(self.anim_status, "playing " .. id, Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 170))

    local self_ref = self
    self.anim_stopped_conn = track.Stopped:Connect(LPH_JIT(function()
        if self_ref.anim_track == track then
            self_ref.anim_track = nil
            if self_ref:is_anim_enabled() and not self_ref.anim_cleaning then
                self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.anim_status, "retrying", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 140))
                self_ref:queue_anim_retry(0.35)
            else
                self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.anim_status, "stopped", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
            end
        end
    end))
end

function Misc:get_stretch_scale(st)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize
    local screen_aspect = (vp and vp.Y > 0) and (vp.X / vp.Y) or (16 / 9)
    local w = math.max(tonumber(st and st.width) or 16, 0.1)
    local h = math.max(tonumber(st and st.height) or 9, 0.1)
    return math.clamp((w / h) / screen_aspect, 0.5, 1.5)
end

function Misc:exit_third_person()
    if not self.was_forcing then return end
    self.was_forcing = false
    local cc = get_camctrl()
    if not cc then return end
    local cs = cc.CameraState
    if cs and cs.States then
        pcall(function() cs:_SetPOVState(cs.States.FirstPerson) end)
    end
    cc._third_person_override = nil
    if cc._pov_spring then
        pcall(function() cc._pov_spring.Target = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end)
    end
end

function Misc:restore_autorotate()
    if self.aa_touched_autorotate then
        local _, _, hum = my_char()
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = true end) end
        self.aa_touched_autorotate = false
    end
end

function Misc:build_gunmods()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local lib = self.lib
    --// gunmods (rapid fire / no spread / etc) lives in lib.gunmods and renders
    --// into this misc tab via a dedicated section. the Gunmods class owns the
    --// hooks; we just hand it the tab and let it add_toggle.
    local gunmods_module = type(lib) == "table" and lib.gunmods
    if gunmods_module and type(gunmods_module.install) == "function" then
        dreamer.gunmods_instance = gunmods_module.install(ui, tab, lib)
    end
end

function Misc:build_mod_detector()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local mod_sec = tab:add_toggle("moderator_detector", "Moderator Detector", desc["moderator_detector"])
    mod_sec:add_switch("check_friends", "check if friends with mod", false)
    mod_sec:add_dropdown("behaviour", "behaviour", { "warn", "kick", "unload" })
    self.mod_status = mod_sec:add_status_row("status", "status")
    self.mod_detail = mod_sec:add_status_row("detail", "detail")

    self.helpers.status(self.mod_status, "off", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
    self.helpers.status(self.mod_detail, "--", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))

    local self_ref = self
    ui:on_change("moderator_detector.enabled", function(v)
        if v == true or self_ref:is_mod_detector_enabled() then
            self_ref:start_mod_detector()
        else
            self_ref:stop_mod_detector()
        end
    end)
    ui:on_change("moderator_detector.check_friends", function()
        if self_ref:is_mod_detector_enabled() then
            task.spawn(function() self_ref:run_mod_detection() end)
        end
    end)
    ui:on_change("moderator_detector.behaviour", function()
        self_ref.mod_action_taken = false
    end)

    self.mod_conns[#self.mod_conns + 1] = players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
        if not self_ref:is_mod_detector_enabled() then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function() plr.CharacterAdded:Wait() end)
            task.wait(0.25)
            self_ref:run_mod_detection()
        end)
    end)

    task.defer(function()
        if self_ref:is_mod_detector_enabled() then self_ref:start_mod_detector() end
    end)
end

function Misc:build_animation_player()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local anim_sec = tab:add_toggle("animation_player", "Animation Player", desc["animation_player"])
    anim_sec:add_textbox("animation_id", "animation id", "", "rbxassetid://...")
    anim_sec:add_switch("override_other", "override other animations", false)
    self.anim_status = anim_sec:add_status_row("status", "status")

    self.helpers.status(self.anim_status, "idle", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))

    local self_ref = self
    ui:on_change("animation_player.enabled", function(v)
        if v == true or self_ref:is_anim_enabled() then
            self_ref:play_animation()
        else
            self_ref.anim_retry_token = self_ref.anim_retry_token + 1
            self_ref:clean_anim_track()
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.anim_status, "stopped", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        end
    end)
    ui:on_change("animation_player.animation_id", function()
        local st = ui.state.animation_player
        if st and st.enabled then
            self_ref:play_animation()
        else
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.anim_status, self_ref:normalize_anim_id(st and st.animation_id) and "ready" or "idle", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        end
    end)
    ui:on_change("animation_player.override_other", function()
        if self_ref:is_anim_enabled() then
            self_ref:play_animation()
        end
    end)

    task.defer(function()
        if self_ref:is_anim_enabled() then
            self_ref:play_animation()
        else
            local st = ui.state.animation_player
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.anim_status, self_ref:normalize_anim_id(st and st.animation_id) and "ready" or "idle", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        end
    end)
end

function Misc:build_stretched_res()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local res_sec = tab:add_toggle("stretched_resolution", "Stretched Resolution", desc["stretched_resolution"])
    res_sec:add_slider("width", "aspect width", 4, 32, "", 16)
    res_sec:add_slider("height", "aspect height", 3, 24, "", 9)

    local self_ref = self
    self.res_conn = run.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local st = ui.state.stretched_resolution
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end

        local scale = self_ref:get_stretch_scale(st)
        local genv = getgenv and getgenv()
        if genv then
            genv.Resolution = genv.Resolution or {}
            genv.Resolution[".gg/scripters"] = scale
        end

        if math.abs(scale - 1) > 0.001 then
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0, 0, 1)
        end
    end))
end

function Misc:build_third_person()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local tp_sec = tab:add_toggle("third_person", "Third Person", desc["third_person"])
    tp_sec:add_slider("distance", "distance", 2, 30, " studs", 8)
    tp_sec:add_slider("height", "height", -10, 30, " studs", 2)
    tp_sec:add_slider("side", "side offset", -20, 20, " studs", 0)

    local self_ref = self
    ui:on_change("third_person.enabled", function(v)
        if v ~= true then self_ref:exit_third_person() end
    end)

    self.tp_conn = run.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local st = ui.state.third_person
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        local cc = get_camctrl()
        if not cc then return end
        local cs = cc.CameraState
        if not (cs and cs.States) then return end
        local states = cs.States

        --// always return true as long as toggle is on
        if cc._third_person_override ~= true then cc._third_person_override = true end

        -- (re)enter third person. respawns/round resets run CameraState._Setup which forces
        -- FirstPerson, so re-grab whenever we've fallen back; _SetPOVState no-ops when the
        -- state is unchanged, and we leave any third-person variant (Mirrored / UnlockedMouse)
        -- the player cycled to with the in-game Camera keybind alone.
        if cs._pov_state == states.FirstPerson or cs._pov_state == nil then
            pcall(function() cs:_SetPOVState(states.ThirdPerson) end)
        end
        self_ref.was_forcing = true

        -- tune the dolly: camera-local +X right, +Y up, +Z BACK. the spring's Value lazily
        -- integrates toward Target on read, so re-asserting Target every frame is smooth +
        -- cheap. the game sets this once on the StateChanged above; we override it with the
        -- slider values. (the POV STATE -- not this offset -- is what shows the body, so the
        -- sliders only move the camera, they never re-hide the character.)
        if cc._pov_spring then
            local back = tonumber(st.distance) or 8
            local up   = tonumber(st.height) or 2
            local side = tonumber(st.side) or 0
            pcall(function() cc._pov_spring.Target = Vector3.new(side, up, back) end)
        end
    end))
end

function Misc:build_anti_aim()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local aa_sec = tab:add_toggle("anti_aim", "Anti-Aim", desc["anti_aim"])
    aa_sec:add_dropdown("mode", "mode", { "Backwards", "Spin", "Jitter", "Left", "Right", "Static" })
    local opt_spin   = aa_sec:add_slider("spin_speed", "spin speed", 90, 2000, "/s", 720)
    local opt_jitter = aa_sec:add_slider("jitter_angle", "jitter angle", 5, 180, "", 90)
    aa_sec:add_slider("yaw_offset", "yaw offset", -180, 180, "", 0)
    aa_sec:add_slider("pitch", "pitch (fake lean)", -90, 90, "", 0)

    local function set_visible(opt, v)
        self.helpers.set_visible(opt, v)
    end

    local function update_mode_opts(mode)
        set_visible(opt_spin, mode == "Spin")
        set_visible(opt_jitter, mode == "Jitter")
    end
    update_mode_opts(ui.state.anti_aim and ui.state.anti_aim.mode or "Backwards")
    ui:on_change("anti_aim.mode", update_mode_opts)

    local self_ref = self
    ui:on_change("anti_aim.enabled", function(v)
        if v ~= true then
            self_ref:restore_autorotate()
            -- snap back to facing our view so the body doesn't stay stuck backwards until
            -- we next move. HRP.CFrame is our own network-owned character physics, so this
            -- write replicates and succeeds from any thread identity (no elevate_thread
            -- needed) -- same reason ragebot writes HRP.CFrame from Heartbeat.
            local _, hrp = my_char()
            if hrp then
                local y = cam_yaw()
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, y, 0) end)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- respawn drops the old body; clear transient state so the new char re-takes cleanly.
    self.char_conn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(LPH_JIT(function()
        self_ref.aa_touched_autorotate = false
        self_ref.spin_angle = 0
        self_ref:clean_anim_track()
        local st = ui.state.animation_player
        if st and st.enabled and self_ref:normalize_anim_id(st.animation_id) then
            task.delay(0.35, function() self_ref:play_animation() end)
        end
    end))

    -- drive the HRP rotation on PreRender -- the LAST step before the frame renders. in THIRD
    -- person the game force-faces the local body to the aim/camera yaw every frame (so your
    -- character looks where you aim); that pass runs mid-frame and BEATS a Heartbeat write --
    -- which is why anti-aim used to only flutter the arms while the body stayed facing the aim.
    -- PreRender fires after the game's facing pass, so our rotation wins the rendered frame and
    -- the WHOLE body turns/jitters in sync. (in FIRST person the game leaves the hidden body
    -- alone, so this holds + replicates exactly like the old Heartbeat write did.) HRP.CFrame
    -- writes replicate + succeed from any thread identity since we own the char (no elevate).
    self.aa_conn = run.PreRender:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
        local st = ui.state.anti_aim
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        local _, hrp, hum = my_char()
        if not hrp then return end

        -- AutoRotate would fight us back toward the move direction; kill it while we drive.
        if hum.AutoRotate then
            pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = false end)
            self_ref.aa_touched_autorotate = true
        end

        local base = cam_yaw()
        local off = math.rad(tonumber(st.yaw_offset) or 0)
        local mode = st.mode or "Backwards"
        local yaw
        if mode == "Spin" then
            -- absolute, view-independent spin (classic spinbot)
            self_ref.spin_angle = (self_ref.spin_angle + math.rad(tonumber(st.spin_speed) or 720) * dt) % (math.pi * 2)
            yaw = self_ref.spin_angle + off
        elseif mode == "Jitter" then
            self_ref.jitter_flip = not self_ref.jitter_flip
            local j = math.rad(tonumber(st.jitter_angle) or 90)
            yaw = base + math.pi + (self_ref.jitter_flip and j or -j) + off
        elseif mode == "Left" then
            yaw = base + math.pi / 2 + off
        elseif mode == "Right" then
            yaw = base - math.pi / 2 + off
        elseif mode == "Static" then
            yaw = base + off
        else -- Backwards
            yaw = base + math.pi + off
        end

        local pitch = math.rad(tonumber(st.pitch) or 0)
        -- rotation only -- keep the body exactly where physics put it. the camera reads
        -- hrp.Position (not its rotation), so our view is unaffected.
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(pitch, 0, 0)
        end)
    end))

    self.anim_conn = run.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
        local st = ui.state.animation_player
        if not (st and (st.enabled == true or st.menu_enabled == true)) then return end
        self_ref.anim_scan_elapsed = self_ref.anim_scan_elapsed + dt
        if self_ref.anim_scan_elapsed < 0.2 then return end
        self_ref.anim_scan_elapsed = 0

        local animator = self_ref:get_animator()
        if not animator then return end
        if st.override_other == true then
            self_ref:stop_competing_tracks(animator)
        end
        if not self_ref.anim_track then
            self_ref:queue_anim_retry(0.05)
        end
    end))
end

function Misc:build_anti_tripmine()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    tab:add_toggle("anti_tripmine", "Anti Tripmine", desc["anti_tripmine"] or "detonates subspaces without damaging you")

    local self_ref = self

    -- keep our cached HRP fresh every heartbeat
    self.tripmine_refresh_conn = run.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local st = ui.state.anti_tripmine
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        pcall(function()
            self_ref.tripmine_hrp = players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        end)
    end))

    -- detonate any SubspaceTripmineHitbox objects every heartbeat
    self.tripmine_detonate_conn = run.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local st = ui.state.anti_tripmine
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        local hrp = self_ref.tripmine_hrp
        if not hrp then return end
        pcall(function()
            for _, s in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if s.Name == "SubspaceTripmineHitbox" then
                    local hb = s:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
                    if hb then
                        firetouchinterest(hrp, hb, 1)
                        firetouchinterest(hrp, hb, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end))
end

function Misc:collect_drops(hrp, want_health, want_ammo)
    local n = 0
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if obj.Name == DROP_NAME and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            local take = (want_health and obj:FindFirstChild("Health") ~= nil)
                or (want_ammo and obj:FindFirstChild("Ammo") ~= nil)
            if take then
                pcall(function()
                    firetouchinterest(hrp, obj, 0)
                    firetouchinterest(hrp, obj, 1)
                end)
                n = n + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return n
end

function Misc:build_auto_collect_buffs()
    local ui = self.ui
    local tab = self.tab
    local desc = ui.descriptions or {}

    local col_sec = tab:add_toggle("auto_collect_buffs", "Auto Collect Buffs", desc["auto_collect_buffs"] or "auto collects health + ammo in ffa")
    col_sec:add_switch("health", "collect health", true)
    col_sec:add_switch("ammo", "collect ammo", true)
    self.collect_status = col_sec:add_status_row("status", "status")

    self.helpers.status(self.collect_status, "off", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))

    local self_ref = self
    ui:on_change("auto_collect_buffs.enabled", function(v)
        if v ~= true then
            self_ref.collect_count = 0
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.collect_status, "off", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        end
    end)

    -- heartbeat rather than RenderStepped: firetouchinterest replicates per call, so 60hz is
    -- plenty and we don't want to burn render frames walking workspace's children.
    self.collect_conn = run.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
        local st = ui.state.auto_collect_buffs
        if not (st and st.enabled) then return end
        local _, hrp, hum = my_char()
        if not hrp then return end

        -- only grab health packs when we can actually use them, otherwise we'd vacuum up
        -- every pack on the map at full hp and leave teammates nothing.
        local want_health = st.health == true and hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth
        local want_ammo = st.ammo == true
        if want_health or want_ammo then
            self_ref.collect_count = self_ref.collect_count + self_ref:collect_drops(hrp, want_health, want_ammo)
        end

        self_ref.collect_elapsed = self_ref.collect_elapsed + dt
        if self_ref.collect_elapsed < 0.25 then return end
        self_ref.collect_elapsed = 0
        if self_ref.collect_count > 0 then
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.collect_status, "collecting", Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 170))
            self_ref.collect_count = 0
        elseif not (want_health or want_ammo) then
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.collect_status, "nothing needed", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        else
            self_ref.helpers.status(self_ref.collect_status, "no drops", Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
        end
    end))
end

function Misc:install_cleanup()
    local self_ref = self
    if shared then
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_misc_cleanup = function()
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.res_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.tp_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.char_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.aa_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.anim_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.tripmine_refresh_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.tripmine_detonate_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect(self_ref.collect_conn)
            self_ref.helpers.disconnect_all(self_ref.mod_conns)
            self_ref:stop_mod_detector()
            self_ref.anim_retry_token = self_ref.anim_retry_token + 1
            self_ref:clean_anim_track()
            self_ref:restore_autorotate()
            self_ref:exit_third_person()
        end
    end
end

function Misc:build()
    local mount_sounds = env.daydreamer_rivals_mount_weapon_sounds
    if type(mount_sounds) == "function" then pcall(mount_sounds, self.tab) end
    self:build_gunmods()
    self:build_mod_detector()
    self:build_animation_player()
    self:build_stretched_res()
    self:build_third_person()
    self:build_anti_aim()
    self:build_anti_tripmine()
    self:build_auto_collect_buffs()
    self:install_cleanup()
    return self.tab
end

return function(ui, lib)
    local tab = ui:add_tab("misc")

    -- re-injection guard: tear down the previous load's render/heartbeat/character
    -- connections and undo any state they left on the body/camera, so reloading the hub
    -- doesn't stack duplicate loops or strand AutoRotate/pov. (same pattern as combat's
    -- shared.daydreamer_rivals_fov_cleanup / nametags / wallhack.)
    if shared and type(shared.daydreamer_rivals_misc_cleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(shared.daydreamer_rivals_misc_cleanup)
        shared.daydreamer_rivals_misc_cleanup = nil
    end

    local misc = Misc.new(ui, tab, lib)
    dreamer.misc = misc
    return misc:build()
end
